===== FILE: ./README.md =====
# 独立外包工具包：第二个大 Boss（哥布林王 · `boss_goblin_king`）

> **本目录是一个 100% 自包含的外包开发包**：可直接用 `all_in_one.txt` 发给**无本地上下文的独立网页端 AI**。工坊像素堆叠渲染内核、哥布林标杆源码、独立预览台与自动化格式校验脚本均已内置，**不需要访问本目录以外的任何文件**。

---

## 一、包内目录说明

```text
分包-哥布林王/
├── README.md                    # 本文件：包内结构、发包与验收流程
├── 设计文档-哥布林王.md          # 完整外观、三维坐标预算、二足重步、4 套换色与 4 大技能规格
├── 开工提示词.md                 # 发给无上下文网页端 AI 的完整提示词（已内置硬性 bundle_boss_goblin_king.zip 交付规范）
├── all_in_one.txt               # 已合并好的单文件合集，直接拖进网页端 AI 即可开工
├── 打包发给网页AI.command        # Mac 双击自动重新合并生成 all_in_one.txt
├── 预览验收.command              # Mac 双击自动跑 node verify.mjs + 开本地服务打开 preview.html
├── preview.html                 # 自带独立预览验收台（360° 旋转、4 套皮肤、7 个动作与 4 大技能）
├── verify.mjs                   # 自带 Node 硬性校验脚本（格式 / 防糊 / 零 NaN / 零禁用字）
├── core/                        # 【只读！严禁修改】主工坊完整像素堆叠内核（7 个文件）
├── references/                  # 【只读参考】主工坊哥布林标杆 goblin.js、slime.js 与骨骼防糊经验
├── bundle/                      # 【验收放置区】把交回的 bundle_boss_goblin_king.zip 解压覆盖到这里即可预览验收
└── 交回/                        # 存放外包交回的原始 bundle_boss_goblin_king.zip 备份
```

---

## 二、发包与验收 3 步走

1. **发出去**：把本目录里的 **`all_in_one.txt`** 拖进网页端 AI 对话框，直接发这句口令（或贴 `开工提示词.md`）：
   > 严格按附件末尾的 `开工提示词.md` 开发。务必用单个脚本一次性生成 3 个文件、自检并打包出可下载的 `bundle_boss_goblin_king.zip`；正文严禁贴任何代码、JSON 或文档内容，若未打包完成直接停下汇报进度。
2. **收回来**：把下载的 `bundle_boss_goblin_king.zip` 存到 `交回/` 备底，并把里面的 3 个文件（`boss_goblin_king.js`、`manifest.json`、`交付说明.md`）覆盖放进本目录的 `bundle/`。
3. **一键验收**：直接双击 **`预览验收.command`** —— 自动先跑 `node verify.mjs`，再在浏览器打开 `http://127.0.0.1:8129/preview.html`（与蜘蛛 Boss 的 8128 端口错开，可同时开着对比！）。确认完美后，把 `bundle/boss_goblin_king.js` 拷进主工坊 `creatures/` 即可零改动合入。


===== FILE: ./设计文档-哥布林王.md =====
# 设计文档：第二个大 Boss · 哥布林王（`boss_goblin_king`）

- **资产 ID**：`boss_goblin_king`
- **定位**：部落型蛮横大 Boss（归属 `T09 林地落果空坪` / `T16 苔藓古石遗迹` 营地），与前作普通「苔绿哥布林」血脉同源，但体型放大至 **Boss 级宽厚壮硕体态**，兼具「贪财滑稽」与「蛮力暴君」的反差气场。
- **技术底座**：纯原生 JS + Canvas 2D，100% 调用本包自带 `./core/`（`PW.Math`、`PW.Voxel`、`PW.Solvers`、`PW.Behaviors`、`PW.Renderer`、`PW.Assets`），严禁修改 `./core/`。
- **坐标约定**：`+Y` 为正前方，`z = 0` 为地面，渲染器常数 `TILT = 0.6`、`HS = 0.8`、`BAKE_SCALE = 2.5`。

---

## 一、外观与四大标志记忆点（0.6 俯视角一眼认出「暴君王者」）

必须严格执行**「体貌特征放大 + 高对比 3 档色阶 + 独立 `partId` 切暗缝」**：

1. **歪戴的巨型嵌宝石锯齿金冠（`crown`）**：
   - 金灿灿的大锯齿王冠，冠额正中并排镶嵌**红、绿、蓝三颗大宝石**（呼应游戏三原色宝石机制！）；
   - 平时微微歪斜在脑门上随步伐轻晃，施法蓄力时宝石脉动，破盾硬直（`stagger`）时歪弹一下再滑回脑门。
2. **狞笑大头 + 超长横展双尖耳 + 獠牙大嘴（`head` + `earL`/`earR`）**：
   - 继承 `./references/goblin.js` 的苔绿肤色与黄橙邪眼（`eyeGlint` **绝不进 `glowKeys`**，确保 0.6 俯视角 0 遮挡、锐利不糊）；
   - 宽阔下颚露出两颗雪白上翘大獠牙；一对超长横展大尖耳（带金耳环）偏心锁死耳根枢轴，大笑、蓄力、受击时独立狂抖或耷拉。
3. **满溢金币与宝石的赤红王者披风 + 巨型黄金大宝袋（`cape` + `lootSack`）**：
   - 宽厚圆鼓的大肚腩，裹着带金边皮带的皮革战裙与厚重赤红大披风；
   - 背后背着一只鼓胀到快撑破的巨型黄金宝袋，袋口堆满金锭与红绿蓝晶石，行进时滞后颠簸并偶尔颠落金色碎粒。
4. **右手超巨型镶金狼牙重锤（`mace`）+ 粗壮二足重步**：
   - 双臂拆为**大臂 + 小臂**两段连续 FK 驱动（小臂滞后随动），右手紧握一把黑铁镶金刺狼牙重锤（柄根用 `pivotToWorldXY` 严格锁死在右手腕枢轴，挥砸零脱节！）；
   - 粗壮短腿深嵌战裙与大肚腩下方（遵循 `goblin.js` 防身腿分离铁律：腿根上延深嵌躯干 `≥ 3` 格、收拢步距、限制脚部最大偏移半径），迈步沉稳厚重。

---

## 二、三维几何坐标与部件拆分预算表

> **硬约束**：每个独立体素盒严格 `≤ 40` 格；总色表 **`≤ 42` 色、0 重色**；技能召唤的 2 只喽啰共享 1 套 `minionGoblin` 资产，6 枚飞散金币块共享 1 套 `goldChunk` 资产。

| 部件名 (`partKey`) | 体素盒 (`w×d×h`) | 基准位置 / 高度 (`z/lift`) | `bias` | 复用与优化说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `torso` | `24×20×18` | `(0, 0)`, `lift: 7.5` | `+0.10` | 宽厚圆鼓大肚腩 + 胸带金属扣 + 皮革战裙下沿（深包大腿根 3 格以上） |
| `cape` | `22×14×16` | `(0, -7.8)`, `lift: 8.5` | `-0.25` | 镶金边赤红王者短披风，随步态/冲锋在局部坐标系后扬，零穿模 |
| `lootSack` | `18×16×16` | `(0, -10.5)`, `lift: 13.5` | `-0.15` | 鼓胀巨型宝袋 + 袋口堆满金锭与红绿蓝晶石，带弹簧滞后颠簸 |
| `head` | `18×17×15` | `(0, +1.5)`, `lift: 21.0` | `+0.40` | 宽大狞笑头颅 + 精雕双邪眼 + 鹰钩鼻 + 露白獠牙大嘴（`eye/nose/mouth` 进 `noCrease`） |
| `crown` | `15×15×9` | 头顶挂点 `(0, +1.2)`, `lift: 32.5` | `+0.65` | 巨型锯齿金冠 + 额前红绿蓝三宝石，独立倾斜角（平时微歪，硬直大歪弹跳） |
| `earL` / `earR` | `10×6×7` | 头侧耳根 `(±8.2, +0.5)`, `lift: 26.5` | `+0.45` | 超长带金环大尖耳，枢轴锁死耳根，情绪抖动极富表现力 |
| `upperArm` / `forearm` | `8×10×8` / `7×10×7` | 肩位 `(±11.5, +1.2)`, `lift: 19.0` | `+0.30` | 左右对称复用两段式手臂，小臂相对大臂滞后随动 |
| `mace` | `12×24×12` | 右手腕枢轴锁死 | `+0.55` | 黑铁镶金巨型狼牙重锤（锤头含少量 `emissive` 蓄力火纹） |
| `leg`（左右复用） | `8×9×11` | 髋位 `(±4.8, 0)`, `lift: 0` | `-0.35` | 粗壮短腿 + 裹皮铁靴，顶部深嵌躯干内，杜绝身腿分离 |
| `goldChunk`（技能道具） | `7×7×6` | 技能 2 漫天抛洒（6 枚复用） | `+0.50` | 立体金锭/金币堆（`symDeg: 90` 省贴图），抛物线砸地弹跳 |
| `warBanner`（技能道具） | `14×6×26` | 技能 3 左手高举狂摇 | `+0.60` | 锯齿赤红部落战旗 + 金色骷髅/冠纹，摇旗产生声波环 |
| `minionGoblin`（技能道具） | `11×11×13` | 技能 3 召出 2 只复用 | `+0.25` | 持小木棒的迷你哥布林喽啰，2 只共享 1 份资产快步跃动 |

- **色表预算**：严格 **`≤ 42` 色、0 重色**。
- **`glowKeys` 严格隔离**：仅限 `['emissiveMid', 'emissiveHot', 'emissiveCore']`（用于蓄力锤头裂纹与王冠宝石爆发脉冲），**`eyeGlint`、`goldHi`、`metalHi` 严禁进 `glowKeys`**！

| 1 | `blood_warlord` | 赤皮战酋（荒野狂酋） | 暗赤褐 `#9c4632` | 焦褐 `#593825` | 熔铜 `#cf7d2b` + 骨白刺 |

## 四、动作清单与 4 大 Boss 技能演出 + 战斗机制设计

在 `Def.actions` 中登记全部 7 个动作，由 `eco.action` 与 `eco.actionTime` 连续驱动，`loop: false` 播完自动平滑归位：

```js
actions: [
  { id: 'patrol',            name: '霸道巡营',   loop: true,  status: 'done' },
  { id: 'idle',              name: '叉腰狞笑',   loop: true,  status: 'done' },
  { id: 'stagger',           name: '破盾歪冠',   loop: false, duration: 1.5, status: 'done' },
  { id: 'skill_mace_smash',  name: '霸王重锤',   loop: false, duration: 2.4, status: 'done' },
  { id: 'skill_gold_rain',   name: '撒币乱砸',   loop: false, duration: 2.8, status: 'done' },
  { id: 'skill_rally_horde', name: '摇旗召众',   loop: false, duration: 3.0, status: 'done' },
  { id: 'skill_greed_charge',name: '贪财蛮冲',   loop: false, duration: 3.2, status: 'done' }
]
```

### 技能 1：`skill_mace_smash` · 霸王重锤（时长 `2.4s`）
- **视觉演出**：
  - `0.0s ~ 0.85s`（高举蓄力）：整只后仰蓄劲，右手抡起巨型狼牙重锤高举过顶，锤头 `emissive` 脉冲亮起，双耳向后紧贴；
  - `0.85s ~ 1.45s`（重步猛砸）：大步前跨 `+5` 格，重锤沿清晰弧线狠砸前方地面，瞬间爆出 `vfx.addDecal` 裂纹焦痕（`kind: 'crack'`）+ 环形震波与飞溅碎石粒子；
  - `1.45s ~ 2.4s`（拔锤喘气回位）：用力把锤从地面拔起扛回肩侧，大步退回原位。
- **对应 code00 战斗机制**：重击扣主角 1 颗心（可被破盾推迟倒计时或打断）。

### 技能 2：`skill_gold_rain` · 撒币乱砸（时长 `2.8s`）
- **视觉演出**：
  - `0.0s ~ 0.6s`（反手抓宝 + 狂笑）：左手叉腰挺肚大笑（头仰、双耳高频狂抖、王冠乱晃），右手伸向背后大宝袋抓出一大把金块；
  - `0.6s ~ 2.1s`（漫天抛洒）：连续向正前方扇形抛射 **6 枚立体 `goldChunk` 金块**，各自沿不同高度抛物线落地弹跳 2 次并溅出金色粒子碎屑；
  - `2.1s ~ 2.8s`（拍肚得意归位）：拍两下圆鼓肚皮，重锤回手。
- **对应 code00 战斗机制**：扣半颗心并往弃牌堆塞入 1 张「沉重假币」废牌（同时暂夺少量金币，击杀哥布林王后双倍奉还！）。

### 技能 3：`skill_rally_horde` · 摇旗召众（时长 `3.0s`）
- **视觉演出**：
  - `0.0s ~ 0.9s`（高举战旗狂摇）：左手猛然擎出巨型赤红 `warBanner` 部落战旗左右大幅挥舞，头顶扩散出 2 道醒目红色声波粒子环；
  - `0.9s ~ 2.4s`（双喽啰窜出冲锋）：左右两侧尘土扬起，窜出 **2 只复用 `minionGoblin` 迷你喽啰**，绕着哥布林王前方高跳碎步冲锋挥棒呐喊；
  - `2.4s ~ 3.0s`（收旗退场）：喽啰跃回尘烟消散，哥布林王收起战旗恢复持锤警戒。
- **对应 code00 战斗机制**：召唤 2 只低血哥布林喽啰并给 Boss 补 2 格色盾，逼玩家用**补色合白**或**彩虹**一波清场！

### 技能 4：`skill_greed_charge` · 贪财蛮冲（时长 `3.2s`）
- **视觉演出**：
  - `0.0s ~ 1.05s`（低身刨地蓄势）：身体大幅前倾压低重心，重锤横抱胸前，双脚原地急促踏地扬起浓尘，王冠三色宝石与锤头高频亮脉冲；
  - `1.05s ~ 2.15s`（重坦蛮冲 + 沿途掉币）：整只向前猛冲 `+18` 格，身后留下泥土犁痕（`vfx.addDecal` `mound`）且大宝袋剧烈颠簸甩落一串金色粒子；终点猛然横扫一记大回旋重锤！
  - `2.15s ~ 3.2s`（挺肚慢步回位）：得意洋洋挺起肚腩、大步走回原位。
- **对应 code00 战斗机制**：蓄力 2 回合的终极大招（亮出红+蓝双色护盾 5+5 格），击破双色盾立即打断冲锋并触发 **`stagger`（破盾歪冠）**！

### 通用硬直：`stagger` · 破盾歪冠（时长 `1.5s`）
- 整只向后踉跄下沉 `2.5` 格，头顶巨型金冠猛然歪飞弹出 `4` 格再扣歪回脑门、大宝袋颠出金色粒子、双尖耳耷拉发抖，`1.5s` 后气急败坏伸手扶正王冠恢复警戒。


===== FILE: ./bundle/README.md =====
# `bundle/` 交付目录

外包方请将最终产出的 3 个文件按 `bundle/xxx` 结构直接打包成可下载的 **`bundle_boss_goblin_king.zip`** 交回：

1. `bundle/boss_goblin_king.js` —— 核心像素堆叠资产脚本（符合 `./core/registry.js` 与 `PW.Creatures.push` 规范）
2. `bundle/manifest.json` —— 资产与技能清单（id、部件尺寸、4 套皮肤、7 个动作与战斗机制说明）
3. `bundle/交付说明.md` —— 自检结果与实机注意点


===== FILE: ./core/behaviors.js =====
'use strict';

window.PixelWorkshop = window.PixelWorkshop || {};

(function (PW) {
  const { TAU, clamp, lerp, smoothstep, expSmooth, wrapAngle, fbm1, hash2i, localToWorldXY } = PW.Math;

  // =========================================================================
  // 自主生物探索导航器（Organic Wander Steering）
  // 告别机械匀速转圈！模拟真实生物：选点 -> 加速切弯游/走 -> 减速停顿观察 -> 突然折返转向
  // 正是「变速启停 + 曲率变化」把 P5.js 链式甩尾、弹簧回弹和四足迈步彻底逼活！
  // =========================================================================
  function stepOrganicTarget(state, dt, time, eco, cfg = {}) {
    const seed = eco.seed ?? 17;
    const maxR = eco.maxRadius ?? 46;
    const minHop = cfg.minHop ?? 22;
    const pauseMin = cfg.pauseMin ?? 0.7;
    const pauseMax = cfg.pauseMax ?? 1.9;

    if (state.navX === undefined) {
      state.navX = (eco.homeX ?? 0) + 20;
      state.navY = (eco.homeY ?? 0) + 12;
      state.navPause = 0;
      state.navSeq = 1;
    }

    if (state.navPause > 0) {
      state.navPause -= dt;
      return { x: state.navX, y: state.navY, pausing: true };
    }

    const d = Math.hypot(state.navX - state.x, state.navY - state.y);
    if (d < (cfg.arriveDist ?? 7.5)) {
      // 到达目标后短暂停顿观察，然后挑选一个新的折返点
      state.navSeq = (state.navSeq || 1) + 1;
      const r1 = hash2i(state.navSeq, seed, 101);
      const r2 = hash2i(state.navSeq, seed, 211);
      const r3 = hash2i(state.navSeq, seed, 307);
      state.navPause = lerp(pauseMin, pauseMax, r3);

      // 优先选与当前位置有明显角度差的新点，制造优雅的大弧线转弯
      const curAng = Math.atan2(state.y, state.x);
      const turnAng = curAng + (r1 > 0.5 ? 1 : -1) * lerp(1.15, 2.55, r2);
      const rad = lerp(minHop * 0.65, maxR, hash2i(state.navSeq, seed, 419));
      state.navX = (eco.centerX || 0) + Math.cos(turnAng) * rad;
      state.navY = (eco.centerY || 0) + Math.sin(turnAng) * rad;
      return { x: state.navX, y: state.navY, pausing: true };
    }

    return { x: state.navX, y: state.navY, pausing: false };
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // 第二层：可混搭「推进积木」（v2 生物感升级版）
  // =========================================================================
  const Propulsion = {
    // 1. 脉冲摆尾游泳（真实鱼类：一甩尾猛蹿 -> 惯性滑行 -> 急转切弯）
    pulseSwim(state, dt, time, params, eco) {
      const speed = params.speed ?? 1.0;
      const freq = (params.gaitFreq ?? 1.0) * 5.2;
      const patrol = eco.patrol !== false;

      if (!patrol) {
        // 原地鲜活悬停划水（缓慢轻微游移 + 尾波反扭）
        state.phase = (state.phase || 0) + dt * freq * 0.65;
        const beat = Math.sin(state.phase);
        const tx = (eco.homeX ?? 0) + Math.sin(time * 0.7) * 3.5;
        const ty = (eco.homeY ?? 0) + Math.cos(time * 0.55) * 3.0;
        const k = expSmooth(dt, 4.5);
        const nx = lerp(state.x, tx, k);
        const ny = lerp(state.y, ty, k);
        state.vx = (nx - state.x) / Math.max(dt, 0.001);
        state.vy = (ny - state.y) / Math.max(dt, 0.001);
        state.x = nx; state.y = ny;
        state.z = lerp(state.z, (eco.baseLift ?? 1.8) + Math.sin(state.phase * 1.6) * 0.85, k);
        const idleAim = (eco.homeYaw ?? 0) + Math.sin(time * 0.85) * 0.32 - beat * 0.12;
        state.yaw += wrapAngle(idleAim - state.yaw) * expSmooth(dt, 6.5);
        state.thrustPulse = Math.abs(beat) * 0.35;
        state.turnRate = 0;
        return state;
      }

      const nav = stepOrganicTarget(state, dt, time, eco, {
        minHop: 26, pauseMin: 0.35, pauseMax: 1.05, arriveDist: 9.0
      });

      const tx = nav.x + (eco.avoidX || 0);
      const ty = nav.y + (eco.avoidY || 0);
      const dx = tx - state.x, dy = ty - state.y;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 0.001;
      const desiredYaw = Math.atan2(-dx, dy);

      // 转向角速度受限（鱼类有最小转弯半径，甩出自然弧线！）
      const yawDiff = wrapAngle(desiredYaw - state.yaw);
      const maxTurn = (nav.pausing ? 2.1 : 3.4) * (0.65 + speed * 0.5);
      const turnStep = clamp(yawDiff, -maxTurn * dt, maxTurn * dt);
      state.yaw = wrapAngle(state.yaw + turnStep);
      state.turnRate = turnStep / Math.max(dt, 0.001);

      // 脉冲摆尾推力（Burst-and-Glide）：摆尾瞬间加速，滑行阶段靠水阻慢降
      state.phase = (state.phase || 0) + dt * freq * (nav.pausing ? 0.55 : (0.78 + speed * 0.42));
      const pulse = Math.pow(Math.abs(Math.sin(state.phase)), 2.2);
      const burstBoost = eco.burstBoost ?? 1.0;

      // 朝向与目标越对齐，往前冲得越快；大转弯时先减速切弯再加速蹿出
      const alignFactor = clamp(Math.cos(yawDiff), 0.18, 1.0);
      const targetSpd = nav.pausing
        ? (5.0 + pulse * 4.0) * speed
        : (14.5 + pulse * 26.0) * speed * alignFactor * burstBoost;

      const fwdX = -Math.sin(state.yaw), fwdY = Math.cos(state.yaw);
      const kVel = expSmooth(dt, nav.pausing ? 4.5 : 6.2);
      state.vx = lerp(state.vx, fwdX * targetSpd, kVel);
      state.vy = lerp(state.vy, fwdY * targetSpd, kVel);
      state.x += state.vx * dt;
      state.y += state.vy * dt;

      // 平台安全软边界
      const curD = Math.hypot(state.x, state.y);
      const maxR = eco.maxRadius ?? 50;
      if (curD > maxR) {
        state.x *= maxR / curD;
        state.y *= maxR / curD;
      }

      // 海豚式波浪潜浮（加速时微微破水昂头，滑行时轻潜）
      const dive = Math.sin(time * 2.1 * speed + (eco.seed || 0)) * 3.4 + pulse * 1.4;
      state.z = lerp(state.z, (eco.baseLift ?? 1.2) + dive, expSmooth(dt, 7.5));
      state.thrustPulse = pulse;
      return state;
    },

    // 2. 噪声随风漂浮（带真实惯性加速/停顿漂移，激发软体果冻晃动与垂腿摆荡！）
    windDrift(state, dt, time, params, eco) {
      const speed = params.speed ?? 1.0;
      const freq = params.gaitFreq ?? 1.0;
      const seed = eco.seed ?? 37;
      const patrol = eco.patrol !== false;
      const baseLift = eco.baseLift ?? 16.0;

      state.phase = (state.phase || 0) + dt * 2.3 * freq;

      let tx, ty, pausing = false;
      if (!patrol) {
        tx = (eco.homeX ?? 0) + (fbm1(time * 0.42 * speed, seed) - 0.5) * 14;
        ty = (eco.homeY ?? 0) + (fbm1(time * 0.38 * speed, seed + 53) - 0.5) * 14;
      } else {
        const nav = stepOrganicTarget(state, dt, time, eco, {
          minHop: 22, pauseMin: 1.0, pauseMax: 2.2, arriveDist: 8.0
        });
        pausing = nav.pausing;
        // 叠加柔和风场扰动
        tx = nav.x + (fbm1(time * 0.45, seed) - 0.5) * 10 + (eco.avoidX || 0);
        ty = nav.y + (fbm1(time * 0.42, seed + 71) - 0.5) * 10 + (eco.avoidY || 0);
      }

      // 模拟浮空惯性（推力 + 空气阻力），启停时有柔和过冲感！
      const dx = tx - state.x, dy = ty - state.y;
      const accel = (pausing ? 1.8 : 4.2) * (0.65 + speed * 0.55);
      const drag = pausing ? 2.4 : 1.75;
      state.vx += (dx * accel - state.vx * drag) * dt;
      state.vy += (dy * accel - state.vy * drag) * dt;
      const maxSpd = 28 * speed;
      const curSpd = Math.hypot(state.vx, state.vy);
      if (curSpd > maxSpd) { state.vx *= maxSpd / curSpd; state.vy *= maxSpd / curSpd; }

      state.x += state.vx * dt;
      state.y += state.vy * dt;

      const dCenter = Math.hypot(state.x, state.y);
      const maxR = eco.maxRadius ?? 50;
      if (dCenter > maxR) { state.x *= maxR / dCenter; state.y *= maxR / dCenter; }

      const bob = Math.sin(state.phase) * 2.8 + (fbm1(time * 0.55, seed + 19) - 0.5) * 4.2;
      state.z = lerp(state.z, baseLift + bob, expSmooth(dt, 4.8));

      const targetYaw = curSpd > 2.2
        ? Math.atan2(-state.vx, state.vy)
        : (eco.homeYaw ?? 0) + Math.sin(time * 0.75 + seed) * 0.45;
      state.yaw += wrapAngle(targetYaw - state.yaw) * expSmooth(dt, 3.2);
      state.thrustPulse = clamp(curSpd / 20, 0, 1);
      return state;
    },

    // 3. 多足差异化陆地移动（支持 heavy / stalk / scurry / crawl 四种生物移动性格）
    //    彻底告别「扫地机器人匀速平推 + 背向硬蹭」：先转头拧身对准方向，再带蹬地步态脉动迈步！
    multiLegWalk(state, dt, time, params, eco) {
      const speed = params.speed ?? 1.0;
      const gFreq = params.gaitFreq ?? 1.0;
      const patrol = eco.patrol !== false;
      const pauseFactor = eco.pauseFactor ?? 1.0;

      const WALK_STYLES = {
        // 沉重巨兽（犀牛/大地懒/牛犊慢走）：稳重慢转、步幅长、停顿久、每步带沉重蹬地顿挫
        heavy:  { baseSpd: 11.8, turnWalk: 1.55, turnPause: 1.15, minHop: 22, pauseMin: 1.4, pauseMax: 2.6, arriveDist: 7.5, velSmooth: 4.4, pulseAmp: 0.24, pulseFreq: 3.1, burstChance: 0 },
        // 敏捷潜行猛兽（五尾红豹/狼鲸）：急转切弯、停顿观察后爆发小跑、刹车干脆
        stalk:  { baseSpd: 19.2, turnWalk: 3.55, turnPause: 2.35, minHop: 24, pauseMin: 0.45, pauseMax: 1.35, arriveDist: 7.0, velSmooth: 7.8, pulseAmp: 0.26, pulseFreq: 5.8, burstChance: 0.45 },
        // 碎步灵动小型/二足（哥布林/竹鸡/尾手水兽）：高频短距折返、转向极快、走走停停张望
        scurry: { baseSpd: 16.0, turnWalk: 4.65, turnPause: 3.10, minHop: 14, pauseMin: 0.35, pauseMax: 1.15, arriveDist: 5.8, velSmooth: 9.2, pulseAmp: 0.28, pulseFreq: 7.6, burstChance: 0.35 },
        // 软体脉冲蠕动（拼骨幼虫）：仅伸展推进相挪动，停顿相几乎静止
        crawl:  { baseSpd: 8.8,  turnWalk: 2.40, turnPause: 1.80, minHop: 13, pauseMin: 0.70, pauseMax: 1.60, arriveDist: 5.5, velSmooth: 6.0, pulseAmp: 0.72, pulseFreq: 3.8, burstChance: 0 },
        // 兼容未显式指定的旧脚本：比原版更自然，不再背向硬蹭且带轻柔步伐脉动
        default:{ baseSpd: 14.5, turnWalk: 2.35, turnPause: 1.55, minHop: 20, pauseMin: 1.0, pauseMax: 2.1, arriveDist: 7.0, velSmooth: 5.8, pulseAmp: 0.22, pulseFreq: 4.4, burstChance: 0 }
      };
      const st = WALK_STYLES[eco.walkStyle] || WALK_STYLES.default;

      if (!patrol || pauseFactor < 0.05) {
        const k = expSmooth(dt, 7.5);
        state.vx = lerp(state.vx, 0, k);
        state.vy = lerp(state.vy, 0, k);
        state.x += state.vx * dt;
        state.y += state.vy * dt;
        state.z = lerp(state.z, eco.baseLift ?? 8.8, expSmooth(dt, 8));
        if (!patrol) {
          const idleYaw = (eco.homeYaw ?? 0) + Math.sin(time * 0.75) * 0.20;
          state.yaw += wrapAngle(idleYaw - state.yaw) * expSmooth(dt, 4.0);
        }
        state.thrustPulse = 0;
        state.turnRate = 0;
        return state;
      }

      const nav = stepOrganicTarget(state, dt, time, eco, {
        minHop: st.minHop,
        pauseMin: st.pauseMin,
        pauseMax: st.pauseMax,
        arriveDist: st.arriveDist
      });

      const tx = nav.x + (eco.avoidX || 0);
      const ty = nav.y + (eco.avoidY || 0);
      const dx = tx - state.x, dy = ty - state.y;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 0.001;
      const desiredYaw = Math.atan2(-dx, dy);
      const yawDiff = wrapAngle(desiredYaw - state.yaw);

      // 转向：大角度调头时先收住前进速度，稳稳拧身对准目标再起步（彻底消除背向硬蹭！）
      const maxTurn = (nav.pausing ? st.turnPause : st.turnWalk) * (0.68 + speed * 0.42);
      const turnStep = clamp(yawDiff, -maxTurn * dt, maxTurn * dt);
      state.yaw = wrapAngle(state.yaw + turnStep);
      state.turnRate = turnStep / Math.max(dt, 0.001);

      // 步态蹬地脉动相位：每一步有「发力推进 -> 落脚微滞」节律，拒绝气垫船匀速平推
      state.walkPhase = (state.walkPhase || 0) + dt * st.pulseFreq * gFreq * (0.72 + speed * 0.36);
      const rawWave = Math.sin(state.walkPhase);
      const strideMod = eco.walkStyle === 'crawl'
        ? (0.12 + Math.pow(Math.max(0, rawWave), 1.6) * 1.45)
        : (1.0 - st.pulseAmp * 0.45 + Math.abs(rawWave) * st.pulseAmp);

      // 敏捷/窜行生物按 navSeq 交替出现“快步窜段”与“慢步审视段”
      const burstSeed = hash2i(state.navSeq || 1, eco.seed ?? 17, 613);
      const burstMul = (st.burstChance > 0 && burstSeed < st.burstChance) ? 1.24 : 0.92;

      // 自然圆弧拐弯：不再像坦克那样停死原地干转！
      // 在场地内保持 0.30~1.0 的弧线切弯前向速度，顺滑划出弧线转向；仅停顿或大角度急掉头时适当收慢
      const minArc = eco.walkStyle === 'stalk' ? 0.42 : (eco.walkStyle === 'scurry' ? 0.34 : 0.26);
      const align = lerp(minArc, 1.0, smoothstep(-0.35, 0.85, Math.cos(yawDiff)));
      const nearSlow = smoothstep(st.arriveDist * 0.30, st.arriveDist * 1.55, dist);
      const walkSpd = nav.pausing ? 0 : (st.baseSpd * speed * burstMul * align * nearSlow * strideMod * pauseFactor);
      const fwdX = -Math.sin(state.yaw), fwdY = Math.cos(state.yaw);

      const kVel = expSmooth(dt, nav.pausing ? st.velSmooth * 1.35 : st.velSmooth);
      state.vx = lerp(state.vx, fwdX * walkSpd, kVel);
      state.vy = lerp(state.vy, fwdY * walkSpd, kVel);
      state.x += state.vx * dt;
      state.y += state.vy * dt;

      const curD = Math.hypot(state.x, state.y);
      const maxR = eco.maxRadius ?? 46;
      if (curD > maxR) { state.x *= maxR / curD; state.y *= maxR / curD; }

      state.z = lerp(state.z, eco.baseLift ?? 8.8, expSmooth(dt, 9));
      state.thrustPulse = clamp(Math.hypot(state.vx, state.vy) / Math.max(8, st.baseSpd), 0, 1);
      return state;
    },

    // 3b. 定点自然走位（供交互归位/接近目标使用，彻底取代 lerp 横移滑冰！）
    //     返回 { dist, arrived, yawDiff }
    walkToPoint(state, tx, ty, dt, params, cfg = {}) {
      const speed = params.speed ?? 1.0;
      const gFreq = params.gaitFreq ?? 1.0;
      const arriveDist = cfg.arriveDist ?? 2.2;
      const maxSpeed = (cfg.maxSpeed ?? 14.5) * speed;
      const maxTurn = (cfg.maxTurn ?? 3.6) * (0.7 + speed * 0.38);
      const pulseAmp = cfg.pulseAmp ?? 0.20;
      const pulseFreq = cfg.pulseFreq ?? 4.8;

      const dx = tx - state.x, dy = ty - state.y;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);

      if (dist <= arriveDist) {
        // 到位后自然刹停并缓缓转向目标朝向（不横移滑冰）
        const kStop = expSmooth(dt, 8.8);
        state.vx = lerp(state.vx, 0, kStop);
        state.vy = lerp(state.vy, 0, kStop);
        state.x += state.vx * dt;
        state.y += state.vy * dt;
        let yawDiff = 0;
        if (cfg.aimYaw !== undefined) {
          yawDiff = wrapAngle(cfg.aimYaw - state.yaw);
          const step = clamp(yawDiff, -maxTurn * 0.9 * dt, maxTurn * 0.9 * dt);
          state.yaw = wrapAngle(state.yaw + step);
          state.turnRate = step / Math.max(dt, 0.001);
        } else {
          state.turnRate = 0;
        }
        if (cfg.baseLift !== undefined) {
          state.z = lerp(state.z, cfg.baseLift, expSmooth(dt, 8.5));
        }
        state.thrustPulse = 0;
        return { dist, arrived: true, yawDiff };
      }

      const desiredYaw = Math.atan2(-dx, dy);
      const yawDiff = wrapAngle(desiredYaw - state.yaw);
      const step = clamp(yawDiff, -maxTurn * dt, maxTurn * dt);
      state.yaw = wrapAngle(state.yaw + step);
      state.turnRate = step / Math.max(dt, 0.001);

      state.walkPhase = (state.walkPhase || 0) + dt * pulseFreq * gFreq;
      const strideMod = 1.0 - pulseAmp * 0.45 + Math.abs(Math.sin(state.walkPhase)) * pulseAmp;
      const align = smoothstep(-0.05, 0.72, Math.cos(yawDiff));
      const nearSlow = clamp(dist / Math.max(arriveDist * 3.0, 7.5), 0.32, 1.0);
      const walkSpd = maxSpeed * align * nearSlow * strideMod;

      const fwdX = -Math.sin(state.yaw), fwdY = Math.cos(state.yaw);
      const kVel = expSmooth(dt, 7.2);
      state.vx = lerp(state.vx, fwdX * walkSpd, kVel);
      state.vy = lerp(state.vy, fwdY * walkSpd, kVel);
      state.x += state.vx * dt;
      state.y += state.vy * dt;

      if (cfg.baseLift !== undefined) {
        state.z = lerp(state.z, cfg.baseLift, expSmooth(dt, 8.5));
      }
      state.thrustPulse = clamp(Math.hypot(state.vx, state.vy) / Math.max(8, maxSpeed), 0, 1);
      return { dist, arrived: false, yawDiff };
    },

    wingFlap(state, dt, time, params, eco) {
      const freq = (params.gaitFreq ?? 1.0) * 6.2;
      state.phase = (state.phase || 0) + dt * freq;
      const liftLag = Math.sin(state.phase - 1.15) * 2.4;
      const res = Propulsion.windDrift(state, dt, time, params, { ...eco, baseLift: (eco.baseLift ?? 18) + liftLag });
      res.wingStroke = Math.sin(state.phase);
      return res;
    },

    chargeJump(state, dt, time, params, eco) {
      const freq = (params.gaitFreq ?? 1.0) * 1.35;
      const cy = (time * freq + (eco.seed || 0)) % 1;
      state.jumpCycle = cy;
      if (cy < 0.25) {
        state.z = 0;
        state.squashZ = lerp(1.0, 0.72, cy / 0.25);
      } else if (cy < 0.80) {
        const u = (cy - 0.25) / 0.55;
        state.z = Math.sin(u * Math.PI) * 14;
        state.squashZ = 1 + Math.sin(u * Math.PI) * 0.22;
      } else {
        const u = (cy - 0.80) / 0.20;
        state.z = 0;
        state.squashZ = 1 - Math.sin(u * Math.PI) * 0.18;
      }
      return state;
    },

    rooted(state, dt, time, params, eco) {
      state.x = eco.homeX ?? 0;
      state.y = eco.homeY ?? 0;
      state.z = eco.baseLift ?? 0;
      state.vx = 0; state.vy = 0;
      state.yaw = (eco.homeYaw ?? 0) + Math.sin(time * 1.2) * 0.08;
      return state;
    }
  };

  // =========================================================================
  // 第二层：环境反馈积木（破水白沫、半潜淡化、落地扬尘、高度接地印）
  // =========================================================================
  const EnvFeedback = {
    calcSubmerge(z, waterLevel = 2.6, range = 5.2, isWater = true) {
      if (!isWater) return 0;
      return clamp((waterLevel - z) / range, 0, 1);
    },

    emitFootstep(vfx, habitat, x, y, power = 1.0) {
      if (habitat.isWater) {
        vfx.addRipple(x, y, 2.5, 14 * power, 0.62, 1.5);
        vfx.spawnSplash(x, y, Math.round(4 * power), power * 0.7, habitat.fx);
      } else {
        vfx.spawnDust(x, y, Math.round(5 * power), power, habitat.fx);
      }
    },

    emitWaterWake(vfx, habitat, x, y, yaw, width, power = 1.0) {
      if (!habitat.isWater) return;
      vfx.addWake(x, y, yaw, width, 1.12);
      if (power > 0.72) {
        vfx.addRipple(x, y, 3, 18, 0.70, 1.7);
      }
    },

    calcGroundShadow(lift, footRadius) {
      const hFactor = clamp(1 - lift / 54, 0.22, 1.0);
      return {
        alpha: 0.46 * hFactor,
        radius: footRadius * (0.58 + 0.42 * hFactor),
        offsetX: 1.6 + lift * 0.14,
        offsetY: -1.6 - lift * 0.14
      };
    }
  };

  function computeEcoAvoidance(self, allActors) {
    let ax = 0, ay = 0;
    const mySize = self.def.ecoWeight ?? 1.0;
    for (const other of allActors) {
      if (other === self) continue;
      const dx = self.state.x - other.state.x;
      const dy = self.state.y - other.state.y;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 0.01;
      const otherSize = other.def.ecoWeight ?? 1.0;
      const safeDist = 20 + (mySize + otherSize) * 6.5;
      if (d < safeDist) {
        const repel = ((safeDist - d) / safeDist) * (otherSize > mySize ? 18 : 9);
        ax += (dx / d) * repel;
        ay += (dy / d) * repel;
      }
    }
    return { avoidX: ax, avoidY: ay };
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // 第三层：通用「伏击流程」状态机（供 M19 装成静物 / M27 陆上弹射共用）
  // 阶段：disguise(伪装静伏) -> tense(察觉绷紧) -> strike(迅速出手)
  //       -> digest(吞下鼓动) -> recover(缓慢复原/破绽) -> [relocate(极慢挪位)] -> disguise
  // =========================================================================
  function createAmbushCycle(cfg = {}) {
    const seed = cfg.seed ?? 53;
    const rngRange = (range, seq, salt, defMin, defMax) => {
      const a = Array.isArray(range) ? range[0] : (range ?? defMin);
      const b = Array.isArray(range) ? range[1] : (range ?? defMax);
      return lerp(a, b, hash2i(seq, seed, salt));
    };

    const st = {
      mode: 'disguise',
      prevMode: 'disguise',
      t: 0,
      dur: 9.0,
      seq: 1,
      cycleCount: 0,
      tremorCd: 2.8,
      tremorT: -1
    };

    function enter(nextMode, customDur) {
      st.prevMode = st.mode;
      st.mode = nextMode;
      st.t = 0;
      st.seq++;
      if (customDur != null) {
        st.dur = Math.max(0.05, customDur);
      } else if (nextMode === 'disguise') {
        st.dur = rngRange(cfg.disguiseDur, st.seq, 101, 8.0, 14.0);
        st.tremorCd = rngRange(cfg.tremorInterval, st.seq, 107, 2.2, 4.4);
        st.tremorT = -1;
      } else if (nextMode === 'tense') {
        st.dur = rngRange(cfg.tenseDur, st.seq, 211, 0.32, 0.56);
      } else if (nextMode === 'strike') {
        st.dur = rngRange(cfg.strikeDur, st.seq, 307, 0.25, 0.40);
      } else if (nextMode === 'digest') {
        st.dur = rngRange(cfg.digestDur, st.seq, 409, 1.5, 2.2);
      } else if (nextMode === 'recover') {
        st.dur = rngRange(cfg.recoverDur, st.seq, 503, 3.0, 4.5);
      } else if (nextMode === 'relocate') {
        st.dur = rngRange(cfg.relocateDur, st.seq, 607, 2.4, 3.8);
      } else {
        st.dur = 2.0;
      }
    }

    function reset(initialMode = 'disguise') {
      st.seq = 1;
      st.cycleCount = 0;
      enter(initialMode);
    }

    function trigger() {
      if (st.mode === 'disguise') {
        enter('tense');
        return true;
      }
      return false;
    }

    function forceState(mode, customDur) {
      enter(mode, customDur);
    }

    function update(dt, opts = {}) {
      const speed = clamp(opts.speed ?? 1.0, 0.2, 3.0);
      const autoTrigger = opts.autoTrigger !== false;
      const canRelocate = opts.canRelocate !== false;
      const relocateEvery = cfg.relocateEvery ?? 2;

      const beforeMode = st.mode;
      st.t += dt * speed;

      // 伪装态偶发微颤（0..1 脉冲，让静伏也有活体呼吸感）
      let tremor = 0;
      if (st.mode === 'disguise') {
        st.tremorCd -= dt * speed;
        if (st.tremorCd <= 0 && st.tremorT < 0) {
          st.tremorT = 0;
        }
        if (st.tremorT >= 0) {
          st.tremorT += dt * speed;
          const trDur = 0.42;
          if (st.tremorT >= trDur) {
            st.tremorT = -1;
            st.tremorCd = rngRange(cfg.tremorInterval, st.seq + Math.floor(st.t * 3), 173, 2.4, 4.8);
          } else {
            tremor = Math.sin((st.tremorT / trDur) * Math.PI);
          }
        }
      }

      if (st.t >= st.dur) {
        if (st.mode === 'disguise') {
          if (autoTrigger) {
            enter('tense');
          } else if (canRelocate && relocateEvery > 0) {
            enter('relocate');
          } else {
            enter('disguise');
          }
        } else if (st.mode === 'tense') {
          enter('strike');
        } else if (st.mode === 'strike') {
          enter('digest');
        } else if (st.mode === 'digest') {
          enter('recover');
        } else if (st.mode === 'recover') {
          st.cycleCount++;
          if (canRelocate && relocateEvery > 0 && (st.cycleCount % relocateEvery === 0)) {
            enter('relocate');
          } else {
            enter('disguise');
          }
        } else {
          enter('disguise');
        }
      }

      return {
        mode: st.mode,
        prevMode: beforeMode,
        changed: st.mode !== beforeMode,
        t: st.t,
        dur: st.dur,
        p: clamp(st.t / Math.max(0.01, st.dur), 0, 1),
        tremor,
        seq: st.seq,
        cycleCount: st.cycleCount
      };
    }

    reset('disguise');
    return { state: st, reset, trigger, forceState, update };
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // 第四层：跨个体挂点搭载 + 宿主受扰/蹭树生态积木（供 M30 / M36 / 停靠小鸟共用）
  // 宿主规约：
  //   - 显式挂点：inst.mountSockets = [{ id, x, y, z, yaw, r?, occupiedBy }]，每帧刷新
  //   - 普通宿主：无 mountSockets 时自动按 state + footRadius/backZ 估算默认背心挂点
  // =========================================================================
  function isValidMountHost(actor, opts = {}) {
    if (!actor || !actor.state || !actor.def) return false;
    if (actor.def.noHost) return false;
    if (!opts.allowPlaceholders && actor.isPlaceholder) return false;
    if (actor.state.mode === 'burrow' || (actor.state.shadowScale !== undefined && actor.state.shadowScale <= 0.02)) {
      return false;
    }
    const r = (actor.def.footRadius || 10) * (actor.scaleMul || 1);
    if (r < (opts.minFootRadius ?? 6)) return false;
    return true;
  }

  function estimateAutoBackHeight(host, footR) {
    if (host.def.mountHeight != null) return host.def.mountHeight * (host.scaleMul || 1);
    if (host.def.backZ != null) return host.def.backZ * (host.scaleMul || 1);
    if (host._autoBackH == null) {
      let est = (host.def.headZ ?? Math.max(9, footR * 1.15));
      if (host.parts && typeof host.parts === 'object') {
        let bestBodyH = 0;
        for (const k in host.parts) {
          const a = host.parts[k];
          if (a && a.h > bestBodyH) bestBodyH = a.h;
        }
        if (bestBodyH > 0) {
          // 多数四足/直立怪躯干抬升约 footR*0.65，取顶高约 82% 落在肩背表皮处
          est = Math.max(est, bestBodyH * 0.86);
        }
      }
      host._autoBackH = clamp(est, 8, Math.max(28, footR * 2.1));
    }
    return host._autoBackH * (host.scaleMul || 1);
  }

  function updateAutoBackSocket(host) {
    const sMul = host.scaleMul || 1;
    const footR = (host.def.footRadius || 10) * sMul;
    const backOff = host.def.mountBackOffset ?? (-footR * 0.12);
    const baseZ = estimateAutoBackHeight(host, footR / sMul);
    const [wx, wy] = localToWorldXY(host.state.x, host.state.y, host.state.yaw || 0, 0, backOff);
    if (!host._autoMountSockets) {
      host._autoMountSockets = [{ id: 'auto_back', x: wx, y: wy, z: (host.state.z || 0) + baseZ, yaw: host.state.yaw || 0, r: footR * 0.55, occupiedBy: null, auto: true }];
    } else {
      const slot = host._autoMountSockets[0];
      slot.x = wx;
      slot.y = wy;
      slot.z = (host.state.z || 0) + baseZ;
      slot.yaw = host.state.yaw || 0;
      slot.r = footR * 0.55;
    }
    return host._autoMountSockets;
  }

  const MountSystem = {
    // 取得宿主的所有可用挂点（显式 mountSockets 优先，否则自动估算背心挂点）
    ensureMountSockets(host) {
      if (!host || !host.state) return [];
      if (Array.isArray(host.mountSockets) && host.mountSockets.length > 0) {
        return host.mountSockets;
      }
      return updateAutoBackSocket(host);
    },

    // 在同台生物中搜寻一个空闲挂点
    findMountHost(self, allActors, opts = {}) {
      if (!Array.isArray(allActors) || allActors.length === 0) return null;
      const riderId = opts.riderId || (self && self.cachePrefix) || 'rider';
      const preferredCodes = opts.preferredCodes || null;
      const excludeCodes = opts.excludeCodes || null;
      const excludeHost = opts.excludeHost || null;
      const maxDist = opts.maxDist ?? 160;
      const sx = self && self.state ? self.state.x : (opts.x || 0);
      const sy = self && self.state ? self.state.y : (opts.y || 0);

      let best = null;
      let bestScore = -Infinity;

      for (let i = 0; i < allActors.length; i++) {
        const actor = allActors[i];
        if (!actor || actor === self || actor === excludeHost) continue;
        if (!isValidMountHost(actor, opts)) continue;
        const code = (actor.ecoData && actor.ecoData.code) || actor.def.code || '';
        if (excludeCodes && excludeCodes.includes(code)) continue;

        const sockets = MountSystem.ensureMountSockets(actor);
        if (!sockets.length) continue;

        let freeSlot = null;
        for (let k = 0; k < sockets.length; k++) {
          const sl = sockets[k];
          if (!sl.occupiedBy || sl.occupiedBy === riderId) {
            freeSlot = sl;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (!freeSlot) continue;

        const dist = Math.hypot(freeSlot.x - sx, freeSlot.y - sy);
        if (dist > maxDist) continue;

        let score = 100 - dist * 0.45 + (actor.def.footRadius || 10) * 1.4;
        if (preferredCodes && preferredCodes.includes(code)) score += 120;
        if (opts.preferExplicit && !freeSlot.auto) score += 60;
        if (score > bestScore) {
          bestScore = score;
          best = { host: actor, slot: freeSlot };
        }
      }
      return best;
    },

    claimMount(riderId, host, slot) {
      if (!host || !slot) return false;
      if (slot.occupiedBy && slot.occupiedBy !== riderId) return false;
      slot.occupiedBy = riderId;
      if (host.state) {
        host.state._riderCount = (host.state._riderCount || 0) + 1;
      }
      return true;
    },

    releaseMount(riderId, allActors) {
      if (!riderId || !Array.isArray(allActors)) return;
      for (let i = 0; i < allActors.length; i++) {
        const actor = allActors[i];
        if (!actor) continue;
        const lists = [actor.mountSockets, actor._autoMountSockets];
        for (let l = 0; l < lists.length; l++) {
          const arr = lists[l];
          if (!Array.isArray(arr)) continue;
          for (let k = 0; k < arr.length; k++) {
            if (arr[k] && arr[k].occupiedBy === riderId) {
              arr[k].occupiedBy = null;
              if (actor.state && actor.state._riderCount > 0) actor.state._riderCount--;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    },

    // 读取挂点最新世界姿态（自动刷新默认背心挂点）
    getMountWorldPose(host, slot, offsetLocal) {
      if (!host || !slot || !host.state) return null;
      if (slot.auto) updateAutoBackSocket(host);
      const alive = isValidMountHost(host, { allowPlaceholders: true, minFootRadius: 0 });
      let x = slot.x, y = slot.y, z = slot.z;
      const yaw = slot.yaw ?? host.state.yaw ?? 0;
      if (offsetLocal) {
        const [ox, oy] = localToWorldXY(x, y, yaw, offsetLocal.lx || 0, offsetLocal.ly || 0);
        x = ox;
        y = oy;
        z += offsetLocal.lz || 0;
      }
      return { x, y, z, yaw, alive };
    },

    // 飞近 / 弹跳接近并停靠上宿主挂点
    stepApproachAndMount(riderState, host, slot, dt, params, cfg = {}) {
      const pose = MountSystem.getMountWorldPose(host, slot, cfg.offsetLocal);
      if (!pose || !pose.alive) return { mounted: false, lost: true, dist: Infinity };
      const speed = (cfg.speed ?? 26) * (params && params.speed ? params.speed : 1);
      const arriveDist = cfg.arriveDist ?? 2.4;
      const dx = pose.x - riderState.x;
      const dy = pose.y - riderState.y;
      const dz = pose.z - riderState.z;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);

      if (dist <= arriveDist) {
        riderState.x = pose.x;
        riderState.y = pose.y;
        riderState.z = pose.z;
        riderState.vx = 0;
        riderState.vy = 0;
        riderState.sortBias = Math.max((host.def.footRadius || 10) * (host.scaleMul || 1) * 1.15, 16);
        riderState.yaw = cfg.matchYaw !== false
          ? wrapAngle(pose.yaw + (cfg.yawOffset || 0))
          : riderState.yaw;
        return { mounted: true, lost: false, dist: 0, x: pose.x, y: pose.y, z: pose.z, yaw: pose.yaw };
      }

      const step = Math.min(dist, speed * dt);
      const nx = riderState.x + (dx / Math.max(dist, 0.001)) * step;
      const ny = riderState.y + (dy / Math.max(dist, 0.001)) * step;
      const arc = cfg.arcLift ? Math.sin(clamp(dist / Math.max(cfg.arcSpan || 28, 6), 0, 1) * Math.PI) * cfg.arcLift : 0;
      const nz = lerp(riderState.z, pose.z + arc, expSmooth(dt, 7.5));
      riderState.vx = (nx - riderState.x) / Math.max(dt, 0.001);
      riderState.vy = (ny - riderState.y) / Math.max(dt, 0.001);
      riderState.x = nx;
      riderState.y = ny;
      riderState.z = nz;
      riderState.yaw = wrapAngle(riderState.yaw + wrapAngle(Math.atan2(-dx, dy) - riderState.yaw) * expSmooth(dt, 9.0));
      return { mounted: false, lost: false, dist, x: nx, y: ny, z: nz, yaw: riderState.yaw };
    }
  };

  // 宿主受寄生/附着扰动反馈（供宿主微晃甩身或去找树干蹭背）
  function notifyHostDisturb(host, riderId, intensity = 1.0) {
    if (!host || !host.state) return;
    host.state._disturbTimer = Math.max(host.state._disturbTimer || 0, 1.4);
    host.state._disturbIntensity = clamp(Math.max(host.state._disturbIntensity || 0, intensity), 0, 2);
    host.state._disturbBy = riderId || null;
  }

  function stepHostDisturb(state, dt, time) {
    if (!state || !state._disturbTimer || state._disturbTimer <= 0) {
      return { active: false, shakeYaw: 0, shakeX: 0, shakeY: 0, wantRub: false, intensity: 0 };
    }
    state._disturbTimer = Math.max(0, state._disturbTimer - dt);
    const env = clamp(state._disturbTimer / 1.4, 0, 1);
    const inten = (state._disturbIntensity || 1.0) * env;
    const w = Math.sin(time * 24.0);
    // 偶发产生蹭树冲动，便于大型宿主脚本在巡游中转向木桩/边缘蹭背
    const wantRub = inten > 0.45 && Math.sin(time * 1.1) > 0.65;
    if (state._disturbTimer <= 0) {
      state._disturbIntensity = 0;
      state._disturbBy = null;
    }
    return {
      active: true,
      shakeYaw: w * 0.09 * inten,
      shakeX: w * 0.45 * inten,
      shakeY: Math.cos(time * 24.0) * 0.35 * inten,
      wantRub,
      intensity: inten
    };
  }

  PW.Behaviors = {
    Propulsion,
    EnvFeedback,
    computeEcoAvoidance,
    createAmbushCycle,
    MountSystem,
    notifyHostDisturb,
    stepHostDisturb
  };
})(window.PixelWorkshop);


===== FILE: ./core/habitats.js =====

    // --- 新增生态生境（T19~T21）---

    {
      id: 'hotspring', code: 'T21', name: '热泉梯田', short: '热泉梯田', group: '地下特殊', isWater: true, waterLevel: 1.6, isNew: true,
      desc: '石灰矿壳台阶与橙黄绿三色热水菌毯',
      skyTop: '#1d1a17', skyMid: '#3a332c', skyBot: '#6b5e4e', skyHalo: 'rgba(255, 196, 140, 0.20)',
      fx: { ringRGB: '150, 220, 220', foamRGB: '240, 250, 248', dustRGB: '210, 200, 186', waterTint: 'rgba(47, 143, 154, 0.26)', accent: '#e88a3c' },

  ];

  const HABITAT_MAP = Object.create(null);
  for (const h of HABITAT_DEFS) HABITAT_MAP[h.id] = h;

  const platformCache = Object.create(null);

  function buildHabitatPlatform(habitatId, makeCanvas, size = 200) {
    const def = HABITAT_MAP[habitatId] || HABITAT_DEFS[0];
    if (platformCache[def.id]) return platformCache[def.id];

    const base = makeCanvas(size, size);
    const bg = base.getContext('2d');
    const bim = bg.createImageData(size, size);
    const half = size * 0.5;
    const rOuter = size * 0.485;
    const rRim = size * 0.435;
    const rInner = size * 0.405;

    const rgba = {};
    for (const k in def.pal) rgba[k] = hexToRgba(def.pal[k]);

    for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) {
      for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
        const dx = x - half, dy = y - half;
        const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (dist > rOuter) continue;

        const ang = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
        const wave = Math.sin(ang * 8) * 1.3 + Math.cos(ang * 5) * 0.9;
        const n1 = periodicNoise2(x, y, 64, 64, 16, 19);
        const n2 = periodicNoise2(x + 13, y + 7, 32, 32, 8, 47);
        const v = n1 * 0.62 + n2 * 0.38;

        let key = 'mid';
        if (def.id === 'tavern') {
          if (dist > rRim) {
            const seg = ((ang + Math.PI) / (Math.PI * 2)) * 32;
            const seam = Math.abs(seg - Math.round(seg)) < 0.08;
            if (dist > rOuter - 2.0) key = 'deep';
            else if (seam) key = 'deep';
            else key = ((Math.floor(seg) & 1) ? (v > 0.56 ? 'stoneHi' : 'rimB') : 'rimA');
          } else if (dist > rInner) {
            const seg = ((ang + Math.PI) / (Math.PI * 2)) * 28;
            const seam = Math.abs(seg - Math.round(seg)) < 0.08;
            const isHearthArc = Math.abs(ang + Math.PI * 0.5) < 0.46 && dist < rInner + 3.2;
            if (isHearthArc && ((Math.floor(seg * 2) & 1) === 0)) key = 'hearth';
            else if (seam || dist < rInner + 1.2) key = 'deep';
            else key = ((Math.floor(seg) & 1) ? 'rimB' : (v > 0.58 ? 'stoneHi' : 'rimA'));
          } else if (dist <= 44.0) {
            // 中央金边绛红圆形编织地毯
            const weave = ((x + y) & 1) ^ (((x >> 1) + (y >> 1)) & 1);
            const petal = Math.sin(ang * 16);
            if (dist > 41.6) {
              key = (petal > 0.15) ? 'speck' : 'rugDark';
            } else if (dist > 37.8) {
              const motif = Math.abs(Math.sin(ang * 12));
              key = (dist > 39.2 && dist < 40.4 && motif > 0.45) ? 'rugDark' : 'speck';
            } else if (dist > 17.5 && dist < 20.5) {
              key = (Math.sin(ang * 8) > -0.25) ? 'speck' : 'rugHi';
            } else if (dist < 7.5) {
              const centerDiamond = Math.abs(dx) + Math.abs(dy);
              key = centerDiamond < 5.2 ? 'speck' : 'rugHi';
            } else {
              if (v > 0.68 && weave) key = 'rugHi';
              else if (v < 0.34 || !weave) key = 'rugDark';
              else key = 'foam';
            }
          } else {
            // 深暖胡桃木拼接长条木板（板缝清晰、带微木纹）
            const plankH = 11;
            const rowIdx = Math.floor(y / plankH);
            const inRowY = y - rowIdx * plankH;
            const jointX = (x + rowIdx * 37) % 36;
            const grain = Math.sin(x * 0.16 + rowIdx * 1.9 + n1 * 3.2);
            if (inRowY === 0 || jointX === 0) {
              key = 'deep';
            } else if (inRowY === 1) {
              key = grain > 0.15 ? 'hi' : 'mid';
            } else if (inRowY === plankH - 1) {
              key = 'deep';
            } else {
              const rowTone = (rowIdx * 13) % 3;
              if (grain > 0.48 || (rowTone === 0 && v > 0.56)) key = 'hi';
              else if (grain < -0.58 && rowTone === 2) key = 'deep';
              else key = 'mid';
            }
          }
        } else if (dist > rRim + wave * 0.45) {
          key = (dist > rOuter - 2.2 || v > 0.66) ? 'foam' : (v < 0.38 ? 'deep' : 'mid');
        } else if (dist > rInner + wave * 0.4) {
          const seg = ((ang + Math.PI) / (Math.PI * 2)) * 26;
          const seam = Math.abs(seg - Math.round(seg)) < 0.09;
          key = seam ? 'rimA' : ((Math.floor(seg) & 1) ? 'rimB' : 'rimA');
        } else {
          if (def.id === 'desert') {
            const dune = Math.sin((x * 0.12 + y * 0.08) + n1 * 2.2);
            key = dune > 0.45 ? 'hi' : (dune < -0.4 ? 'deep' : 'mid');
            if (v > 0.74) key = 'foam';
          } else if (def.id === 'oasis') {
            // 沙地环抱中央浅碧绿洲水潭 + 岸边棕榈草缘
            const poolR = 46 + Math.sin(ang * 3 + n1 * 2.4) * 7.5;
            if (dist < poolR - 5) key = (v > 0.52) ? 'mid' : 'deep';
            else if (dist < poolR) key = 'foam';
            else if (dist < poolR + 9 && n2 > 0.42) key = 'speck';
            else key = (v > 0.58) ? 'hi' : 'rimB';
          } else if (def.id === 'sky') {
            const puff = Math.sin(x * 0.09) * Math.cos(y * 0.09) + v;
            key = puff > 0.85 ? 'foam' : (puff > 0.45 ? 'hi' : (puff > 0.15 ? 'mid' : 'deep'));
          } else if (def.id === 'thunderPeak') {
            const bolt = Math.abs(Math.sin(x * 0.08 + y * 0.06 + n1 * 4.2));
            if (bolt < 0.06 && dist < rInner - 10 && n2 > 0.58) key = bolt < 0.025 ? 'speck' : 'foam';
            else key = v > 0.66 ? 'hi' : (v < 0.36 ? 'deep' : 'mid');
          } else if (def.id === 'iceSea') {
            // 洁白冰面裂开深蓝冰缝
            const fissure = Math.abs(Math.sin(x * 0.065 - y * 0.045 + n1 * 3.2));
            if (fissure < 0.14 && dist < rInner - 4) key = fissure < 0.07 ? 'deep' : 'mid';
            else key = v > 0.56 ? 'foam' : (v > 0.34 ? 'speck' : 'hi');
          } else if (def.id === 'openSea') {
            const swell = Math.sin(y * 0.14 + Math.sin(x * 0.05) * 2.0 + n1 * 1.4);
            if (swell > 0.86) key = 'foam';
            else if (swell > 0.30) key = 'hi';
            else if (swell < -0.35) key = 'deep';
            else key = 'mid';
          } else if (def.id === 'stoneBay') {
            // 浅滩鹅卵石 + 岸边岩缝 + 漫过浅水
            const riverBand = Math.sin(x * 0.045) * 16 + dy;
            if (Math.abs(riverBand) < 26 + n1 * 12) key = (v > 0.62) ? 'foam' : (v > 0.35 ? 'mid' : 'deep');
            else key = (n2 > 0.68) ? 'speck' : (v > 0.46 ? 'hi' : 'rimB');
          } else if (def.id === 'canopy') {
            // 粗壮树干横枝 + 层叠叶冠
            const branch = Math.abs(Math.sin((dx * 0.032 - dy * 0.028) + n1 * 1.8));
            if (branch < 0.18 && dist < rInner - 6) key = v > 0.5 ? 'speck' : 'rimB';
            else key = v > 0.72 ? 'foam' : (v > 0.50 ? 'hi' : (v < 0.30 ? 'deep' : 'mid'));
          } else if (def.id === 'bamboo') {
            // 竹林地台 + 横向竹节纹 + 散落竹叶
            const stalk = ((Math.round(x + n1 * 3) % 14) === 0);
            const node = ((y % 18) === 0 && (x % 14) < 3);
            if (node) key = 'foam';
            else if (stalk) key = 'deep';
            else if (n2 > 0.80 && ((x + y) & 3) === 0) key = 'speck';
            else key = v > 0.64 ? 'hi' : (v < 0.34 ? 'deep' : 'mid');
          } else if (def.id === 'cave') {
            // 地下暗河贯穿深色溶洞岩台
            const stream = Math.abs(dx + Math.sin(dy * 0.06) * 18);
            if (stream < 18 + n1 * 8) key = (stream > 14 && v > 0.52) ? 'foam' : 'deep';
            else key = v > 0.66 ? 'hi' : (v < 0.32 ? 'speck' : 'mid');
          } else if (def.id === 'volcano') {
            const crack = Math.abs(Math.sin(x * 0.07 + n1 * 3.8) + Math.cos(y * 0.07 - n2 * 2.5));
            if (crack < 0.14 && dist < rInner - 6) key = crack < 0.06 ? 'foam' : 'speck';
            else key = v > 0.64 ? 'hi' : (v < 0.34 ? 'deep' : 'mid');
          } else if (def.id === 'forest') {
            if (n2 > 0.86 && ((x * 5 + y * 3) & 7) === 0) key = 'foam';
            else if (v > 0.66) key = 'hi';
            else if (v < 0.32) key = 'deep';
            else if (n2 > 0.78) key = 'speck';
            else key = 'mid';
          } else if (def.id === 'meadow') {
            if (n2 > 0.84 && ((x + y) & 3) === 0) key = ((x * 7 + y) & 1) ? 'foam' : 'speck';
            else key = v > 0.65 ? 'hi' : (v < 0.34 ? 'deep' : 'mid');
          } else if (def.id === 'ruins') {
            const tile = (x % 16 === 0 || y % 16 === 0);
            if (tile) key = 'speck';
            else if (v > 0.70) key = 'foam';
            else key = v > 0.55 ? 'hi' : (v < 0.35 ? 'deep' : 'mid');
          } else if (def.id === 'tideflat') {
            if (v > 0.56) key = v > 0.72 ? 'speck' : 'hi';
            else if (v > 0.48) key = 'foam';
            else key = v > 0.32 ? 'mid' : 'deep';
          } else if (def.id === 'carcass') {
            // 黑褐腐土打底 + 5 道白色肋骨拱（带暗边）+ 淡青菌斑
            key = v > 0.66 ? 'hi' : (v < 0.36 ? 'deep' : 'mid');
            for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) {
              const ribX = 42 + k * 29 + Math.sin(y * 0.036 + k * 0.9) * 9;
              const dRib = Math.abs(x - ribX);
              if (Math.abs(dy) < 70 && dRib < 2.7) { key = 'foam'; break; }
              if (Math.abs(dy) < 70 && dRib < 4.0) { key = 'rimA'; break; }
            }
            if (key !== 'foam' && key !== 'rimA' && n2 > 0.79 && ((x * 3 + y * 5) & 3) === 0) key = 'speck';
          } else if (def.id === 'saltlake') {
            // 白色六边形盐壳 + 浅灰翘边 + 粉红浓盐水裂隙与浅洼
            const S = 15, HY = S * 0.866;
            const j0 = Math.round(y / HY);
            let d1 = 1e9, d2 = 1e9, best = 0;
            for (let j = j0 - 1; j <= j0 + 1; j++) {
              const off = (j & 1) ? S * 0.5 : 0;
              const i0 = Math.round((x - off) / S);
              for (let i = i0 - 1; i <= i0 + 1; i++) {
                const dd = Math.hypot(x - (i * S + off), y - j * HY);
                if (dd < d1) { d2 = d1; d1 = dd; best = i * 31 + j * 17; }
                else if (dd < d2) d2 = dd;
              }
            }
            const pool = (((best * 2654435761) >>> 0) % 9) === 0;
            if (d2 - d1 < 1.35) key = (n2 > 0.54) ? 'speck' : 'foam';
            else if (pool) key = d1 < S * 0.38 ? 'deep' : 'speck';
            else key = (v > 0.62 || (n2 > 0.86 && ((x + y) & 3) === 0)) ? 'hi' : 'mid';
          } else if (def.id === 'hotspring') {
            // 石灰梯田台阶 + 白矿堰边 + 橙红 / 黄 / 绿三色热水菌毯
            const yy = y + Math.sin(x * 0.06 + n1 * 3.0) * 6.0;
            const band = Math.floor(yy / 26);
            const rel = ((yy % 26) + 26) % 26;
            const mats = ['foam', 'foam', 'speck', 'green', 'green', 'green', 'green', 'green'];
            if (rel < 2.8) key = 'hi';
            else if (rel < 4.4) key = 'rimB';
            else if (v > 0.64) key = 'deep';
            else key = mats[Math.max(0, Math.min(mats.length - 1, band))];
          } else if (def.isWater) {
            const caustic = Math.sin((x + y * 0.55) * 0.13) * Math.cos((y - x * 0.48) * 0.13);
            if (dist > rInner - 2.4 && v > 0.5) key = 'foam';
            else if (def.id === 'reef' && dist > rInner - 10 && n2 > 0.76) key = 'speck';
            else if (dist < 48 && (v > 0.38 || caustic > 0.48)) key = 'mid';
            else key = (v > 0.54 || caustic > 0.58) ? 'mid' : 'deep';
          } else {
            if (v > 0.68) key = 'hi';
            else if (v < 0.32) key = 'deep';
            else if (n2 > 0.78) key = 'speck';
            else key = 'mid';
            if (dist > rInner - 3.5 && v > 0.56) key = 'rimB';
          }
        }

        const c = rgba[key];
        const i = (y * size + x) * 4;
        bim.data[i] = c[0]; bim.data[i + 1] = c[1]; bim.data[i + 2] = c[2]; bim.data[i + 3] = 255;
      }
    }

    bg.putImageData(bim, 0, 0);
    const res = { id: def.id, def, size, base, rInner, rRim, rOuter };
    platformCache[def.id] = res;
    return res;
  }

  PW.Habitats = { HABITAT_DEFS, HABITAT_MAP, buildHabitatPlatform };
})(window.PixelWorkshop);


===== FILE: ./core/math.js =====
'use strict';

window.PixelWorkshop = window.PixelWorkshop || {};

(function (PW) {
  const TAU = Math.PI * 2;
  const clamp = (v, a, b) => Math.max(a, Math.min(b, v));
  const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + (b - a) * t;
  const smoothstep = (e0, e1, x) => {
    const t = clamp((x - e0) / (e1 - e0), 0, 1);
    return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
  };
  const smin = (a, b, k) => {
    const h = clamp(0.5 + 0.5 * (b - a) / k, 0, 1);
    return lerp(b, a, h) - k * h * (1 - h);
  };
  const expSmooth = (dt, speed) => 1 - Math.exp(-Math.max(0, dt) * speed);
  const wrapAngle = (a) => ((((a + Math.PI) % TAU) + TAU) % TAU) - Math.PI;
  const lerpAngle = (a, b, t) => a + wrapAngle(b - a) * t;

  // 局部坐标转世界坐标（正面 +Y，绕 Z 轴旋转 yaw）
  function localToWorldXY(rx, ry, yaw, lx, ly) {
    const cy = Math.cos(yaw), sy = Math.sin(yaw);
    return [rx + cy * lx - sy * ly, ry + sy * lx + cy * ly];
  }

  // 关节枢轴锁死摆放：若部件真实旋转轴在局部 (px, py)，返回体素盒中心的世界坐标
  function pivotToWorldXY(jointX, jointY, partYaw, pivotX, pivotY) {
    return localToWorldXY(jointX, jointY, partYaw, -pivotX, -pivotY);
  }

  // 绕 X 轴向前倾倒（pitch > 0 时 +Z 倒向 +Y）
  function pitchPt(pt, pitch, pivotY, pivotZ) {
    const cp = Math.cos(pitch), sp = Math.sin(pitch);
    const dy = pt[1] - pivotY, dz = pt[2] - pivotZ;
    return [pt[0], pivotY + dy * cp + dz * sp, pivotZ - dy * sp + dz * cp];
  }

  function distSeg3Sq(px, py, pz, ax, ay, az, bx, by, bz) {
    const abx = bx - ax, aby = by - ay, abz = bz - az;
    const apx = px - ax, apy = py - ay, apz = pz - az;
    const denom = abx * abx + aby * aby + abz * abz || 1;
    const t = clamp((apx * abx + apy * aby + apz * abz) / denom, 0, 1);
    const dx = apx - abx * t, dy = apy - aby * t, dz = apz - abz * t;
    return [dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz, t];
  }

  function distTri3Sq(px, py, pz, a, b, c) {
    const abx = b[0] - a[0], aby = b[1] - a[1], abz = b[2] - a[2];
    const acx = c[0] - a[0], acy = c[1] - a[1], acz = c[2] - a[2];
    const apx = px - a[0], apy = py - a[1], apz = pz - a[2];
    const d1 = abx * apx + aby * apy + abz * apz;
    const d2 = acx * apx + acy * apy + acz * apz;
    if (d1 <= 0 && d2 <= 0) return apx * apx + apy * apy + apz * apz;
    const bpx = px - b[0], bpy = py - b[1], bpz = pz - b[2];
    const d3 = abx * bpx + aby * bpy + abz * bpz;
    const d4 = acx * bpx + acy * bpy + acz * bpz;
    if (d3 >= 0 && d4 <= d3) return bpx * bpx + bpy * bpy + bpz * bpz;
    const vc = d1 * d4 - d3 * d2;
    if (vc <= 0 && d1 >= 0 && d3 <= 0) {
      const v = d1 / (d1 - d3);
      const qx = apx - abx * v, qy = apy - aby * v, qz = apz - abz * v;
      return qx * qx + qy * qy + qz * qz;
    }
    const cpx = px - c[0], cpy = py - c[1], cpz = pz - c[2];
    const d5 = abx * cpx + aby * cpy + abz * cpz;
    const d6 = acx * cpx + acy * cpy + acz * cpz;
    if (d6 >= 0 && d5 <= d6) return cpx * cpx + cpy * cpy + cpz * cpz;
    const vb = d5 * d2 - d1 * d6;
    if (vb <= 0 && d2 >= 0 && d6 <= 0) {
      const w = d2 / (d2 - d6);
      const qx = apx - acx * w, qy = apy - acy * w, qz = apz - acz * w;
      return qx * qx + qy * qy + qz * qz;
    }
    const va = d3 * d6 - d5 * d4;
    if (va <= 0 && (d4 - d3) >= 0 && (d5 - d6) >= 0) {
      const w = (d4 - d3) / ((d4 - d3) + (d5 - d6));
      const qx = bpx - (c[0] - b[0]) * w, qy = bpy - (c[1] - b[1]) * w, qz = bpz - (c[2] - b[2]) * w;
      return qx * qx + qy * qy + qz * qz;
    }
    const denom = 1 / (va + vb + vc || 1);
    const v = vb * denom, w = vc * denom;
    const qx = apx - abx * v - acx * w, qy = apy - aby * v - acy * w, qz = apz - abz * v - acz * w;
    return qx * qx + qy * qy + qz * qz;
  }

  function hash2i(ix, iy, seed) {
    let h = (ix * 374761393 + iy * 668265263 + seed * 1442695041) | 0;
    h = Math.imul(h ^ (h >>> 13), 1274126177);
    return ((h ^ (h >>> 16)) >>> 0) / 4294967295;
  }

  function periodicNoise2(x, y, periodX, periodY, cell, seed) {
    const gx = x / cell, gy = y / cell;
    const px = Math.max(1, Math.round(periodX / cell));
    const py = Math.max(1, Math.round(periodY / cell));
    const ix = Math.floor(gx), iy = Math.floor(gy);
    const fx = gx - ix, fy = gy - iy;
    const ux = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx), uy = fy * fy * (3 - 2 * fy);
    const wMod = (v, m) => ((v % m) + m) % m;
    const x0 = wMod(ix, px), x1 = wMod(ix + 1, px);
    const y0 = wMod(iy, py), y1 = wMod(iy + 1, py);
    return lerp(
      lerp(hash2i(x0, y0, seed), hash2i(x1, y0, seed), ux),
      lerp(hash2i(x0, y1, seed), hash2i(x1, y1, seed), ux),
      uy
    );
  }

  // 1D/2D 连续平滑值噪声（P5.js noise 风格，用于自然漂浮、微动与呼吸）
  function valueNoise1(x, seed = 11) {
    const ix = Math.floor(x);
    const fx = x - ix;
    const u = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx);
    return lerp(hash2i(ix, 0, seed), hash2i(ix + 1, 0, seed), u);
  }

  function fbm1(x, seed = 11, octaves = 3) {
    let sum = 0, amp = 0.5, freq = 1, norm = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
      sum += valueNoise1(x * freq, seed + i * 31) * amp;
      norm += amp;
      amp *= 0.5;
      freq *= 2.03;
    }
    return sum / (norm || 1);
  }

  function hexToRgba(hex) {
    const n = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16);
    return [(n >> 16) & 255, (n >> 8) & 255, n & 255, 255];
  }

  PW.Math = {
    TAU, clamp, lerp, smoothstep, smin, expSmooth, wrapAngle, lerpAngle,
    localToWorldXY, pivotToWorldXY, pitchPt, distSeg3Sq, distTri3Sq,
    hash2i, periodicNoise2, valueNoise1, fbm1, hexToRgba
  };
})(window.PixelWorkshop);


===== FILE: ./core/registry.js =====
'use strict';

window.PixelWorkshop = window.PixelWorkshop || {};

// =============================================================================
// 资产登记：工坊里所有能放上台的东西（怪物 / NPC / 建筑 / 场景摆件）都在这里登记。
//
// 每个资产脚本照旧调用：
//   PW.Creatures.push(Def)                 // 怪物（旧写法，继续可用）
//   PW.Assets.register(Def, 'npc')         // NPC / 建筑 / 场景摆件
//
// Def 里可以多写这几个字段（都可选）：
//   code:     '天1'          对上 文档/生物图鉴与逐只精制规划.md 和 app/catalog.js 里的编号；
//                            不写的话，id 用 sky1_xxx / water3_xxx 这种前缀也能自动对上
//   status:   'draft' | 'done'   初版 / 精制完成（不写当 draft）
//   actions:  [{ id, name, loop, duration, status }]
//             展示动作清单。默认是「自由巡游」「原地待机」两个循环动作；
//             以后加技能展示，就在这里加 { id: 'skill_xxx', name: '…', loop: false, duration: 2.4 }，
//             脚本的 update() 从 eco.action / eco.actionTime 读当前在放哪个动作、放了几秒。
// =============================================================================
(function (PW) {
  const CATEGORIES = [
    { id: 'creature', name: '怪物', dir: 'creatures/' },
    { id: 'npc', name: 'NPC', dir: 'npc/' },
    { id: 'building', name: '建筑', dir: 'buildings/' },
    { id: 'scene', name: '场景环境', dir: 'scenes/' }
  ];

  const DEFAULT_ACTIONS = [
    { id: 'patrol', name: '自由巡游', loop: true, status: 'draft' },
    { id: 'idle', name: '原地待机', loop: true, status: 'draft' }
  ];

  // id 前缀 → 清单编号的字头
  const CODE_PREFIX = { sky: '天', water: '水', sea: '海', earth: '地', tree: '树', sand: '沙', cave: '洞', marsh: '泽', special: '特' };

  const list = [];
  const byId = Object.create(null);

  function guessCode(id) {
    const m = /^([a-z]+)(\d+)_/.exec(id || '');
    if (m && CODE_PREFIX[m[1]]) return CODE_PREFIX[m[1]] + m[2];
    return null;
  }

  function register(def, category) {
    if (!def || !def.id) throw new Error('资产缺 id');
    if (byId[def.id]) return def;
    def.category = def.category || category || 'creature';
    if (!def.code) def.code = guessCode(def.id);
    if (!def.status) def.status = 'draft';
    if (!def._scriptSrc && typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.currentScript) {
      def._scriptSrc = document.currentScript.getAttribute('src') || '';
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(def.actions) || !def.actions.length) def.actions = DEFAULT_ACTIONS.map(a => ({ ...a }));
    list.push(def);
    byId[def.id] = def;
    return def;
  }

  // 生成一个实例。传 template（同一种资产的另一个实例）时共用它的部件：
  // 体素模型不再重建，烘焙缓存也共用（缓存挂在部件对象上），同屏放几十只同种怪也不涨内存。
  function spawn(def, opts, template) {
    const makeCanvas = PW.Renderer.makeCanvas;
    const inst = def.createInstance(makeCanvas, opts || {});
    if (template && template.parts && inst.parts) {
      for (const k of Object.keys(template.parts)) inst.parts[k] = template.parts[k];
      if (inst.partListAssets) inst.partListAssets = Object.values(inst.parts);
      inst.sharedWith = template;
    }
    return inst;
  }

  // 旧写法 PW.Creatures.push(Def) 也走登记
  const creatures = PW.Creatures || [];
  for (const d of creatures) register(d, 'creature');
  creatures.push = function (...defs) {
    for (const d of defs) register(d, 'creature');
    return Array.prototype.push.apply(this, defs);
  };
  PW.Creatures = creatures;

  PW.Assets = { CATEGORIES, DEFAULT_ACTIONS, CODE_PREFIX, list, byId, register, spawn, guessCode };
})(window.PixelWorkshop);


===== FILE: ./core/renderer.js =====
'use strict';

window.PixelWorkshop = window.PixelWorkshop || {};

(function (PW) {
  const { TAU, clamp, lerp, smoothstep, localToWorldXY, hexToRgba } = PW.Math;
  const { EnvFeedback } = PW.Behaviors;

  // ===========================================================================
  // 投影与烘焙常数（这几个决定美术风格，改了画风就变了，别轻动）
  // ===========================================================================
  const TILT = 0.6;          // 俯视压扁
  const HS = 0.8;            // 每升高 1 格，屏幕上抬 0.8 格
  const BAKE_SCALE = 2.5;    // 1 格体素 = 2.5 个烘焙像素（1 个烘焙像素 = 1 个「美术像素」）
  const BAKE_STEP = 2;       // 每层抬 2 个烘焙像素（= HS × BAKE_SCALE）
  const BAKE_HALF = 1;       // 双子步压实，消掉斜角百叶窗缝
  const GROUND_SUPERSAMPLE = 2;

  // ===========================================================================
  // 流畅度参数（2026-11-23 claude 优化，原因见 文档/性能与流畅度-2026-11-23.md）
  // ===========================================================================
  // 角度档位按部件大小自适应：换一档角度时，部件最远端最多挪 ANGLE_EDGE_PX 个烘焙像素。
  // 大部件档位细（不再一格一格跳），小部件档位粗（省缓存）。旧版是一律 3°。
  const ANGLE_EDGE_PX = 1.75;
  const ANGLE_MIN_DEG = 1.5;
  const ANGLE_MAX_DEG = 7.5;
  // 浸水改「染水色」（不透明），不再半透明叠出珠子斑；档位 0..3
  const SUBMERGE_LEVELS = 3;
  const SUBMERGE_MIX = 0.44;   // 完全浸没时往水色混 44%，跟旧版 alpha 0.56 的观感对齐

  // ===========================================================================
  // 性能参数
  // 实测（Chrome 内核，2560×1440）：一帧里引用的贴图总量超过 32MB 左右，GPU 耗时会暴涨 7~35 倍。
  // 所以图集总量封顶 28MB（工坊右栏可改），天空只画在 1/8 大小的小画布上再放大贴，不占整屏贴图。
  // ===========================================================================
  const ATLAS_PAGE = 512;       // 烘焙图集一页 512²（1MB）；页小，淘汰时丢掉的热数据少
  const ATLAS_BUDGET_MB = 28;   // 图集总量上限（超过 32MB 会掉到 GPU 的慢路径，留一点余量）
  const BAKE_BUDGET_MS = 2.5;   // 每帧烘焙预算，超出就先借相邻角度顶一帧
  const SCRATCH_POOL = 8;       // 烘焙临时画布轮换，避免同一帧里反复改同一张画布

  function makeCanvas(w, h) {
    const c = document.createElement('canvas');
    c.width = Math.max(1, w);
    c.height = Math.max(1, h);
    return c;
  }

  // 部件的几何信息（只算一次，挂在部件上）：
  //   u0..u1 / v0..v1 / zMin..zMax：真正有体素的范围（体素盒常留了很多空边，烘焙时按这个裁紧）
  //   sym：左右对称（x ↔ w-1-x 完全一样）→ 只烘 0~180°，另一半水平翻转着画
  //   R：转动半径（烘焙像素），决定角度档位多细
  function assetInfo(asset) {
    if (asset._pw) return asset._pw;
    const { w, d, h, grid } = asset;
    let x0 = w, x1 = -1, y0 = d, y1 = -1, z0 = h, z1 = -1;
    let sym = true;
    for (let z = 0; z < h; z++) {
      for (let y = 0; y < d; y++) {
        const row = (z * d + y) * w;
        for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
          const id = grid[row + x];
          if (sym && x < (w >> 1) && id !== grid[row + w - 1 - x]) sym = false;
          if (!id) continue;
          if (x < x0) x0 = x; if (x > x1) x1 = x;
          if (y < y0) y0 = y; if (y > y1) y1 = y;
          if (z < z0) z0 = z; if (z > z1) z1 = z;
        }
      }
    }
    if (x1 < 0) { x0 = 0; x1 = 0; y0 = 0; y1 = 0; z0 = 0; z1 = 0; }
    const u0 = x0 - w * 0.5, u1 = x1 + 1 - w * 0.5, v0 = y0 - d * 0.5, v1 = y1 + 1 - d * 0.5;
    const R = Math.max(4, Math.hypot(Math.max(-u0, u1), Math.max(-v0, v1)) * BAKE_SCALE);
    asset._pw = { u0, u1, v0, v1, zMin: z0, zMax: z1, sym, R };
    return asset._pw;
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // 通用 VFX 容器（涟漪、V 形尾迹、3D 像素粒子、接地扬尘）
  // ===========================================================================
  function compactInPlace(arr, keep) {
    let w = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
      const it = arr[i];
      if (keep(it)) arr[w++] = it;
    }
    arr.length = w;
  }

  class VFXPool {
    constructor() {
      this.particles = [];
      this.ripples = [];
      this.wakes = [];
      this.maxParticles = 600;

      // 地表痕迹层（环形缓冲，数量封顶，直接绘入 drawGround 的 gbuf，不另开大画布）
      this.maxDecals = 220;
      this.decals = new Array(this.maxDecals);
      this.decalHead = 0;
      this.decalCount = 0;

      // 预留「留成摆件」环形缓冲（供 火1 冷石板 / 洞5 小蘑菇 / 树3 嫩芽复用）
      this.maxProps = 48;
      this.props = new Array(this.maxProps);
      this.propHead = 0;
      this.propCount = 0;
    }

    clear() {
      this.particles.length = 0;
      this.ripples.length = 0;
      this.wakes.length = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < this.maxDecals; i++) this.decals[i] = null;
      this.decalHead = 0;
      this.decalCount = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < this.maxProps; i++) this.props[i] = null;
      this.propHead = 0;
      this.propCount = 0;
    }

    // 新增贴地痕迹：kind 支持 'mound'(沙垄/土隆) | 'wet'(水沟/湿痕) | 'crack'(焦痕/裂纹)
    // 可选 propOnExpire: { kind, radius, life, color, rimColor } 在痕迹结束时自动留成摆件
    addDecal(opts) {
      if (!opts) return null;
      const idx = this.decalHead;
      let d = this.decals[idx];
      if (!d) {
        d = {};
        this.decals[idx] = d;
      }
      d.active = true;
      d.kind = opts.kind || 'mound';
      d.x = opts.x || 0;
      d.y = opts.y || 0;
      d.yaw = opts.yaw || 0;
      d.rx = opts.rx ?? opts.radius ?? 4.6;
      d.ry = opts.ry ?? opts.radius ?? 5.6;
      d.t = 0;
      d.life = Math.max(0.2, opts.life ?? 7.5);
      d.alpha = opts.alpha ?? 0.88;
      d.color = opts.color || '';
      d.hiColor = opts.hiColor || '';
      d.darkColor = opts.darkColor || '';
      d.seed = opts.seed ?? ((Math.abs(Math.round(d.x * 13 + d.y * 29)) % 97) + 1);
      d.propOnExpire = opts.propOnExpire || null;

      this.decalHead = (idx + 1) % this.maxDecals;
      if (this.decalCount < this.maxDecals) this.decalCount++;
      return d;
    }

    // 预留「留成摆件」接口：在地面留下一件低矮贴地摆件（石板、蘑菇、嫩芽等）
    addGroundProp(opts) {
      if (!opts) return null;
      const idx = this.propHead;
      let p = this.props[idx];
      if (!p) {
        p = {};
        this.props[idx] = p;
      }
      p.active = true;
      p.kind = opts.kind || 'slab';
      p.x = opts.x || 0;
      p.y = opts.y || 0;
      p.yaw = opts.yaw || 0;
      p.radius = opts.radius ?? 5.2;
      p.t = 0;
      p.life = opts.life ?? Infinity; // Infinity 表示常驻直到环形缓冲覆盖或清台
      p.color = opts.color || '';
      p.rimColor = opts.rimColor || '';
      p.seed = opts.seed ?? ((Math.abs(Math.round(p.x * 17 + p.y * 31)) % 89) + 1);

      this.propHead = (idx + 1) % this.maxProps;
      if (this.propCount < this.maxProps) this.propCount++;
      return p;
    }

    spawnParticle(x, y, z, vx, vy, vz, life, sz, color, glow = false, gravity = 0) {
      if (this.particles.length >= this.maxParticles) return;
      this.particles.push({ x, y, z, vx, vy, vz, t: 0, life, sz, color, glow, gravity });
    }

    addRipple(x, y, r0, r1, life, width = 1.8) {
      if (this.ripples.length > 80) this.ripples.shift();
      this.ripples.push({ x, y, r0, r1, t: 0, life, width });
    }

    addWake(x, y, yaw, width, life = 1.05) {
      if (this.wakes.length > 96) this.wakes.shift();
      this.wakes.push({ x, y, yaw, width, t: 0, life });
    }

    spawnSplash(wx, wy, count, power, fx) {
      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const a = Math.random() * TAU;
        const sp = (8 + Math.random() * 16) * power;
        this.spawnParticle(
          wx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 3, wy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 3, 1.2,
          Math.cos(a) * sp, Math.sin(a) * sp, (9 + Math.random() * 14) * power,
          0.35 + Math.random() * 0.22, 1.4 + Math.random() * 0.8,
          `rgb(${i % 2 === 0 ? fx.foamRGB : fx.ringRGB})`, i % 3 === 0, 48
        );
      }
    }

    spawnDust(wx, wy, count, power, fx) {
      for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
        const a = Math.random() * TAU;
        const sp = (6 + Math.random() * 14) * power;
        this.spawnParticle(
          wx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, wy + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, 0.8,
          Math.cos(a) * sp, Math.sin(a) * sp, (6 + Math.random() * 10) * power,
          0.40 + Math.random() * 0.25, 1.8 + Math.random() * 1.1,
          `rgb(${fx.dustRGB})`, false, 24
        );
      }
    }

    update(dt) {
      const ps = this.particles;
      for (let i = 0; i < ps.length; i++) {
        const p = ps[i];
        p.t += dt;
        p.vz -= p.gravity * dt;
        p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt; p.z += p.vz * dt;
        if (p.z < 0.4) { p.z = 0.4; p.vz = Math.abs(p.vz) * 0.25; p.vx *= 0.6; p.vy *= 0.6; }
      }
      compactInPlace(ps, p => p.t < p.life);
      for (const r of this.ripples) r.t += dt;
      compactInPlace(this.ripples, r => r.t < r.life);
      for (const w of this.wakes) w.t += dt;
      compactInPlace(this.wakes, w => w.t < w.life);

      if (this.decalCount > 0) {
        let alive = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < this.maxDecals; i++) {
          const d = this.decals[i];
          if (!d || !d.active) continue;
          d.t += dt;
          if (d.t >= d.life) {
            d.active = false;
            if (d.propOnExpire) {
              const po = d.propOnExpire;
              this.addGroundProp({
                x: d.x, y: d.y, yaw: d.yaw,
                kind: po.kind || 'slab',
                radius: po.radius ?? Math.max(d.rx, d.ry),
                life: po.life ?? Infinity,
                color: po.color || d.color,
                rimColor: po.rimColor || d.hiColor,
                seed: d.seed
              });
              d.propOnExpire = null;
            }
          } else {
            alive++;
          }
        }
        this.decalCount = alive;
      }

      if (this.propCount > 0) {
        let aliveP = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < this.maxProps; i++) {
          const p = this.props[i];
          if (!p || !p.active) continue;
          if (Number.isFinite(p.life)) {
            p.t += dt;
            if (p.t >= p.life) { p.active = false; continue; }
          }
          aliveP++;
        }
        this.propCount = aliveP;
      }
    }
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // 烘焙图集：几十张 512² 小图集页代替几千张小画布，总量封顶，按页做 LRU 淘汰
  // ===========================================================================
  class AtlasPage {
    constructor(size, id) {
      this.size = size;
      this.id = id;
      this.canvas = makeCanvas(size, size);
      this.ctx = this.canvas.getContext('2d');
      this.gen = 0;
      this.reset();
    }

    reset() {
      this.gen++;
      this.shelves = [];
      this.entries = [];
      this.nextY = 0;
      this.lastUse = -1;
      this.usedPx = 0;
      const ctx = this.ctx;
      ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, this.size, this.size);
    }

    alloc(w, h) {
      const size = this.size;
      if (w > size || h > size) return null;
      let best = null;
      for (let i = 0; i < this.shelves.length; i++) {
        const s = this.shelves[i];
        if (s.h >= h && s.h <= h * 1.3 + 4 && s.x + w <= size && (!best || s.h < best.h)) best = s;
      }
      if (!best) {
        if (this.nextY + h > size) return null;
        best = { y: this.nextY, h, x: 0 };
        this.shelves.push(best);
        this.nextY += h;
      }
      const x = best.x;
      best.x += w;
      this.usedPx += w * h;
      return { x, y: best.y };
    }
  }

  // 图集总量封顶。满了就挑一页清掉：优先挑最久没用的；
  // 清之前把这一两帧还在用的贴图记下来，清完马上重烘回这一页（贴图对象原地更新，正在用它的部件不受影响），
  // 等于顺手把冷数据扔掉、热数据挤紧。
  class SpriteAtlas {
    constructor(pageSize = ATLAS_PAGE, budgetMB = ATLAS_BUDGET_MB) {
      this.pageSize = pageSize;
      this.budgetBytes = budgetMB * 1048576;
      this.pages = [];
      this.frame = 0;
      this.evictions = 0;
      this.rescued = 0;        // 淘汰时顺手重烘保住的热贴图数
      this.lost = 0;           // 实在保不住、少画一帧的贴图数（单帧用量都超预算时才会有）
      this.repaint = null;     // (entry, page) => bool，由渲染器提供
    }

    setBudgetMB(mb) {
      this.budgetBytes = Math.max(4, mb) * 1048576;
      while (this.bytes() > this.budgetBytes && this.pages.length > 1) {
        this._dropPage(this._pickVictim(0) || this.pages[0]);
      }
    }

    bytes() { let b = 0; for (const p of this.pages) b += p.size * p.size * 4; return b; }

    _hotCount(p) {
      let n = 0;
      const f = this.frame - 1;
      for (const e of p.entries) if (e.lastUse >= f) n++;
      return n;
    }

    // 挑要清的页：先找这两帧没用过的最久的页；都用过就挑热贴图最少的
    _pickVictim(sizeOnly) {
      let best = null;
      const f = this.frame - 1;
      for (const p of this.pages) {
        if (sizeOnly && p.size !== sizeOnly) continue;
        if (p.lastUse < f && (!best || p.lastUse < best.lastUse)) best = p;
      }
      if (best) return best;
      let bestHot = Infinity;
      for (const p of this.pages) {
        if (sizeOnly && p.size !== sizeOnly) continue;
        const h = this._hotCount(p);
        if (h < bestHot) { bestHot = h; best = p; }
      }
      return best;
    }

    _evictInPlace(p) {
      const f = this.frame - 1;
      const keep = p.entries.filter(e => e.lastUse >= f);
      keep.sort((a, b) => b.bh - a.bh);
      p.reset();
      this.evictions++;
      for (const e of keep) {
        if (this.repaint && this.repaint(e, p)) this.rescued++;
        else { e.gen = -1; this.lost++; }
      }
    }

    _dropPage(p) {
      const i = this.pages.indexOf(p);
      if (i < 0) return;
      for (const e of p.entries) if (e.lastUse >= this.frame - 1) { e.gen = -1; this.lost++; }
      p.reset();
      p.canvas.width = 1; p.canvas.height = 1;
      this.pages.splice(i, 1);
      this.evictions++;
    }

    alloc(w, h) {
      for (let i = this.pages.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const r = this.pages[i].alloc(w, h);
        if (r) { r.page = this.pages[i]; return r; }
      }
      const need = Math.max(w, h);
      const size = need > this.pageSize ? Math.pow(2, Math.ceil(Math.log2(need))) : this.pageSize;
      const pageBytes = size * size * 4;

      if (size === this.pageSize && this.bytes() + pageBytes > this.budgetBytes) {
        for (let tries = 0; tries < 4; tries++) {
          const victim = this._pickVictim(size);
          if (!victim) break;
          this._evictInPlace(victim);
          const r = victim.alloc(w, h);
          if (r) { r.page = victim; return r; }
        }
      }
      // 超大部件（以后的 Boss、建筑）用单独的大页，腾够预算再开
      while (this.pages.length && this.bytes() + pageBytes > this.budgetBytes) {
        const victim = this._pickVictim(0);
        if (!victim) break;
        this._dropPage(victim);
      }
      const p = new AtlasPage(size, this.pages.length);
      this.pages.push(p);
      const r = p.alloc(w, h);
      if (r) r.page = p;
      return r;
    }

    clear() {
      for (const p of this.pages) {
        for (const e of p.entries) e.gen = -1;
        p.reset();
        p.canvas.width = 1; p.canvas.height = 1;
      }
      this.pages.length = 0;
    }
  }

  // ===========================================================================
  // 核心像素堆叠渲染器
  //
  // cached（游戏缓存，默认）：
  //   1) 每个部件按「角度档位 × 浸水档」烘进图集，一次烘出三格：本体 / 描边环 / 自亮；
  //   2) 每只生物：先画所有部件的描边环，再按深度画部件本体，直接画到屏幕上。
  //      各部件外扩一圈的并集 = 整只外扩一圈，所以效果跟原来 actorBuf 整体描边完全一样，
  //      但不用每只怪一张离屏画布，部件位置也精确到设备像素（不再 0.4 格一跳）；
  //   3) 屏幕缩放只取「1 美术像素 = 整数个设备像素」，像素宽窄永远一致。
  // crisp / retro：旧版的逐层实时画法，留着对照，不为性能优化。
  // ===========================================================================
  class StackRenderer {
    constructor(canvas) {
      this.cv = canvas;
      this.dctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      this.vfx = new VFXPool();

      this.mc = makeCanvas(1, 1);  this.mctx = this.mc.getContext('2d');
      this.oc = makeCanvas(1, 1);  this.octx = this.oc.getContext('2d');
      this.ec = makeCanvas(1, 1);  this.ectx = this.ec.getContext('2d');
      this.eb = makeCanvas(1, 1);  this.ebctx = this.eb.getContext('2d');
      this.gbuf = makeCanvas(200 * GROUND_SUPERSAMPLE, 200 * GROUND_SUPERSAMPLE);
      this.gbctx = this.gbuf.getContext('2d');

      this.atlas = new SpriteAtlas();
      this.atlas.repaint = (e, page) => this._repaint(e, page);
      this.assetCache = new WeakMap();
      this.scratch = [];
      this.scratchIdx = 0;
      this.frame = 0;

      this.tintEpoch = 0;
      this.tintKey = '';
      this.tintStyles = ['', '', '', ''];

      this.stats = {
        hits: 0, misses: 0, borrowed: 0,
        frameBakes: 0, frameBakeMs: 0, frameDraws: 0, frameParts: 0
      };
      // 兼容旧字段
      this.cacheHits = 0;
      this.cacheMisses = 0;

      this.MODES = ['cached', 'crisp', 'retro'];
      this.MODE_LABELS = { cached: '游戏缓存', crisp: '高清逐层（对照）', retro: '复古低分（对照）' };
      this.modeIdx = 0;

      this.DPR = 1;
      this.artPx = 4;            // 1 个美术像素 = artPx 个设备像素（整数）
      this.SCALE = this.artPx * BAKE_SCALE;
      this.CW = 1; this.CH = 1;
      this.EW = 1; this.EH = 1;
      this.zoom = 0;
      this.th = 0.56;
      this.useOutline = true;
      this.lastHabitatId = null;
      this.bakeBudgetMs = BAKE_BUDGET_MS;

      this._glowOn = false;
      this._ecDirty = false;
      this._g = [0, 0, 0, 0];
      this._sorted = [];
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 画布尺寸
    // cssW / cssH：画布在页面上的 CSS 尺寸；不传就量画布自己的布局尺寸
    fit(habitatDef, cssW, cssH, dpr) {
      this._lastCssW = cssW; this._lastCssH = cssH; this._lastDpr = dpr;
      this.DPR = dpr || Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1);
      const w = cssW || this.cv.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
      const h = cssH || this.cv.clientHeight || window.innerHeight;
      this.cv.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(w * this.DPR));
      this.cv.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(h * this.DPR));

      // 缩放档位按「美术像素 = 整数个设备像素」取，像素宽窄永远一致
      const baseArt = Math.max(1, Math.round(Math.max(2, h / 148) * this.DPR / BAKE_SCALE));
      this.artPx = clamp(baseArt + this.zoom, 1, 16);
      this.SCALE = this.artPx * BAKE_SCALE;

      const mode = this.getRenderMode();
      if (mode === 'retro') {
        this.CW = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(this.cv.width / this.SCALE));
        this.CH = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(this.cv.height / this.SCALE));
      } else {
        this.CW = this.cv.width;
        this.CH = this.cv.height;
      }
      if (mode === 'cached') {
        this.mc.width = this.oc.width = 1;
        this.mc.height = this.oc.height = 1;
      } else {
        this.mc.width = this.oc.width = this.CW;
        this.mc.height = this.oc.height = this.CH;
      }
      this.EW = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(this.cv.width / 4));
      this.EH = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(this.cv.height / 4));
      this.ec.width = this.eb.width = this.EW;
      this.ec.height = this.eb.height = this.EH;
      this._ecDirty = false;
      if (habitatDef) this.renderSky(habitatDef);
    }

    // 天空：画在 1/8 分辨率的小图上，每帧平滑放大贴一次（渐变放大看不出差别；
    // 小图只有零点几 MB，不占图集那 32MB 的贴图预算，也比每帧整屏算径向渐变便宜）
    renderSky(habitatDef) {
      const { cv } = this;
      if (!this.skyC) { this.skyC = makeCanvas(1, 1); this.skyCtx = this.skyC.getContext('2d'); }
      const w = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(cv.width / 8)), h = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(cv.height / 8));
      this.skyC.width = w; this.skyC.height = h;
      const g = this.skyCtx;
      const sky = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h);
      sky.addColorStop(0, habitatDef.skyTop);
      sky.addColorStop(0.55, habitatDef.skyMid);
      sky.addColorStop(1, habitatDef.skyBot);
      g.fillStyle = sky;
      g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
      const halo = g.createRadialGradient(w * 0.5, h * 0.44, 2, w * 0.5, h * 0.44, h * 0.56);
      halo.addColorStop(0, habitatDef.skyHalo);
      halo.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)');
      g.fillStyle = halo;
      g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
      this.lastHabitatId = habitatDef.id;
    }

    drawSky() {
      const { dctx, cv } = this;
      if (!this.skyC) { dctx.fillStyle = '#123'; dctx.fillRect(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height); return; }
      dctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
      dctx.drawImage(this.skyC, 0, 0, cv.width, cv.height);
      dctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
    }
