    getRenderMode() { return this.MODES[this.modeIdx] || 'cached'; }
    getRenderModeLabel() { return this.MODE_LABELS[this.getRenderMode()] || '游戏缓存'; }
    setRenderMode(modeOrIdx, habitatDef) {
      if (typeof modeOrIdx === 'string') {
        const idx = this.MODES.indexOf(modeOrIdx);
        if (idx >= 0) this.modeIdx = idx;
      } else if (typeof modeOrIdx === 'number') {
        this.modeIdx = ((modeOrIdx % this.MODES.length) + this.MODES.length) % this.MODES.length;
      }
      this.fit(habitatDef, this._lastCssW, this._lastCssH, this._lastDpr);
      return this.getRenderMode();
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 缓存管理
    // 换色时 recolorAsset 会把 asset.rev +1，旧缓存自动失效，这个函数留着兼容旧调用
    flushCacheByPrefix() {}

    clearCache() {
      this.atlas.clear();
      this.assetCache = new WeakMap();
    }

    cacheInfo() {
      return {
        pages: this.atlas.pages.length,
        mb: Math.round(this.atlas.bytes() / 1048576),
        budgetMB: Math.round(this.atlas.budgetBytes / 1048576),
        evictions: this.atlas.evictions,
        rescued: this.atlas.rescued,
        lost: this.atlas.lost
      };
    }

    // 这个生境的水色（浸水染色用），换生境时让染过色的缓存失效
    updateTint(hab) {
      const key = hab.isWater ? (hab.pal && hab.pal.mid) || '#1e80a6' : '';
      if (key === this.tintKey) return;
      this.tintKey = key;
      this.tintEpoch++;
      if (!key) return;
      const [r, g, b] = hexToRgba(key);
      for (let lv = 1; lv <= SUBMERGE_LEVELS; lv++) {
        this.tintStyles[lv] = `rgba(${r}, ${g}, ${b}, ${(lv / SUBMERGE_LEVELS) * SUBMERGE_MIX})`;
      }
    }

    slotCount(asset, symDeg) {
      const R = assetInfo(asset).R;
      const stepDeg = clamp((ANGLE_EDGE_PX / R) * (180 / Math.PI), ANGLE_MIN_DEG, ANGLE_MAX_DEG);
      return Math.max(4, Math.ceil(symDeg / stepDeg));
    }

    _scratch(w, h) {
      const i = this.scratchIdx++ % SCRATCH_POOL;
      let c = this.scratch[i];
      if (!c) c = this.scratch[i] = makeCanvas(w, h);
      if (c.width < w) c.width = w;
      if (c.height < h) c.height = h;
      return c;
    }

    _valid(e, asset, tint, outlineHex) {
      return e && e.page.gen === e.gen && e.rev === (asset.rev | 0)
        && (tint === 0 || e.tintEpoch === this.tintEpoch) && e.outline === outlineHex;
    }

    // 取一个部件在某个角度的烘焙贴图（没有就烘；本帧烘焙超预算时先借相邻角度）。
    // 左右对称的部件只烘 0~180°，另一半返回时 this._mirror = true，画的时候水平翻转。
    getSprite(asset, angle, symDeg, tint, outlineHex) {
      let map = this.assetCache.get(asset);
      if (!map) { map = new Map(); this.assetCache.set(asset, map); }
      const slots = this.slotCount(asset, symDeg);
      const step = symDeg / slots;
      let deg = (angle * 180 / Math.PI) % symDeg;
      if (deg < 0) deg += symDeg;
      let slot = Math.round(deg / step) % slots;
      this._mirror = false;
      if (symDeg === 360 && slot > (slots >> 1) && assetInfo(asset).sym) {
        slot = slots - slot;
        this._mirror = true;
      }
      const base = symDeg << 16;
      const key = base | (slot << 2) | tint;

      let e = map.get(key);
      if (this._valid(e, asset, tint, outlineHex)) {
        this.stats.hits++;
        e.lastUse = this.frame;
        e.page.lastUse = this.frame;
        return e;
      }

      if (this.stats.frameBakeMs > this.bakeBudgetMs) {
        for (let dSlot = 1; dSlot <= 8; dSlot++) {
          for (const sgn of [1, -1]) {
            const s2 = (slot + sgn * dSlot + slots) % slots;
            const e2 = map.get(base | (s2 << 2) | tint);
            if (this._valid(e2, asset, tint, outlineHex)) {
              this.stats.borrowed++;
              e2.lastUse = this.frame;
              e2.page.lastUse = this.frame;
              return e2;
            }
          }
        }
      }

      const t0 = performance.now();
      e = this.bake(asset, slot * step * Math.PI / 180, tint, outlineHex);
      this.stats.frameBakeMs += performance.now() - t0;
      this.stats.frameBakes++;
      this.stats.misses++;
      map.set(key, e);
      return e;
    }

    // 某个部件在某个角度下的烘焙尺寸和锚点（按真正有体素的范围算，不按体素盒外框，省掉大片空白）
    _layout(asset, qAng) {
      const c = Math.cos(qAng), s = Math.sin(qAng);
      const sc = BAKE_SCALE;
      const pad = 2;
      const info = assetInfo(asset);
      let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
      for (let ci = 0; ci < 4; ci++) {
        const u = (ci & 1) ? info.u1 : info.u0;
        const v = (ci & 2) ? info.v1 : info.v0;
        const X = sc * (c * u - s * v);
        const Y = sc * TILT * (s * u + c * v);
        if (X < minX) minX = X; if (X > maxX) maxX = X;
        if (Y < minY) minY = Y; if (Y > maxY) maxY = Y;
      }
      const top = minY - info.zMax * BAKE_STEP;
      const bottom = maxY - info.zMin * BAKE_STEP + BAKE_HALF;
      const fx0 = Math.floor(minX), fy0 = Math.floor(top);
      return {
        bw: Math.ceil(maxX) - fx0 + pad * 2,
        bh: Math.ceil(bottom) - fy0 + pad * 2,
        ax: pad - fx0,
        ay: pad - fy0,
        cells: (asset.glowStrip && asset.glowLayers.length) ? 3 : 2
      };
    }

    bake(asset, qAng, tint, outlineHex) {
      const L = this._layout(asset, qAng);
      const r = this.atlas.alloc(L.cells * (L.bw + 1), L.bh + 1);
      const e = {
        asset, qAng, tint, outline: outlineHex,
        page: r.page, gen: r.page.gen, x: r.x, y: r.y, rx: 0, gx: -1,
        bw: L.bw, bh: L.bh, ax: L.ax, ay: L.ay, cells: L.cells,
        rev: asset.rev | 0, tintEpoch: this.tintEpoch, lastUse: this.frame
      };
      this._place(e, r);
      this._paint(e);
      return e;
    }

    // 图集淘汰一页时，把还在用的贴图重烘回这一页（对象原地更新）
    _repaint(e, page) {
      const r = page.alloc(e.cells * (e.bw + 1), e.bh + 1);
      if (!r) return false;
      r.page = page;
      this._place(e, r);
      this._paint(e);
      return true;
    }

    _place(e, r) {
      e.page = r.page;
      e.gen = r.page.gen;
      e.x = r.x; e.y = r.y;
      e.rx = r.x + e.bw + 1;
      e.gx = e.cells === 3 ? r.x + 2 * (e.bw + 1) : -1;
      r.page.lastUse = Math.max(r.page.lastUse, e.lastUse);
      r.page.entries.push(e);
    }

    // 往图集里画一格：本体 / 描边剪影 / 自亮
    _paint(e) {
      const asset = e.asset;
      const { w, d, activeLayers, glowLayers, glowSlot, glowStrip, strip } = asset;
      const c = Math.cos(e.qAng), s = Math.sin(e.qAng);
      const sc = BAKE_SCALE;
      const { bw, bh, ax: anchorX, ay: anchorY } = e;
      const pctx = e.page.ctx;
      const mx = e.x, my = e.y, ringX = e.rx, glowX = e.gx;
      const cs = c * sc, ss = s * sc, tss = TILT * ss, tcs = TILT * cs;
      const hw = -w * 0.5, hd = -d * 0.5;

      // 1. 本体切片画进临时画布
      const scr = this._scratch(bw, bh);
      const sx = scr.getContext('2d');
      sx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      sx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      sx.globalAlpha = 1;
      sx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
      sx.clearRect(0, 0, bw, bh);
      for (let li = 0; li < activeLayers.length; li++) {
        const z = activeLayers[li];
        const yy = anchorY - z * BAKE_STEP;
        const sxo = z * w;
        sx.setTransform(cs, tss, -ss, tcs, anchorX, yy + BAKE_HALF);
        sx.drawImage(strip, sxo, 0, w, d, hw, hd, w, d);
        sx.setTransform(cs, tss, -ss, tcs, anchorX, yy);
        sx.drawImage(strip, sxo, 0, w, d, hw, hd, w, d);
      }
      sx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      if (e.tint > 0 && this.tintStyles[e.tint]) {
        // 只往已有像素上混水色，alpha 保持 255（守「切片 alpha 只能 0/255」）
        sx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-atop';
        sx.fillStyle = this.tintStyles[e.tint];
        sx.fillRect(0, 0, bw, bh);
        sx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      }

      // 2. 本体格
      pctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      pctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      pctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      pctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
      pctx.drawImage(scr, 0, 0, bw, bh, mx, my, bw, bh);

      // 3. 描边格：四向外扩 1 个烘焙像素的实心剪影，填描边色。
      //    画的时候先铺所有部件的剪影、再画本体，露在外面的一圈就是整只的外轮廓；
      //    半透明部件（果冻壳）底下也垫着描边色，跟旧版 actorBuf 的观感一致。
      pctx.save();
      pctx.beginPath();
      pctx.rect(ringX, my, bw, bh);
      pctx.clip();
      pctx.drawImage(scr, 0, 0, bw, bh, ringX - 1, my, bw, bh);
      pctx.drawImage(scr, 0, 0, bw, bh, ringX + 1, my, bw, bh);
      pctx.drawImage(scr, 0, 0, bw, bh, ringX, my - 1, bw, bh);
      pctx.drawImage(scr, 0, 0, bw, bh, ringX, my + 1, bw, bh);
      pctx.drawImage(scr, 0, 0, bw, bh, ringX, my, bw, bh);
      pctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-in';
      pctx.fillStyle = e.outline;
      pctx.fillRect(ringX, my, bw, bh);
      pctx.restore();

      // 4. 自亮格（被上层本体挡住的部分挖掉）
      if (glowX >= 0) {
        const minGlowZ = glowLayers[0];
        pctx.save();
        pctx.beginPath();
        pctx.rect(glowX, my, bw, bh);
        pctx.clip();
        for (let li = 0; li < activeLayers.length; li++) {
          const z = activeLayers[li];
          if (z < minGlowZ) continue;
          const yy = my + anchorY - z * BAKE_STEP;
          const ax = glowX + anchorX;
          if (z > minGlowZ) {
            pctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
            pctx.setTransform(cs, tss, -ss, tcs, ax, yy + BAKE_HALF);
            pctx.drawImage(strip, z * w, 0, w, d, hw, hd, w, d);
            pctx.setTransform(cs, tss, -ss, tcs, ax, yy);
            pctx.drawImage(strip, z * w, 0, w, d, hw, hd, w, d);
          }
          const gSlot = glowSlot[z];
          if (gSlot >= 0) {
            pctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
            pctx.setTransform(cs, tss, -ss, tcs, ax, yy);
            pctx.drawImage(glowStrip, gSlot * w, 0, w, d, hw, hd, w, d);
          }
        }
        pctx.restore();
      }
      pctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      pctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 投影
    proj(x, y, z) {
      const c = Math.cos(this.th), s = Math.sin(this.th);
      const rx = c * x - s * y, ry = s * x + c * y;
      const sc = this.SCALE;
      return [this.cv.width / 2 + rx * sc, this.cv.height / 2 + 16 * sc + ry * TILT * sc - z * HS * sc, ry];
    }

    projBuf(x, y, z, isRetro) {
      const c = Math.cos(this.th), s = Math.sin(this.th);
      const rx = c * x - s * y, ry = s * x + c * y;
      const sc = isRetro ? 1 : this.SCALE;
      const step = isRetro ? HS : Math.max(1, Math.round(HS * sc));
      return [this.CW / 2 + rx * sc, this.CH / 2 + 16 * sc + ry * TILT * sc - z * step, ry];
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 像素对齐画线
    _projectLinePoints(p, c, s, W2, H2, SC, grid) {
      const sag = p.sag || 0;
      const jit = p.jitter || 0;
      const jPhase = p.jitterPhase || 0;
      const segs = (sag === 0 && jit === 0) ? 1 : clamp(Math.round(p.segments || 10), 2, 24);
      const pts = new Array((segs + 1) * 2);
      for (let i = 0; i <= segs; i++) {
        const u = i / segs;
        const wx = lerp(p.x0, p.x1, u);
        const wy = lerp(p.y0, p.y1, u);
        const sagZ = sag ? (4 * u * (1 - u) * sag) : 0;
        const wz = lerp(p.z0, p.z1, u) - sagZ;
        const jitOff = jit ? (jit * Math.sin(u * Math.PI) * Math.sin(u * Math.PI * 3 + jPhase) * SC) : 0;
        const X = W2 + (c * wx - s * wy) * SC + jitOff;
        const Y = H2 + (s * wx + c * wy) * TILT * SC - wz * HS * SC;
        pts[i * 2] = Math.round(X / grid);
        pts[i * 2 + 1] = Math.round(Y / grid);
      }
      return pts;
    }

    _drawPixelLine(ctx, pts, grid, thick, color) {
      if (!pts || pts.length < 4) return;
      ctx.fillStyle = color;
      const halfOff = (thick - grid) >> 1;
      for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 2; i += 2) {
        let x0 = pts[i], y0 = pts[i + 1];
        const x1 = pts[i + 2], y1 = pts[i + 3];
        if (!Number.isFinite(x0) || !Number.isFinite(y0) || !Number.isFinite(x1) || !Number.isFinite(y1)) continue;
        const dx = Math.abs(x1 - x0), sx = x0 < x1 ? 1 : -1;
        const dy = -Math.abs(y1 - y0), sy = y0 < y1 ? 1 : -1;
        let err = dx + dy;
        let guard = 0;
        while (guard++ < 2048) {
          ctx.fillRect(x0 * grid - halfOff, y0 * grid - halfOff, thick, thick);
          if (x0 === x1 && y0 === y1) break;
          const e2 = err * 2;
          if (e2 >= dy) { err += dy; x0 += sx; }
          if (e2 <= dx) { err += dx; y0 += sy; }
        }
      }
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- cached：准备
    // 先把所有生物的贴图都解析好（要烘的在这一步烘完），再统一画；
    // 这样画的时候图集不会再被改，浏览器不用为「改了正在用的图」额外复制。
    prepareActor(actor, hab, c, s) {
      const partList = actor.buildPartList(hab);
      const sizeScale = actor.scaleMul ?? 1.0;
      const rootX = actor.state.x, rootY = actor.state.y;
      for (let i = 0; i < partList.length; i++) {
        const p = partList[i];
        if (p.kind === 'line') {
          const mx = (p.x0 + p.x1) * 0.5;
          const my = (p.y0 + p.y1) * 0.5;
          const mz = (p.z0 + p.z1) * 0.5;
          const dxW = (mx - rootX) / sizeScale;
          const dyW = (my - rootY) / sizeScale;
          p._depth = s * dxW + c * dyW + (mz / sizeScale) * 0.08 + (p.bias || 0);
        } else {
          const dxW = (p.x - rootX) / sizeScale;
          const dyW = (p.y - rootY) / sizeScale;
          p._depth = s * dxW + c * dyW + (p.lift / sizeScale) * 0.08 + (p.bias || 0);
        }
      }
      partList.sort((a, b) => a._depth - b._depth);

      const outlineHex = (actor.curSkin && actor.curSkin.outline) || '#0c121a';
      const k = this.artPx;
      const grid = Math.max(1, k);
      const SC = this.SCALE;
      const W2 = this.cv.width / 2, H2 = this.cv.height / 2 + 16 * SC;
      const isWater = !!hab.isWater;
      const th = this.th;

      for (let i = 0; i < partList.length; i++) {
        const p = partList[i];
        if (p.kind === 'line') {
          const widthArt = Math.max(1, Math.round(p.widthArtPx ?? p.widthPx ?? 1));
          p._pts = this._projectLinePoints(p, c, s, W2, H2, SC, grid);
          p._grid = grid;
          p._thick = widthArt * grid;
          p._op = p.opacity ?? 1;
          p._color = p.color || '#d8d0c0';
          p._outlineCol = p.outlineColor || '';
          continue;
        }
        const sub = p.submerge > 0 ? p.submerge : 0;
        let tint = 0;
        let op = p.opacity ?? 1;
        if (sub > 0) {
          if (isWater) tint = Math.min(SUBMERGE_LEVELS, Math.round(sub * SUBMERGE_LEVELS));
          else op *= 1 - sub * 0.44; // 旧写法：非水域里用 submerge 表示半透明，照旧
        }
        const e = this.getSprite(p.asset, p.ro + th, p.symDeg || 360, tint, outlineHex);
        const mir = this._mirror;
        const X = W2 + (c * p.x - s * p.y) * SC;
        const Y = H2 + (s * p.x + c * p.y) * TILT * SC - p.lift * HS * SC;
        const kx = k * sizeScale * (p.scaleX || 1);
        const ky = k * sizeScale * (p.scaleY || 1);
        p._e = e;
        p._op = op;
        p._m = mir;
        p._dx = Math.round(X - (mir ? e.bw - e.ax : e.ax) * kx);
        p._dy = Math.round(Y - e.ay * ky);
        p._dw = Math.round(e.bw * kx);
        p._dh = Math.round(e.bh * ky);
      }
      this.stats.frameParts += partList.length;
      return partList;
    }

    _glowUnion(x, y, w, h) {
      const g = this._g;
      if (!this._glowOn) {
        g[0] = x; g[1] = y; g[2] = x + w; g[3] = y + h;
        this._glowOn = true;
        return;
      }
      if (x < g[0]) g[0] = x;
      if (y < g[1]) g[1] = y;
      if (x + w > g[2]) g[2] = x + w;
      if (y + h > g[3]) g[3] = y + h;
    }

    drawPreparedActor(parts) {
      const d = this.dctx, ec = this.ectx;
      const eS = this.EW / this.cv.width;
      let calls = 0;

      d.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      d.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      d.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;

      // 第 1 遍：需要描边的部件外扩轮廓（单部件设 noOutline: true 时跳过）
      // 半透明部件的轮廓按旧版「四向叠 4 次」的累积不透明度画：1 - (1 - a)^4
      let alpha = 1, flipped = false;
      d.globalAlpha = 1;
      if (this.useOutline) {
        for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
          const p = parts[i];
          if (p.noOutline) continue;
          if (p.kind === 'line') {
            if (!p._outlineCol) continue;
            if (flipped) { d.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); flipped = false; }
            if (p._op !== alpha) { alpha = p._op; d.globalAlpha = alpha; }
            this._drawPixelLine(d, p._pts, p._grid, p._thick + p._grid * 2, p._outlineCol);
            calls++;
            continue;
          }
          const e = p._e;
          if (!e || e.gen !== e.page.gen) continue; // 这一帧里所在页被挤掉了，跳过一帧
          const op = p._op >= 1 ? 1 : 1 - Math.pow(1 - p._op, 4);
          if (op !== alpha) { alpha = op; d.globalAlpha = alpha; }
          if (p._m) {
            d.setTransform(-1, 0, 0, 1, 2 * p._dx + p._dw, 0); flipped = true;
          } else if (flipped) {
            d.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); flipped = false;
          }
          d.drawImage(e.page.canvas, e.rx, e.y, e.bw, e.bh, p._dx, p._dy, p._dw, p._dh);
          calls++;
        }
      }

      // 第 2 遍：按深度画本体与像素画线；自亮层同步遮挡
      const g = this._g;
      for (let i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
        const p = parts[i];
        if (p.kind === 'line') {
          if (flipped) { d.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); flipped = false; }
          if (p._op !== alpha) { alpha = p._op; d.globalAlpha = alpha; }
          this._drawPixelLine(d, p._pts, p._grid, p._thick, p._color);
          calls++;
          continue;
        }
        const e = p._e;
        if (!e || e.gen !== e.page.gen) continue;
        if (p._op !== alpha) { alpha = p._op; d.globalAlpha = alpha; }
        if (p._m) {
          d.setTransform(-1, 0, 0, 1, 2 * p._dx + p._dw, 0); flipped = true;
        } else if (flipped) {
          d.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0); flipped = false;
        }
        d.drawImage(e.page.canvas, e.x, e.y, e.bw, e.bh, p._dx, p._dy, p._dw, p._dh);
        calls++;

        const ex = p._dx * eS, ey = p._dy * eS, ew = p._dw * eS, eh = p._dh * eS;
        const occlude = this._glowOn && ex < g[2] && ex + ew > g[0] && ey < g[3] && ey + eh > g[1];
        if (occlude || e.gx >= 0) {
          ec.setTransform(p._m ? -1 : 1, 0, 0, 1, p._m ? 2 * ex + ew : 0, 0);
          if (occlude) {
            ec.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
            ec.globalAlpha = 1;
            ec.drawImage(e.page.canvas, e.x, e.y, e.bw, e.bh, ex, ey, ew, eh);
            calls++;
          }
          if (e.gx >= 0) {
            ec.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
            ec.globalAlpha = p._op;
            ec.drawImage(e.page.canvas, e.gx, e.y, e.bw, e.bh, ex, ey, ew, eh);
            this._glowUnion(ex, ey, ew, eh);
            calls++;
          }
        }
      }
      d.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      d.globalAlpha = 1;
      ec.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      ec.globalAlpha = 1;
      ec.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      return calls;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- crisp / retro（对照用）
    drawPartRealtime(p, sizeScale, isRetro) {
      const { mctx, ectx, CW, CH, EW, EH, SCALE, th } = this;
      const sc = (isRetro ? 1 : SCALE) * sizeScale;
      const [X, Y] = this.projBuf(p.x, p.y, p.lift, isRetro);
      const a = p.ro + th, c = Math.cos(a), s = Math.sin(a);
      const X0 = Math.round(X), Y0 = Math.round(Y);
      const step = isRetro ? (HS * sizeScale) : Math.max(1, Math.round(HS * sc));
      const halfStep = (!isRetro && step > Math.floor(TILT * sc)) ? Math.max(1, Math.round(step * 0.5)) : 0;
      const { w, d, activeLayers, glowLayers, glowSlot, glowStrip, strip } = p.asset;
      const hasGlow = !!glowStrip && glowLayers.length > 0;
      const minGlowZ = hasGlow ? glowLayers[0] : 9999;
      const eScaleX = EW / CW, eScaleY = EH / CH;
      const alpha = (p.opacity ?? 1) * (p.submerge > 0 ? (1 - p.submerge * 0.44) : 1);
      const hw = -w * 0.5, hd = -d * 0.5;
      let calls = 0;

      mctx.globalAlpha = alpha;
      for (let li = 0; li < activeLayers.length; li++) {
        const z = activeLayers[li];
        const yy = Y0 - z * step;
        const sx = z * w;
        if (halfStep) {
          mctx.setTransform(c * sc, TILT * s * sc, -s * sc, TILT * c * sc, X0, yy + halfStep);
          mctx.drawImage(strip, sx, 0, w, d, hw, hd, w, d);
          calls++;
        }
        mctx.setTransform(c * sc, TILT * s * sc, -s * sc, TILT * c * sc, X0, yy);
        mctx.drawImage(strip, sx, 0, w, d, hw, hd, w, d);
        calls++;
        if (!hasGlow || z >= minGlowZ) {
          ectx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
          ectx.globalAlpha = 1;
          ectx.setTransform(c * sc * eScaleX, TILT * s * sc * eScaleY, -s * sc * eScaleX, TILT * c * sc * eScaleY, X0 * eScaleX, yy * eScaleY);
          ectx.drawImage(strip, sx, 0, w, d, hw, hd, w, d);
        }
        if (hasGlow && glowSlot[z] >= 0) {
          ectx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
          ectx.globalAlpha = alpha;
          ectx.drawImage(glowStrip, glowSlot[z] * w, 0, w, d, hw, hd, w, d);
          calls++;
        }
      }
      mctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ectx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ectx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      ectx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      return calls;
    }

    drawActorRealtime(actor, hab, c, s, isRetro) {
      const { dctx, mctx, octx, mc, oc, CW, CH, SCALE } = this;
      const partList = actor.buildPartList(hab);
      const outlineHex = (actor.curSkin && actor.curSkin.outline) || '#0c121a';
      const sizeScale = actor.scaleMul ?? 1.0;
      let calls = 0;
      mctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      mctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      mctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
      mctx.clearRect(0, 0, CW, CH);

      const rootX = actor.state.x, rootY = actor.state.y;
      let hasSpecial = false;
      for (let i = 0; i < partList.length; i++) {
        const p = partList[i];
        if (p.noOutline || p.kind === 'line') hasSpecial = true;
        if (p.kind === 'line') {
          const mx = (p.x0 + p.x1) * 0.5, my = (p.y0 + p.y1) * 0.5, mz = (p.z0 + p.z1) * 0.5;
          p._depth = s * ((mx - rootX) / sizeScale) + c * ((my - rootY) / sizeScale) + (mz / sizeScale) * 0.08 + (p.bias || 0);
        } else {
          const dxW = (p.x - rootX) / sizeScale;
          const dyW = (p.y - rootY) / sizeScale;
          p._depth = s * dxW + c * dyW + (p.lift / sizeScale) * 0.08 + (p.bias || 0);
        }
      }
      partList.sort((a, b) => a._depth - b._depth);

      const drawW = isRetro ? CW * SCALE : CW;
      const drawH = isRetro ? CH * SCALE : CH;
      const scBuf = isRetro ? 1 : SCALE;
      const gridBuf = isRetro ? 1 : Math.max(1, this.artPx);
      const W2B = CW / 2, H2B = CH / 2 + 16 * scBuf;
      const drawLineInBuf = (p, targetCtx, col, extraPx) => {
        const pts = this._projectLinePoints(p, c, s, W2B, H2B, scBuf, gridBuf);
        const wArt = Math.max(1, Math.round(p.widthArtPx ?? p.widthPx ?? 1));
        targetCtx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
        targetCtx.globalAlpha = p.opacity ?? 1;
        this._drawPixelLine(targetCtx, pts, gridBuf, wArt * gridBuf + extraPx, col);
        targetCtx.globalAlpha = 1;
      };

      if (!hasSpecial) {
        for (let i = 0; i < partList.length; i++) calls += this.drawPartRealtime(partList[i], sizeScale, isRetro);
        dctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
        dctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
        if (this.useOutline) {
          octx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
          octx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
          octx.clearRect(0, 0, CW, CH);
          octx.drawImage(mc, 0, 0);
          octx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-in';
          octx.fillStyle = outlineHex;
          octx.fillRect(0, 0, CW, CH);
          octx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
          const op = isRetro ? SCALE : Math.max(1, Math.round(SCALE * 0.30));
          dctx.drawImage(oc, -op, 0, drawW, drawH);
          dctx.drawImage(oc,  op, 0, drawW, drawH);
          dctx.drawImage(oc, 0, -op, drawW, drawH);
          dctx.drawImage(oc, 0,  op, drawW, drawH);
          calls += 4;
        }
        dctx.drawImage(mc, 0, 0, drawW, drawH);
        return calls + 1;
      }

      // 含 noOutline 部件或像素画线：先为需描边件生成轮廓，再按严密深度画全部本体与线条
      dctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      dctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
      if (this.useOutline) {
        for (let i = 0; i < partList.length; i++) {
          const p = partList[i];
          if (p.noOutline) continue;
          if (p.kind === 'line') {
            if (p.outlineColor) drawLineInBuf(p, mctx, p.outlineColor, gridBuf * 2);
          } else {
            calls += this.drawPartRealtime(p, sizeScale, isRetro);
          }
        }
        octx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
        octx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
        octx.clearRect(0, 0, CW, CH);
        octx.drawImage(mc, 0, 0);
        octx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-in';
        octx.fillStyle = outlineHex;
        octx.fillRect(0, 0, CW, CH);
        octx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
        const op = isRetro ? SCALE : Math.max(1, Math.round(SCALE * 0.30));
        dctx.drawImage(oc, -op, 0, drawW, drawH);
        dctx.drawImage(oc,  op, 0, drawW, drawH);
        dctx.drawImage(oc, 0, -op, drawW, drawH);
        dctx.drawImage(oc, 0,  op, drawW, drawH);
        calls += 4;
        mctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
        mctx.clearRect(0, 0, CW, CH);
      }

      for (let i = 0; i < partList.length; i++) {
        const p = partList[i];
        if (p.kind === 'line') {
          drawLineInBuf(p, mctx, p.color || '#d8d0c0', 0);
          calls++;
        } else {
          calls += this.drawPartRealtime(p, sizeScale, isRetro);
        }
      }
      dctx.drawImage(mc, 0, 0, drawW, drawH);
      return calls + 1;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 地表
    drawGround(platform, actors, animTime, c, s) {
      const { dctx, gbctx, gbuf, cv, SCALE } = this;
      const hab = platform.def;
      const fx = hab.fx;
      const bSize = platform.size;
      const halfB = bSize * 0.5;
      gbctx.setTransform(GROUND_SUPERSAMPLE, 0, 0, GROUND_SUPERSAMPLE, 0, 0);
      gbctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      gbctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
      gbctx.clearRect(0, 0, bSize, bSize);
      gbctx.drawImage(platform.base, 0, 0);

      gbctx.save();
      gbctx.translate(halfB, halfB);
      gbctx.beginPath();
      gbctx.arc(0, 0, platform.rInner, 0, TAU);
      gbctx.clip();

      if (hab.isWater) {
        gbctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${fx.foamRGB}, 0.16)`;
        gbctx.lineWidth = 1.0;
        for (let w = 0; w < 9; w++) {
          const angW = w * 0.72 + animTime * 0.14;
          const radW = 22 + (w % 3) * 19;
          gbctx.beginPath();
          gbctx.arc(Math.cos(angW) * radW, Math.sin(angW) * radW, 8 + Math.sin(animTime * 1.6 + w) * 2.0, -0.5, 0.7);
          gbctx.stroke();
        }
      }

      for (const wk of this.vfx.wakes) {
        const u = clamp(wk.t / wk.life, 0, 1);
        const fade = (1 - u) * (1 - u);
        const spread = wk.width * (0.65 + u * 2.5);
        const back = 4.5 + u * 14.0;
        const [lx, ly] = localToWorldXY(wk.x, wk.y, wk.yaw, -spread, -back);
        const [rx, ry] = localToWorldXY(wk.x, wk.y, wk.yaw,  spread, -back);
        const [cx, cy] = localToWorldXY(wk.x, wk.y, wk.yaw, 0, 2.0);
        gbctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${fx.ringRGB}, ${fade * 0.62})`;
        gbctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1.1, (1 - u) * 3.0);
        gbctx.beginPath(); gbctx.moveTo(lx, ly); gbctx.lineTo(cx, cy); gbctx.lineTo(rx, ry); gbctx.stroke();
        gbctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${fx.foamRGB}, ${fade * 0.85})`;
        gbctx.lineWidth = Math.max(0.7, (1 - u) * 1.6);
        gbctx.beginPath(); gbctx.moveTo(lx, ly); gbctx.lineTo(cx, cy); gbctx.lineTo(rx, ry); gbctx.stroke();
      }

      for (const rp of this.vfx.ripples) {
        const u = clamp(rp.t / rp.life, 0, 1);
        const rr = lerp(rp.r0, rp.r1, smoothstep(0, 1, u));
        gbctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${fx.ringRGB}, ${(1 - u) * 0.84})`;
        gbctx.lineWidth = rp.width;
        gbctx.beginPath(); gbctx.arc(rp.x, rp.y, rr, 0, TAU); gbctx.stroke();
      }

      // 地表痕迹层（沙垄/土隆 mound、水沟/湿痕 wet、焦痕/裂纹 crack）与留成摆件（slab/mushroom/sprout）
      this._drawGroundTraces(gbctx, hab);

      for (const actor of actors) {
        const shScale = actor.state.shadowScale ?? 1;
        if (shScale > 0.01) {
          const sh = EnvFeedback.calcGroundShadow(actor.state.z, (actor.def.footRadius || 10) * (actor.scaleMul || 1));
          const shA = sh.alpha * shScale;
          gbctx.fillStyle = hab.isWater ? `rgba(6, 24, 38, ${shA * 0.95})` : `rgba(10, 14, 22, ${shA})`;
          gbctx.beginPath();
          gbctx.ellipse(actor.state.x + sh.offsetX, actor.state.y + sh.offsetY, sh.radius, sh.radius * 0.85, actor.state.yaw, 0, TAU);
          gbctx.fill();
        }

        if (actor.footContacts) {
          for (const fc of actor.footContacts) {
            const fl = clamp(1 - fc.z / 12, 0.25, 1);
            gbctx.fillStyle = `rgba(8, 12, 20, ${0.46 * fl})`;
            gbctx.beginPath();
            gbctx.arc(fc.x, fc.y, (fc.r || 3.2) * (0.7 + 0.3 * fl), 0, TAU);
            gbctx.fill();
          }
        }

        if (hab.isWater && actor.state.z < 6.0 && actor.def.useWaterFoam) {
          const rFoam = (actor.def.footRadius || 9) * (actor.scaleMul || 1);
          gbctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${fx.foamRGB}, 0.62)`;
          gbctx.lineWidth = 1.4;
          gbctx.beginPath();
          gbctx.ellipse(actor.state.x, actor.state.y, rFoam + Math.sin(animTime * 4) * 0.9, rFoam * 0.86, actor.state.yaw, 0, TAU);
          gbctx.stroke();
        }
      }
      gbctx.restore();

      dctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      dctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      dctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      dctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
      this.drawSky();
      dctx.setTransform(c * SCALE, TILT * s * SCALE, -s * SCALE, TILT * c * SCALE,
        cv.width / 2, cv.height / 2 + 16 * SCALE);
      dctx.drawImage(gbuf, -halfB, -halfB, bSize, bSize);
      dctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      return 2;
    }

    // 绘制地表痕迹层与留成摆件（直接绘进 gbuf，随地台自动旋转与圆台裁切，零额外贴图预算）
    _drawGroundTraces(gbctx, hab) {
      const vfx = this.vfx;
      if (vfx.decalCount === 0 && vfx.propCount === 0) return;

      const pal = hab.pal || {};
      const fx = hab.fx || {};
      const palDeep = pal.deep || '#7a5832';
      const palMid  = pal.mid  || '#b8864b';
      const palHi   = pal.hi   || '#e2b676';
      const palRim  = pal.rimA || '#4a3520';
      const foamRGB = fx.foamRGB || '235, 224, 196';

      // 1. 贴地痕迹（从最旧到最新绘制，保证新拱起的沙垄盖在旧痕之上）
      if (vfx.decalCount > 0) {
        const cap = vfx.maxDecals;
        const start = vfx.decalHead;
        for (let k = 0; k < cap; k++) {
          const d = vfx.decals[(start + k) % cap];
          if (!d || !d.active) continue;
          const u = clamp(d.t / d.life, 0, 1);
          const fadeIn = smoothstep(0, 0.08, u);
          const fadeOut = 1 - smoothstep(0.62, 1.0, u);
          const a = d.alpha * fadeIn * fadeOut;
          if (a <= 0.02) continue;

          gbctx.save();
          gbctx.translate(d.x, d.y);
          gbctx.rotate(d.yaw);

          if (d.kind === 'mound') {
            // 沙垄 / 土隆：柔和暗缘 + 沿行进方向拉长的隆起主脊 + 亮脊线 + 侧缘翻土碎粒
            const rx = d.rx * 0.90, ry = d.ry * 1.15;
            gbctx.globalAlpha = a * 0.34;
            gbctx.fillStyle = d.darkColor || palRim;
            gbctx.beginPath();
            gbctx.ellipse(-0.5, 0.6, rx * 1.10, ry * 1.04, 0, 0, TAU);
            gbctx.fill();

            gbctx.globalAlpha = a * 0.78;
            gbctx.fillStyle = d.color || palDeep;
            gbctx.beginPath();
            gbctx.ellipse(0, 0, rx * 0.96, ry * 0.96, 0, 0, TAU);
            gbctx.fill();

            gbctx.globalAlpha = a * 0.88;
            gbctx.fillStyle = d.hiColor || palHi;
            gbctx.beginPath();
            gbctx.ellipse(0.30, -0.25, rx * 0.54, ry * 0.68, 0, 0, TAU);
            gbctx.fill();

            // 两侧细碎翻沙点（由 seed 固定，不闪烁）
            gbctx.globalAlpha = a * 0.55;
            gbctx.fillStyle = d.darkColor || palDeep;
            const s1 = ((d.seed * 37) % 100) / 100 - 0.5;
            const s2 = ((d.seed * 61) % 100) / 100 - 0.5;
            gbctx.fillRect(Math.round(rx * 0.82 + s1 * 1.1), Math.round(s2 * ry), 1.4, 1.4);
            gbctx.fillRect(Math.round(-rx * 0.92 + s2 * 1.1), Math.round(-s1 * ry), 1.4, 1.4);
          } else if (d.kind === 'wet') {
            // 水沟 / 湿痕：深色湿槽 + 居中水膜反色条
            const rx = d.rx, ry = d.ry;
            gbctx.globalAlpha = a * 0.52;
            gbctx.fillStyle = d.darkColor || palDeep;
            gbctx.beginPath();
            gbctx.ellipse(0, 0, rx * 1.12, ry * 1.08, 0, 0, TAU);
            gbctx.fill();

            gbctx.globalAlpha = a * 0.68;
            gbctx.fillStyle = d.color || palRim;
            gbctx.beginPath();
            gbctx.ellipse(0, 0, rx * 0.74, ry * 0.84, 0, 0, TAU);
            gbctx.fill();

            gbctx.globalAlpha = a * 0.34;
            gbctx.fillStyle = d.hiColor || `rgb(${foamRGB})`;
            gbctx.beginPath();
            gbctx.ellipse(rx * 0.12, -ry * 0.08, rx * 0.30, ry * 0.52, 0, 0, TAU);
            gbctx.fill();
          } else if (d.kind === 'crack') {
            // 焦痕 / 裂纹：焦枯底斑 + 放射折线裂隙
            const r = Math.max(d.rx, d.ry);
            gbctx.globalAlpha = a * 0.45;
            gbctx.fillStyle = d.color || palRim;
            gbctx.beginPath();
            gbctx.ellipse(0, 0, d.rx, d.ry, 0, 0, TAU);
            gbctx.fill();

            gbctx.globalAlpha = a * 0.88;
            gbctx.strokeStyle = d.darkColor || '#1e1612';
            gbctx.lineWidth = 1.05;
            const rays = 4;
            for (let ri = 0; ri < rays; ri++) {
              const ang = (ri / rays) * TAU + ((d.seed + ri * 19) % 17) * 0.07;
              const midR = r * (0.45 + ((d.seed + ri * 7) % 5) * 0.06);
              const endR = r * (0.95 + ((d.seed + ri * 11) % 5) * 0.07);
              const bend = (((d.seed + ri * 13) % 2) ? 1 : -1) * 0.24;
              gbctx.beginPath();
              gbctx.moveTo(0, 0);
              gbctx.lineTo(Math.cos(ang + bend) * midR, Math.sin(ang + bend) * midR);
              gbctx.lineTo(Math.cos(ang) * endR, Math.sin(ang) * endR);
              gbctx.stroke();
            }
          } else {
            // 兜底通用贴地印
            gbctx.globalAlpha = a * 0.65;
            gbctx.fillStyle = d.color || palMid;
            gbctx.beginPath();
            gbctx.ellipse(0, 0, d.rx, d.ry, 0, 0, TAU);
            gbctx.fill();
          }

          gbctx.restore();
        }
      }

      // 2. 留成摆件（石板 / 小蘑菇 / 嫩芽等）
      if (vfx.propCount > 0) {
        const capP = vfx.maxProps;
        const startP = vfx.propHead;
        for (let k = 0; k < capP; k++) {
          const p = vfx.props[(startP + k) % capP];
          if (!p || !p.active) continue;
          const fadeOut = Number.isFinite(p.life) ? (1 - smoothstep(0.75, 1.0, p.t / p.life)) : 1;
          if (fadeOut <= 0.02) continue;

          gbctx.save();
          gbctx.translate(p.x, p.y);
          gbctx.rotate(p.yaw);
          gbctx.globalAlpha = fadeOut;

          if (p.kind === 'slab') {
            // 冷石板（供 火1 复用）
            const r = p.radius;
            gbctx.fillStyle = p.rimColor || '#1e191a';
            gbctx.fillRect(-r, -r * 0.85, r * 2, r * 1.7);
            gbctx.fillStyle = p.color || '#4a3d3c';
            gbctx.fillRect(-r + 1, -r * 0.85 + 1, r * 2 - 2, r * 1.7 - 2);
          } else if (p.kind === 'mushroom') {
            // 菌圈小蘑菇（供 洞5 复用）
            const r = Math.max(1.8, p.radius * 0.45);
            gbctx.fillStyle = p.rimColor || '#d8ccb2';
            gbctx.beginPath(); gbctx.arc(0, 0, r, 0, TAU); gbctx.fill();
            gbctx.fillStyle = p.color || '#a87d56';
            gbctx.beginPath(); gbctx.arc(-0.3, -0.3, r * 0.72, 0, TAU); gbctx.fill();
          } else if (p.kind === 'sprout') {
            // 嫩芽（供 树3 复用）
            gbctx.fillStyle = p.color || '#66b852';
            gbctx.beginPath(); gbctx.ellipse(-1.4, -0.5, 1.8, 0.9, -0.45, 0, TAU); gbctx.fill();
            gbctx.beginPath(); gbctx.ellipse( 1.4, -0.5, 1.8, 0.9,  0.45, 0, TAU); gbctx.fill();
          } else {
            gbctx.fillStyle = p.color || palMid;
            gbctx.beginPath(); gbctx.arc(0, 0, p.radius * 0.6, 0, TAU); gbctx.fill();
          }

          gbctx.restore();
        }
      }
      gbctx.globalAlpha = 1;
    }

    drawWaterFront(actor, hab) {
      const { dctx, SCALE } = this;
      const fx = hab.fx;
      if (!(hab.isWater && actor.state.z < 5.5 && actor.def.useWaterFoam)) return;
      const wet = clamp((5.5 - actor.state.z) / 5.5, 0.35, 1.0);
      const [wxP, wyP] = this.proj(actor.state.x, actor.state.y, 0.8);
      const wrX = (actor.def.footRadius || 9.5) * (actor.scaleMul || 1) * SCALE;
      const wrY = wrX * TILT;
      dctx.save();
      dctx.fillStyle = fx.waterTint;
      dctx.beginPath();
      dctx.ellipse(wxP, wyP, wrX * 0.94, wrY * 0.88, 0, 0, Math.PI);
      dctx.fill();
      dctx.strokeStyle = `rgba(${fx.foamRGB}, ${0.80 * wet})`;
      dctx.lineWidth = Math.max(1.5, SCALE * 0.34);
      dctx.beginPath();
      dctx.ellipse(wxP, wyP, wrX, wrY, 0, 0.1, Math.PI - 0.1);
      dctx.stroke();
      dctx.restore();
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- 一帧
    renderScene(platform, actors, animTime) {
      const { dctx, ectx, ebctx, cv, EW, EH, SCALE } = this;
      const hab = platform.def;
      const st = this.stats;
      this.frame++;
      this.atlas.frame = this.frame;
      this.scratchIdx = 0;
      st.frameBakes = 0; st.frameBakeMs = 0; st.frameDraws = 0; st.frameParts = 0;

      if (this.lastHabitatId !== hab.id) this.renderSky(hab);
      this.updateTint(hab);
      const c = Math.cos(this.th), s = Math.sin(this.th);
      const mode = this.getRenderMode();

      // 自亮缓冲：上一帧画过才需要清
      ectx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
      ectx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      ectx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ectx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
      if (this._ecDirty || mode !== 'cached') ectx.clearRect(0, 0, EW, EH);
      this._glowOn = false;

      // 远 → 近（支持寄生/搭载个体用 actor.state.sortBias 稳压在宿主前方）
      const sorted = this._sorted;
      sorted.length = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < actors.length; i++) {
        const a = actors[i];
        sorted.push({ actor: a, ry: s * a.state.x + c * a.state.y + (a.state.sortBias || 0), parts: null });
      }
      sorted.sort((a, b) => a.ry - b.ry);

      // 1. cached：先解析全部贴图（烘焙都在这一步）
      if (mode === 'cached') {
        for (const item of sorted) item.parts = this.prepareActor(item.actor, hab, c, s);
      }

      // 2. 背景 + 地台
      let drawCalls = this.drawGround(platform, actors, animTime, c, s);

      // 3. 生物
      const isRetro = mode === 'retro';
      for (const item of sorted) {
        if (mode === 'cached') drawCalls += this.drawPreparedActor(item.parts);
        else { drawCalls += this.drawActorRealtime(item.actor, hab, c, s, isRetro); this._glowOn = true; this._g[0] = 0; this._g[1] = 0; this._g[2] = EW; this._g[3] = EH; }
        this.drawWaterFront(item.actor, hab);
      }

      // 4. 3D 像素粒子
      const eScale = EW / cv.width;
      const W2 = cv.width / 2, H2 = cv.height / 2 + 16 * SCALE;
      for (const p of this.vfx.particles) {
        const u = p.t / p.life;
        const px = W2 + (c * p.x - s * p.y) * SCALE;
        const py = H2 + (s * p.x + c * p.y) * TILT * SCALE - p.z * HS * SCALE;
        const sPx = Math.max(2, Math.round(p.sz * (1 - u * 0.5) * SCALE));
        dctx.fillStyle = p.color;
        dctx.fillRect(Math.round(px) - (sPx >> 1), Math.round(py) - (sPx >> 1), sPx, sPx);
        if (p.glow) {
          const es = Math.max(1, Math.round(sPx * eScale));
          const gx = Math.round(px * eScale) - (es >> 1), gy = Math.round(py * eScale) - (es >> 1);
          ectx.fillStyle = p.color;
          ectx.fillRect(gx, gy, es, es);
          this._glowUnion(gx, gy, es, es);
        }
      }

      // 5. 自亮柔化：只处理有光的那一块；整帧没光就整段跳过
      if (this._glowOn) {
        const blur = Math.max(1, Math.round(SCALE * 0.16));
        const pad = blur * 3 + 2;
        const g = this._g;
        const x0 = clamp(Math.floor(g[0] - pad), 0, EW), y0 = clamp(Math.floor(g[1] - pad), 0, EH);
        const x1 = clamp(Math.ceil(g[2] + pad), 0, EW), y1 = clamp(Math.ceil(g[3] + pad), 0, EH);
        const w = x1 - x0, h = y1 - y0;
        if (w > 0 && h > 0) {
          const inv = cv.width / EW;
          ebctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
          ebctx.clearRect(0, 0, EW, EH);
          ebctx.filter = `blur(${blur}px)`;
          ebctx.drawImage(this.ec, x0, y0, w, h, x0, y0, w, h);
          ebctx.filter = 'none';

          dctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
          dctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'screen';
          dctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = true;
          dctx.globalAlpha = 0.68;
          dctx.drawImage(this.eb, x0, y0, w, h, x0 * inv, y0 * inv, w * inv, h * inv);
          dctx.globalAlpha = 0.48;
          dctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
          dctx.drawImage(this.ec, x0, y0, w, h, x0 * inv, y0 * inv, w * inv, h * inv);
          dctx.globalAlpha = 1;
          dctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
          drawCalls += 3;
        }
      }
      this._ecDirty = this._glowOn;

      st.frameDraws = drawCalls;
      this.cacheHits = st.hits;
      this.cacheMisses = st.misses;
      return { drawCalls };
    }
  }

  PW.Renderer = {
    TILT, HS, BAKE_SCALE, BAKE_STEP,
    makeCanvas, VFXPool, SpriteAtlas, StackRenderer
  };
})(window.PixelWorkshop);


===== FILE: ./core/solvers.js =====
'use strict';

window.PixelWorkshop = window.PixelWorkshop || {};

(function (PW) {
  const { clamp, lerp, expSmooth, wrapAngle, localToWorldXY, fbm1 } = PW.Math;

  // =========================================================================
  // 1. 真·P5.js 拖拽 + 最大转角约束物理链（ChainSolver v2）
  //    头一拐弯，后半截身体留在原水路上被一节节拽着走，自然甩出流畅 S 弯！
  // =========================================================================
  class ChainSolver {
    constructor(count, segLen, opts = {}) {
      this.count = count;
      this.segLen = segLen;
      this.maxAngle = opts.maxAngle ?? 0.52; // 相邻节最大弯曲弧度（防折成锐角）
      this.waveFreq = opts.waveFreq ?? 4.2;
      this.wavePhaseStep = opts.wavePhaseStep ?? 0.48;
      this.waveAmp = opts.waveAmp ?? 0.35;
      this.nodes = Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => ({
        x: 0, y: -i * segLen, z: 4, yaw: 0
      }));
    }

    reset(rootX, rootY, rootZ, yaw) {
      for (let i = 0; i < this.count; i++) {
        const [wx, wy] = localToWorldXY(rootX, rootY, yaw, 0, -i * this.segLen);
        this.nodes[i].x = wx;
        this.nodes[i].y = wy;
        this.nodes[i].z = rootZ;
        this.nodes[i].yaw = yaw;
      }
    }

    // 真·距离 + 转角约束 + 切线法向行进波（彻底告别“跟着整体旋转的硬扇子”）
    update(dt, rootX, rootY, rootZ, rootYaw, time, cfg = {}) {
      const nodes = this.nodes;
      const segLen = cfg.segLen ?? this.segLen;
      const flex = cfg.flexibility ?? 1.0;
      const maxAng = clamp((cfg.maxAngle ?? this.maxAngle) * (0.65 + flex * 0.55), 0.22, 1.05);
      const fanAngle = cfg.fanAngle ?? 0;
      const waveFreq = cfg.waveFreq ?? this.waveFreq;
      const waveAmp = (cfg.waveAmp ?? this.waveAmp) * flex;
      const wavePhaseStep = cfg.wavePhaseStep ?? this.wavePhaseStep;
      const phaseOffset = cfg.phaseOffset ?? 0;
      const restAlign = cfg.restAlign ?? 0.08; // 极轻柔的归位趋势，保留 92% 真实拖拽惯性
      const zTargetFn = cfg.zTargetFn || (() => rootZ);
      const kz = expSmooth(dt, cfg.zFollowSpeed ?? 11.0);

      // 根节点紧贴身体挂点
      nodes[0].x = rootX;
      nodes[0].y = rootY;
      nodes[0].z = rootZ;
      nodes[0].yaw = rootYaw + fanAngle;

      // 从第 1 节往末节逐节做 P5.js 距离约束 + 最大夹角约束
      for (let i = 1; i < nodes.length; i++) {
        const u = i / Math.max(1, nodes.length - 1);
        const prev = nodes[i - 1];
        const cur = nodes[i];

        // 1) 当前节点相对前一节的方向向量（保留上一帧世界坐标的真实拖拽轨迹！）
        let dx = cur.x - prev.x;
        let dy = cur.y - prev.y;
        let dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (dist < 0.001) {
          const [fx, fy] = localToWorldXY(0, 0, prev.yaw, 0, -1);
          dx = fx; dy = fy; dist = 1;
        }

        // 前一节指向后方的基准角（正面 +Y，后方 -Y 对应 yaw + PI）
        let backAng = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
        const prevBackAng = prev.yaw - Math.PI * 0.5; // -Y 在 (cos, sin) 极坐标下为 yaw - PI/2

        // 2) 叠加上极轻柔的游泳推进侧摆 + 扇形张开趋势（只施加柔和角度偏移，不抹杀拖拽弯曲！）
        const swimWave = Math.sin(time * waveFreq - i * wavePhaseStep + phaseOffset) * waveAmp * (0.35 + u * 0.95);
        const desiredBack = prevBackAng + (i === 1 ? fanAngle * 0.35 : 0) + swimWave * 0.35;
        backAng += wrapAngle(desiredBack - backAng) * clamp(restAlign * dt * 60, 0, 0.35);

        // 3) 角度约束（Angle Constraint）：限制相对前一节的最大折角，保证肉体曲线圆润顺滑
        const rel = wrapAngle(backAng - prevBackAng);
        const clampedRel = clamp(rel, -maxAng, maxAng);
        const finalBack = prevBackAng + clampedRel;

        // 4) 严格距离约束（Distance Constraint）
        cur.x = prev.x + Math.cos(finalBack) * segLen;
        cur.y = prev.y + Math.sin(finalBack) * segLen;
        cur.z = lerp(cur.z, zTargetFn(u, i, time), kz);

        // 当前节朝向（指向前一节的方向，即 finalBack + PI/2）
        cur.yaw = wrapAngle(finalBack + Math.PI * 0.5);
      }

      return nodes;
    }
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // 2. 精确 2-Bone 平面解析 IK + 通用多足步态器（2 / 4 / 6 足共用）
  //    支持急转身自动补步、支撑重心偏移量输出、平滑抛物线抬脚与落地冲击事件
  // =========================================================================
  function solveIK2D(hx, hz, tx, tz, L1, L2, bendDir = 1) {
    const dx = tx - hx, dz = tz - hz;
    const rawD = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 0.0001;
    const minD = Math.abs(L1 - L2) + 0.02;
    const maxD = L1 + L2 - 0.02;
    const d = clamp(rawD, minD, maxD);
    const cosA = clamp((L1 * L1 + d * d - L2 * L2) / (2 * L1 * d), -1, 1);
    const baseAng = Math.atan2(dz, dx);
    const kneeAng = baseAng + bendDir * Math.acos(cosA);
    return {
      kx: hx + Math.cos(kneeAng) * L1,
      kz: hz + Math.sin(kneeAng) * L1,
      ex: hx + (dx / rawD) * d,
      ez: hz + (dz / rawD) * d
    };
  }

  // 矢状面 3D 2-Bone IK：在躯干自身前后矢状面内解算膝肘（前肢肘向后折 kneeSign=-1，后肢膝向前折 kneeSign=+1）
  // 彻底消灭传统「髋->足射线平面」IK 在后足滞后时把膝盖甩到屁股后面、以及过髋时 180° 翻转的奇点
  function solveSagittalLegIK(hx, hy, hz, fx, fy, fz, ayaw, L1, L2, kneeSign, sMul = 1.0) {
    const fwdX = -Math.sin(ayaw), fwdY = Math.cos(ayaw);
    const rightX = Math.cos(ayaw), rightY = Math.sin(ayaw);
    const dx = fx - hx, dy = fy - hy;
    const dFwd = dx * fwdX + dy * fwdY;
    const dSide = dx * rightX + dy * rightY;
    const dZ = fz - hz;
    const rawD = Math.hypot(dFwd, dZ) || 0.001;
    const minD = Math.abs(L1 - L2) + 0.15 * sMul;
    const maxD = L1 + L2 - 0.12 * sMul;
    const d = clamp(rawD, minD, maxD);
    const cosA = clamp((L1 * L1 + d * d - L2 * L2) / (2 * L1 * d), -1, 1);
    const sinA = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 1 - cosA * cosA));
    const uFwd = dFwd / rawD;
    const uZ = dZ / rawD;
    const along = L1 * cosA;
    const perp = L1 * sinA * kneeSign;
    const kFwd = along * uFwd + perp * (-uZ);
    const kZ = hz + along * uZ + perp * uFwd;
    const kSide = dSide * 0.45;
    return {
      kx: hx + kFwd * fwdX + kSide * rightX,
      ky: hy + kFwd * fwdY + kSide * rightY,
      kz: Math.max(1.3 * sMul, kZ)
    };
  }

  class MultiLegGaitSolver {
    constructor(legs, opts = {}) {
      const autoMode = legs.length === 2 ? 'biped' : (legs.length === 6 ? 'tripod' : 'walk4');
      this.mode = opts.mode || autoMode; // 'walk4' | 'trot' | 'bound' | 'biped' | 'tripod'
      this.stepThreshold = opts.stepThreshold ?? 5.2;
      this.stepDuration = opts.stepDuration ?? 0.25;
      this.stepHeight = opts.stepHeight ?? 4.6;
      this.strideAnticipation = opts.strideAnticipation ?? 0.50;
      this.settleDelay = opts.settleDelay ?? 0.16;
      this.settleThreshold = opts.settleThreshold ?? 1.05;
      this.idleTime = 0;
      this.lastStepClock = -10;
      this.clock = 0;
      this.gaitPhase = 0;
      this.prevBodyYaw = 0;
      this.hasPrevYaw = false;
      this.lastOrderIdx = -1;

      this.legs = legs.map((l, idx) => {
        const isFront = (l.restY ?? 0) >= 0;
        const isLeft = (l.restX ?? 0) < 0;
        // 自然四拍顺序：左后(0) -> 左前(1) -> 右后(2) -> 右前(3)
        let walkOrder = idx;
        let walkOff = 0, trotOff = 0, boundOff = 0;
        let tripodGroup = l.tripodGroup;
        let tripodOff = 0;
        if (legs.length === 4) {
          if (!isFront && isLeft)       { walkOrder = 0; walkOff = 0.00; trotOff = 0.00; boundOff = 0.00; }
          else if (isFront && isLeft)   { walkOrder = 1; walkOff = 0.25; trotOff = 0.55; boundOff = 0.48; }
          else if (!isFront && !isLeft) { walkOrder = 2; walkOff = 0.50; trotOff = 0.50; boundOff = 0.08; }
          else                          { walkOrder = 3; walkOff = 0.75; trotOff = 0.05; boundOff = 0.56; }
        } else if (legs.length === 6) {
          // 6 足三角步态默认分组：L1+R2+L3 为 0 组（0°），R1+L2+R3 为 1 组（180° 反相）
          if (tripodGroup === undefined) {
            const rowIdx = (l.restY ?? 0) > 2.0 ? 0 : ((l.restY ?? 0) < -2.0 ? 2 : 1);
            tripodGroup = ((isLeft ? 0 : 1) + rowIdx) & 1;
          }
          tripodOff = tripodGroup ? 0.50 : 0.00;
          trotOff = tripodOff;
        }
        return {
          ...l,
          index: idx,
          isFront,
          isLeft,
          walkOrder,
          walkOff,
          trotOff,
          boundOff,
          tripodGroup: tripodGroup ?? 0,
          tripodOff,
          kneeSign: l.kneeSign ?? (isFront ? -1 : 1),
          footX: l.restX,
          footY: l.restY,
          footZ: 0,
          startX: l.restX,
          startY: l.restY,
          targetX: l.restX,
          targetY: l.restY,
          stepping: false,
          stepT: 0,
          curStepDur: this.stepDuration,
          curStepHeight: this.stepHeight,
          isSettling: false,
          cooldown: 0,
          kneeX: l.restX,
          kneeY: l.restY,
          kneeZ: (l.hipZ || 8) * 0.55,
          hipWX: l.hipX,
          hipWY: l.hipY,
          hipWZ: l.hipZ || 8,
          fwdOffset: 0,
          sideOffset: 0,
          strideState: 'mid', // 'forward' | 'mid' | 'back'
          justLanded: false
        };
      });
    }

    reset(bodyX, bodyY, bodyYaw, scaleMul = 1.0, legAnchors = null) {
      this.idleTime = 0;
      this.clock = 0;
      this.gaitPhase = 0;
      this.lastStepClock = -10;
      this.prevBodyYaw = bodyYaw;
      this.hasPrevYaw = true;
      for (const leg of this.legs) {
        const anc = (legAnchors && (legAnchors[leg.id] || legAnchors[leg.index])) || null;
        const ax = anc ? anc.x : bodyX;
        const ay = anc ? anc.y : bodyY;
        const ayaw = anc ? anc.yaw : bodyYaw;
        const [rx, ry] = localToWorldXY(ax, ay, ayaw, leg.restX * scaleMul, leg.restY * scaleMul);
        const [hx, hy] = localToWorldXY(ax, ay, ayaw, leg.hipX * scaleMul, leg.hipY * scaleMul);
        leg.hipWX = hx;
        leg.hipWY = hy;
        leg.anchorYaw = ayaw;
        leg.footX = leg.startX = leg.targetX = rx;
        leg.footY = leg.startY = leg.targetY = ry;
        leg.footZ = 0;
        leg.stepping = false;
        leg.stepT = 0;
        leg.isSettling = false;
        leg.cooldown = 0;
        leg.fwdOffset = 0;
        leg.sideOffset = 0;
        leg.strideState = 'mid';
        leg.justLanded = false;
      }
    }

    update(dt, bodyX, bodyY, bodyZ, bodyYaw, velX, velY, opts = {}) {
      const sMul = opts.scaleMul ?? 1.0;
      const mode = opts.mode || this.mode;
      const stepFreq = opts.stepFreq ?? 1.0;
      const baseStepHeight = opts.stepHeight ?? (this.stepHeight * sMul);
      const baseStepDur = clamp((opts.stepDuration ?? this.stepDuration) / Math.max(0.35, stepFreq), 0.11, 0.48);
      const threshold = (opts.stepThreshold ?? (this.stepThreshold * sMul)) / Math.max(0.55, Math.sqrt(stepFreq));
      const settleThr = (opts.settleThreshold ?? 1.35) * sMul;
      const legAnchors = opts.legAnchors || null;
      const landed = [];

      this.clock += dt;
      const yawRate = this.hasPrevYaw ? Math.abs(wrapAngle(bodyYaw - this.prevBodyYaw)) / Math.max(dt, 0.001) : 0;
      this.prevBodyYaw = bodyYaw;
      this.hasPrevYaw = true;

      const moveSpd = Math.hypot(velX, velY);
      if (moveSpd < 1.35 && yawRate < 0.22) {
        this.idleTime += dt;
      } else {
        this.idleTime = 0;
      }

      // 第一步：更新髋位（支持每条腿挂在独立胸/胯锚点上）、理想落点与距离
      let liftSum = 0;
      let frontLift = 0, rearLift = 0;
      let localRoll = 0, localPitch = 0;
      let steppingCount = 0;
      const isQuadruped = this.legs.length === 4 && mode !== 'biped';
      const isHexapod = this.legs.length === 6 && mode !== 'biped';
      const isMoving = moveSpd > 1.35 || yawRate > 0.22;
      const isSettlingNow = this.idleTime > this.settleDelay;

      if (isQuadruped) {
        // 四足动物专用：位移驱动循环步态 + 支撑期钉地防拖腿 + 矢状面 3D IK（彻底消灭后腿拖行与膝盖后翻）
        const strideLen = Math.max(8.5 * sMul, threshold * 2.45);
        if (isMoving) {
          const dPhase = ((moveSpd / strideLen) + yawRate * 0.22) * dt * clamp(stepFreq, 0.45, 2.0);
          this.gaitPhase = (this.gaitPhase + dPhase) % 1;
        }
        const swingFrac = mode === 'bound' ? 0.48 : (mode === 'trot' ? 0.46 : 0.40);
        const reachHalf = clamp(threshold * 0.66, 2.1 * sMul, 3.8 * sMul);
        const maxBackBehindHip = Math.max(2.8 * sMul, threshold * 0.78);
        const maxFromIdeal = Math.max(4.0 * sMul, threshold * 1.15);
        const activeStepH = Math.max(baseStepHeight, 2.6 * sMul);
        let settlingAny = this.legs.some(l => l.isSettling && l.stepping);

        for (const leg of this.legs) {
          leg.justLanded = false;
          const anc = (legAnchors && (legAnchors[leg.id] || legAnchors[leg.index])) || null;
          const ax = anc ? anc.x : bodyX;
          const ay = anc ? anc.y : bodyY;
          const az = (anc && anc.z !== undefined) ? anc.z : bodyZ;
          const ayaw = anc ? anc.yaw : bodyYaw;
          leg.anchorYaw = ayaw;

          const [hx, hy] = localToWorldXY(ax, ay, ayaw, leg.hipX * sMul, leg.hipY * sMul);
          const hz = az + (leg.hipZ ?? 8) * sMul;
          leg.hipWX = hx; leg.hipWY = hy; leg.hipWZ = hz;

          const [idealX, idealY] = localToWorldXY(ax, ay, ayaw, leg.restX * sMul, leg.restY * sMul);
          leg._idealX = idealX;
          leg._idealY = idealY;
          const fwdX = -Math.sin(ayaw), fwdY = Math.cos(ayaw);
          const rightX = Math.cos(ayaw), rightY = Math.sin(ayaw);

          if (isMoving) {
            leg.isSettling = false;
            const off = mode === 'bound' ? leg.boundOff : (mode === 'trot' ? leg.trotOff : leg.walkOff);
            const phi = (this.gaitPhase + off) % 1;
            const wantSwing = phi < swingFrac;
            const speedRatio = clamp(moveSpd / 12.0, 0.30, 1.12);
            const landX = idealX + fwdX * reachHalf * speedRatio;
            const landY = idealY + fwdY * reachHalf * speedRatio;

            if (wantSwing) {
              if (!leg.stepping) {
                leg.stepping = true;
                leg.startX = leg.footX;
                leg.startY = leg.footY;
              }
              const u = clamp(phi / swingFrac, 0, 1);
              leg.stepT = u;
              const s = u * u * (3 - 2 * u);
              leg.footX = lerp(leg.startX, landX, s);
              leg.footY = lerp(leg.startY, landY, s);
              const arcPow = mode === 'walk4' ? 0.84 : (mode === 'bound' ? 0.74 : 0.78);
              leg.footZ = Math.pow(Math.sin(u * Math.PI), arcPow) * activeStepH * (0.60 + 0.40 * speedRatio);
              liftSum += leg.footZ;
              if (leg.isFront) frontLift += leg.footZ;
              else rearLift += leg.footZ;
              steppingCount++;
            } else {
              if (leg.stepping) {
                leg.stepping = false;
                leg.stepT = 1;
                leg.footX = landX;
                leg.footY = landY;
                leg.footZ = 0;
                leg.justLanded = true;
                landed.push(leg);
              } else {
                leg.footZ = 0;
              }
              // 支撑期钉地同时限制最大髋后滞后，彻底杜绝后腿被拖着走
              const dxH = leg.footX - hx, dyH = leg.footY - hy;
              const fwdFromHip = dxH * fwdX + dyH * fwdY;
              const sideFromHip = dxH * rightX + dyH * rightY;
              if (fwdFromHip < -maxBackBehindHip) {
                leg.footX = hx - maxBackBehindHip * fwdX + sideFromHip * rightX;
                leg.footY = hy - maxBackBehindHip * fwdY + sideFromHip * rightY;
              }
              const dId = Math.hypot(leg.footX - idealX, leg.footY - idealY);
              if (dId > maxFromIdeal && dId > 0.001) {
                const kFix = maxFromIdeal / dId;
                leg.footX = idealX + (leg.footX - idealX) * kFix;
                leg.footY = idealY + (leg.footY - idealY) * kFix;
              }
            }
          } else {
            // 停步收脚：平稳落完空中脚，再依次轻抬收回肩胯正下
            if (leg.stepping && !leg.isSettling) {
              leg.isSettling = true;
              leg.stepT = 0.25;
              leg.startX = leg.footX;
              leg.startY = leg.footY;
              settlingAny = true;
            }
            const dId = Math.hypot(leg.footX - idealX, leg.footY - idealY);
            if (!leg.stepping && !settlingAny && isSettlingNow && dId > settleThr) {
              leg.stepping = true;
              leg.isSettling = true;
              leg.stepT = 0;
              leg.startX = leg.footX;
              leg.startY = leg.footY;
              settlingAny = true;
            }
            if (leg.stepping && leg.isSettling) {
              leg.stepT += dt / 0.17;
              if (leg.stepT >= 1) {
                leg.stepping = false;
                leg.isSettling = false;
                leg.stepT = 1;
                leg.footX = idealX;
                leg.footY = idealY;
                leg.footZ = 0;
                leg.justLanded = true;
                landed.push(leg);
              } else {
                const u = leg.stepT;
                const s = u * u * (3 - 2 * u);
                leg.footX = lerp(leg.startX, idealX, s);
                leg.footY = lerp(leg.startY, idealY, s);
                leg.footZ = Math.sin(u * Math.PI) * activeStepH * 0.48;
                liftSum += leg.footZ;
                steppingCount++;
              }
            } else {
              leg.footZ = 0;
            }
          }
          leg._dist = Math.hypot(idealX - leg.footX, idealY - leg.footY);
        }
      } else if (isHexapod) {
        // 6 足节肢专用：三角步态（Tripod Gait，L1+R2+L3 与 R1+L2+R3 180° 反相，占空比 β≈0.54）
        const strideLen = Math.max(7.2 * sMul, threshold * 2.25);
        if (isMoving) {
          const dPhase = ((moveSpd / strideLen) + yawRate * 0.24) * dt * clamp(stepFreq, 0.45, 2.2);
          this.gaitPhase = (this.gaitPhase + dPhase) % 1;
        }
        const swingFrac = 0.46; // 占空比 β = 0.54，双组交替瞬间有短暂 6 足全接地防悬空
        const reachHalf = clamp(threshold * 0.54, 1.4 * sMul, 2.5 * sMul);
        const maxFromIdeal = Math.max(3.2 * sMul, threshold * 1.08);
        const activeStepH = clamp(baseStepHeight, 1.3 * sMul, 3.0 * sMul);
        // 停步收足时按组收拢，保证另一组 3 足稳稳支撑
        let activeSettleGroup = -1;
        for (const l of this.legs) {
          if (l.isSettling && l.stepping) { activeSettleGroup = l.tripodGroup; break; }
        }
        if (!isMoving && isSettlingNow && activeSettleGroup < 0) {
          let g0Err = 0, g1Err = 0;
          for (const l of this.legs) {
            const anc = (legAnchors && (legAnchors[l.id] || legAnchors[l.index])) || null;
            const ax = anc ? anc.x : bodyX;
            const ay = anc ? anc.y : bodyY;
            const ayaw = anc ? anc.yaw : bodyYaw;
            const [ix, iy] = localToWorldXY(ax, ay, ayaw, l.restX * sMul, l.restY * sMul);
            const d = Math.hypot(ix - l.footX, iy - l.footY);
            if (l.tripodGroup === 0) g0Err = Math.max(g0Err, d);
            else g1Err = Math.max(g1Err, d);
          }
          if (g0Err > settleThr || g1Err > settleThr) {
            activeSettleGroup = g0Err >= g1Err ? 0 : 1;
          }
        }

        for (const leg of this.legs) {
          leg.justLanded = false;
          const anc = (legAnchors && (legAnchors[leg.id] || legAnchors[leg.index])) || null;
          const ax = anc ? anc.x : bodyX;
          const ay = anc ? anc.y : bodyY;
          const az = (anc && anc.z !== undefined) ? anc.z : bodyZ;
          const ayaw = anc ? anc.yaw : bodyYaw;
          leg.anchorYaw = ayaw;

          const [hx, hy] = localToWorldXY(ax, ay, ayaw, leg.hipX * sMul, leg.hipY * sMul);
          const hz = az + (leg.hipZ ?? 4.6) * sMul;
          leg.hipWX = hx; leg.hipWY = hy; leg.hipWZ = hz;

          const [idealX, idealY] = localToWorldXY(ax, ay, ayaw, leg.restX * sMul, leg.restY * sMul);
          leg._idealX = idealX;
          leg._idealY = idealY;
          const fwdX = -Math.sin(ayaw), fwdY = Math.cos(ayaw);

          if (isMoving) {
            leg.isSettling = false;
            const phi = (this.gaitPhase + leg.tripodOff) % 1;
            const wantSwing = phi < swingFrac;
            const speedRatio = clamp(moveSpd / 11.0, 0.32, 1.08);
            const landX = idealX + fwdX * reachHalf * speedRatio;
            const landY = idealY + fwdY * reachHalf * speedRatio;

            if (wantSwing) {
              if (!leg.stepping) {
                leg.stepping = true;
                leg.startX = leg.footX;
                leg.startY = leg.footY;
              }
              const u = clamp(phi / swingFrac, 0, 1);
              leg.stepT = u;
              const s = u * u * (3 - 2 * u);
              leg.footX = lerp(leg.startX, landX, s);
              leg.footY = lerp(leg.startY, landY, s);
              leg.footZ = Math.pow(Math.sin(u * Math.PI), 0.85) * activeStepH * (0.68 + 0.32 * speedRatio);
              liftSum += leg.footZ;
              if (leg.isFront) frontLift += leg.footZ;
              else rearLift += leg.footZ;
              steppingCount++;
            } else {
              if (leg.stepping) {
                leg.stepping = false;
                leg.stepT = 1;
                leg.footX = landX;
                leg.footY = landY;
                leg.footZ = 0;
                leg.justLanded = true;
                landed.push(leg);
              } else {
                leg.footZ = 0;
              }
              // 支撑期钉地，同时约束与理想落点最大间距防止急转交叉打架
              const dId = Math.hypot(leg.footX - idealX, leg.footY - idealY);
              if (dId > maxFromIdeal && dId > 0.001) {
                const kFix = maxFromIdeal / dId;
                leg.footX = idealX + (leg.footX - idealX) * kFix;
                leg.footY = idealY + (leg.footY - idealY) * kFix;
              }
            }
          } else {
            // 停步收足：先平稳落完空中足，再按三角分组轻抬收回理想落点
            if (leg.stepping && !leg.isSettling) {
              leg.isSettling = true;
              leg.stepT = 0.25;
              leg.startX = leg.footX;
              leg.startY = leg.footY;
            }
            const dId = Math.hypot(leg.footX - idealX, leg.footY - idealY);
            if (!leg.stepping && isSettlingNow && activeSettleGroup === leg.tripodGroup && dId > settleThr) {
              leg.stepping = true;
              leg.isSettling = true;
              leg.stepT = 0;
              leg.startX = leg.footX;
              leg.startY = leg.footY;
            }
            if (leg.stepping && leg.isSettling) {
              leg.stepT += dt / 0.16;
              if (leg.stepT >= 1) {
                leg.stepping = false;
                leg.isSettling = false;
                leg.stepT = 1;
                leg.footX = idealX;
                leg.footY = idealY;
                leg.footZ = 0;
                leg.justLanded = true;
                landed.push(leg);
              } else {
                const u = leg.stepT;
                const s = u * u * (3 - 2 * u);
                leg.footX = lerp(leg.startX, idealX, s);
                leg.footY = lerp(leg.startY, idealY, s);
                leg.footZ = Math.sin(u * Math.PI) * activeStepH * 0.55;
                liftSum += leg.footZ;
                steppingCount++;
              }
            } else {
              leg.footZ = 0;
            }
          }
          leg._dist = Math.hypot(idealX - leg.footX, idealY - leg.footY);
        }
      } else {
        // 二足 / 其他足数：保留原阈值交替步态
        for (const leg of this.legs) {
          leg.justLanded = false;
          if (leg.cooldown > 0) leg.cooldown = Math.max(0, leg.cooldown - dt);

          const anc = (legAnchors && (legAnchors[leg.id] || legAnchors[leg.index])) || null;
          const ax = anc ? anc.x : bodyX;
          const ay = anc ? anc.y : bodyY;
          const az = (anc && anc.z !== undefined) ? anc.z : bodyZ;
          const ayaw = anc ? anc.yaw : bodyYaw;
          leg.anchorYaw = ayaw;

          const [hx, hy] = localToWorldXY(ax, ay, ayaw, leg.hipX * sMul, leg.hipY * sMul);
          const hz = az + (leg.hipZ ?? 8) * sMul;
          leg.hipWX = hx; leg.hipWY = hy; leg.hipWZ = hz;

          const [idealX, idealY] = localToWorldXY(ax, ay, ayaw, leg.restX * sMul, leg.restY * sMul);
          leg._idealX = idealX;
          leg._idealY = idealY;
          leg._dist = Math.hypot(idealX - leg.footX, idealY - leg.footY);

          const maxReach = Math.max(threshold * 2.15, ((leg.L1 ?? 6.0) + (leg.L2 ?? 6.2)) * sMul * 0.92);
          if (leg._dist > maxReach && leg._dist > 0.001) {
            const kFix = maxReach / leg._dist;
            leg.footX = idealX + (leg.footX - idealX) * kFix;
            leg.footY = idealY + (leg.footY - idealY) * kFix;
            leg._dist = maxReach;
          }

          if (leg.stepping) {
            steppingCount++;
            leg.stepT += dt / Math.max(0.08, leg.curStepDur);
            if (leg.stepT >= 1) {
              leg.stepT = 1;
              leg.stepping = false;
              leg.isSettling = false;
              leg.footX = leg.targetX;
              leg.footY = leg.targetY;
              leg.footZ = 0;
              leg.cooldown = mode === 'biped' ? 0.04 : 0.055;
              leg.justLanded = true;
              steppingCount--;
              landed.push(leg);
            } else {
              const u = leg.stepT;
              const s = u * u * (3 - 2 * u);
              leg.footX = lerp(leg.startX, leg.targetX, s);
              leg.footY = lerp(leg.startY, leg.targetY, s);
              leg.footZ = Math.pow(Math.sin(u * Math.PI), 0.86) * leg.curStepHeight;
              liftSum += leg.footZ;
              if (leg.isFront) frontLift += leg.footZ;
              else rearLift += leg.footZ;
            }
          } else {
            leg.footZ = 0;
          }
        }

        const startLegStep = (leg, isSettle) => {
          leg.stepping = true;
          leg.stepT = 0;
          leg.isSettling = isSettle;
          leg.startX = leg.footX;
          leg.startY = leg.footY;
          leg.curStepDur = clamp(baseStepDur * (isSettle ? 0.85 : 1.0), 0.10, 0.48);
          leg.curStepHeight = isSettle ? baseStepHeight * 0.42 : baseStepHeight;
          if (isSettle) {
            leg.targetX = leg._idealX;
            leg.targetY = leg._idealY;
          } else {
            leg.targetX = leg._idealX + velX * leg.curStepDur * this.strideAnticipation;
            leg.targetY = leg._idealY + velY * leg.curStepDur * this.strideAnticipation;
          }
          this.lastStepClock = this.clock;
          this.lastOrderIdx = leg.walkOrder;
          steppingCount++;
        };

        if (isSettlingNow) {
          if (steppingCount === 0) {
            let worst = null, maxD = settleThr;
            for (const l of this.legs) {
              if (!l.stepping && l.cooldown <= 0 && l._dist > maxD) { maxD = l._dist; worst = l; }
            }
            if (worst) startLegStep(worst, true);
          }
        } else if (steppingCount === 0) {
          let best = null, bestD = threshold;
          for (const l of this.legs) {
            if (!l.stepping && l.cooldown <= 0 && l._dist > bestD) { bestD = l._dist; best = l; }
          }
          if (best) startLegStep(best, false);
        }
      }

      // 第三步：计算局部重心倾摆、前后跨步偏移与 IK（四足用矢状面 3D IK，二足沿用 2D 平面 IK）
      let stridePulse = 0;
      const stateBand = Math.max(1.25 * sMul, threshold * 0.28);
      for (const leg of this.legs) {
        const ayaw = leg.anchorYaw ?? bodyYaw;
        const fwdDirX = -Math.sin(ayaw), fwdDirY = Math.cos(ayaw);
        const rightDirX = Math.cos(ayaw), rightDirY = Math.sin(ayaw);

        if (leg.stepping) {
          const w = Math.sin(leg.stepT * Math.PI);
          const rollGain = mode === 'biped' ? 0.85 : (mode === 'walk4' ? 0.40 : 0.28);
          const pitchGain = mode === 'bound' ? 0.72 : (mode === 'walk4' ? 0.26 : 0.22);
          localRoll -= Math.sign(leg.restX || 0) * w * rollGain;
          localPitch -= Math.sign(leg.restY || 0) * w * pitchGain;
          stridePulse = Math.max(stridePulse, w);
        }

        const relX = leg.footX - leg._idealX;
        const relY = leg.footY - leg._idealY;
        const fwd = relX * fwdDirX + relY * fwdDirY;
        const side = relX * rightDirX + relY * rightDirY;
        leg.fwdOffset = fwd;
        leg.sideOffset = side;

        if (leg.strideState === 'forward') {
          if (fwd < stateBand * 0.45) leg.strideState = fwd < -stateBand ? 'back' : 'mid';
        } else if (leg.strideState === 'back') {
          if (fwd > -stateBand * 0.45) leg.strideState = fwd > stateBand ? 'forward' : 'mid';
        } else {
          if (fwd > stateBand) leg.strideState = 'forward';
          else if (fwd < -stateBand) leg.strideState = 'back';
        }

        if (isQuadruped) {
          const ik = solveSagittalLegIK(
            leg.hipWX, leg.hipWY, leg.hipWZ, leg.footX, leg.footY, leg.footZ,
            ayaw, (leg.L1 ?? 6.2) * sMul, (leg.L2 ?? 6.4) * sMul, leg.kneeSign, sMul
          );
          leg.kneeX = ik.kx;
          leg.kneeY = ik.ky;
          leg.kneeZ = ik.kz;
        } else {
          const dx = leg.footX - leg.hipWX, dy = leg.footY - leg.hipWY;
          const horiz = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 0.001;
          const ik = solveIK2D(0, leg.hipWZ, horiz, leg.footZ, (leg.L1 ?? 6.8) * sMul, (leg.L2 ?? 7.4) * sMul, leg.bendDir ?? 1);
          const ratio = ik.kx / horiz;
          leg.kneeX = leg.hipWX + dx * ratio;
          leg.kneeY = leg.hipWY + dy * ratio;
          leg.kneeZ = Math.max(1.2 * sMul, ik.kz);
        }
      }

      // 将局部重心倾摆正确旋转到世界坐标（修复旧版直接加世界 XY 的方向错乱 Bug！）
      const [tiltX, tiltY] = localToWorldXY(0, 0, bodyYaw, localRoll, localPitch);

      return {
        landed,
        liftSum,
        frontLift,
        rearLift,
        tiltX,
        tiltY,
        localRoll,
        localPitch,
        stridePulse,
        steppingCount,
        isSettling: isSettlingNow
      };
    }
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // 3. 二阶弹簧阻尼求解器（SpringDamper1D / SpringDamper3D）
  // =========================================================================
  class SpringDamper1D {
    constructor(initial = 0, stiffness = 90, damping = 10) {
      this.x = initial;
      this.v = 0;
      this.stiffness = stiffness;
      this.damping = damping;
    }

    impulse(dv) { this.v += dv; }

    update(dt, target, stiffness = this.stiffness, damping = this.damping) {
      const sub = Math.min(dt, 0.033);
      const acc = (target - this.x) * stiffness - this.v * damping;
      this.v += acc * sub;
      this.x += this.v * sub;
      return this.x;
    }
  }

  class SpringDamper3D {
    constructor(x = 0, y = 0, z = 0, stiffness = 85, damping = 9) {
      this.x = x; this.y = y; this.z = z;
      this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0; this.vz = 0;
      this.stiffness = stiffness;
      this.damping = damping;
    }

    reset(x, y, z) {
      this.x = x; this.y = y; this.z = z;
      this.vx = this.vy = this.vz = 0;
    }

    impulse(dvx, dvy, dvz) {
      this.vx += dvx; this.vy += dvy; this.vz += dvz;
    }

    update(dt, tx, ty, tz, stiffness = this.stiffness, damping = this.damping) {
      const sub = Math.min(dt, 0.033);
      const ax = (tx - this.x) * stiffness - this.vx * damping;
      const ay = (ty - this.y) * stiffness - this.vy * damping;
      const az = (tz - this.z) * stiffness - this.vz * damping;
      this.vx += ax * sub; this.vy += ay * sub; this.vz += az * sub;
      this.x += this.vx * sub; this.y += this.vy * sub; this.z += this.vz * sub;
      return this;
    }
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // 4. 呼吸缩放与噪声微动（体积守恒 Squash & Stretch：sx * sy * sz ≈ 1）
  // =========================================================================
  function evalBreathing(time, opts = {}) {
    const freq = opts.freq ?? 2.2;
    const amp = opts.amp ?? 0.08;
    const noiseAmp = opts.noiseAmp ?? 0.025;
    const seed = opts.seed ?? 17;
    const pulse = Math.sin(time * freq) * amp + (fbm1(time * 0.9, seed) - 0.5) * 2 * noiseAmp;
    const sz = 1 + pulse;
    const sxy = 1 / Math.sqrt(Math.max(0.55, sz));
    return {
      sx: sxy,
      sy: sxy,
      sz,
      liftOffset: pulse * (opts.liftGain ?? 3.0),
      pulse
    };
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // 5. 挂点跟随追踪器（SocketTracker：单一真源正向运动学）
  // =========================================================================
  class SocketTracker {
    constructor() {
      this.map = Object.create(null);
    }

    setLocal(name, rootX, rootY, rootZ, yaw, lx, ly, lz, scaleMul = 1.0) {
      const [wx, wy] = localToWorldXY(rootX, rootY, yaw, lx * scaleMul, ly * scaleMul);
      this.map[name] = { x: wx, y: wy, z: rootZ + lz * scaleMul, yaw };
      return this.map[name];
    }

    setWorld(name, x, y, z, yaw = 0) {
      this.map[name] = { x, y, z, yaw };
      return this.map[name];
    }

    get(name) {
      return this.map[name] || { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0, yaw: 0 };
    }
  }

  PW.Solvers = {
    ChainSolver,
    solveIK2D,
    solveSagittalLegIK,
    MultiLegGaitSolver,
    SpringDamper1D,
    SpringDamper3D,
    evalBreathing,
    SocketTracker
  };
})(window.PixelWorkshop);


===== FILE: ./core/voxel.js =====
'use strict';

window.PixelWorkshop = window.PixelWorkshop || {};

(function (PW) {
  const { clamp, distSeg3Sq, distTri3Sq, hexToRgba } = PW.Math;

  const MAT_BASE = 128;

  // 创建独立调色板上下文（支持 <= 48 色校验、自亮键隔离、零重建模换色）
  function createPaletteSpec(config) {
    const pal = config.pal || {};
    const glowKeys = new Set(config.glowKeys || ['emissiveMid', 'emissiveHot', 'emissiveCore']);
    const keys = Object.keys(pal);
    if (keys.length > 48) {
      throw new Error(`Palette exceeds 48 colors (${keys.length})`);
    }
    const keyToId = Object.create(null);
    const idToKey = [null];
    const idToRgba = [null];
    const glowIdSet = new Set();

    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
      const k = keys[i];
      const id = i + 1;
      keyToId[k] = id;
      idToKey[id] = k;
      idToRgba[id] = hexToRgba(pal[k]);
      if (glowKeys.has(k)) glowIdSet.add(id);
    }

    const partNames = ['none', ...(config.parts || [])];
    const partId = Object.create(null);
    for (let i = 0; i < partNames.length; i++) partId[partNames[i]] = i;

    const numParts = partNames.length;
    const noCreaseLut = new Uint8Array(numParts * numParts);
    for (const pair of (config.noCrease || [])) {
      const ia = partId[pair[0]], ib = partId[pair[1]];
      if (ia && ib) {
        noCreaseLut[ia * numParts + ib] = 1;
        noCreaseLut[ib * numParts + ia] = 1;
      }
    }

    const matNames = [null, ...(config.materials ? Object.keys(config.materials) : [])];
    const matId = Object.create(null);
    const rampsByMat = Object.create(null);
    for (let i = 1; i < matNames.length; i++) {
      const mName = matNames[i];
      matId[mName] = i;
      const rampKeys = config.materials[mName];
      rampsByMat[i] = rampKeys.map(rk => keyToId[rk]);
    }

    const mat = (name) => MAT_BASE + matId[name];
    const protectedParts = new Set((config.protectedParts || ['eye']).map(n => partId[n]).filter(Boolean));

    function buildRgbaTable(skin) {
      const tbl = idToRgba.slice();
      if (skin && skin.pal) {
        for (const k in skin.pal) {
          const id = keyToId[k];
          if (id) tbl[id] = hexToRgba(skin.pal[k]);
        }
      }
      return tbl;
    }

    return {
      pal, keys, keyToId, idToKey, idToRgba, glowKeys, glowIdSet,
      partNames, partId, numParts, noCreaseLut,
      matId, rampsByMat, mat, protectedParts, buildRgbaTable
    };
  }

  // 零重建模换色：直接按 Uint8Array 索引刷写长条图集 ImageData
  function recolorAsset(asset, rgbaTable, glowIdSet) {
    const { w, d, h, grid, activeLayers, glowLayers, glowSlot, strip, glowStrip, sim, eim } = asset;
    const stripW = w * h;
    const glowW = w * glowLayers.length;
    const sData = sim.data;
    const eData = eim ? eim.data : null;

    for (let li = 0; li < activeLayers.length; li++) {
      const z = activeLayers[li];
      const gxOff = z * w;
      const gSlot = glowSlot[z];
      const zBase = z * d * w;
      for (let y = 0; y < d; y++) {
        const row = zBase + y * w;
        const sRow = y * stripW + gxOff;
        const eRow = gSlot >= 0 ? (y * glowW + gSlot * w) : -1;
        for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
          const id = grid[row + x];
          if (!id) continue;
          const rgba = rgbaTable[id];
          const si = (sRow + x) * 4;
          sData[si] = rgba[0]; sData[si + 1] = rgba[1]; sData[si + 2] = rgba[2]; sData[si + 3] = 255;
          if (eData && eRow >= 0 && glowIdSet.has(id)) {
            const ei = (eRow + x) * 4;
            eData[ei] = rgba[0]; eData[ei + 1] = rgba[1]; eData[ei + 2] = rgba[2]; eData[ei + 3] = 255;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    strip.getContext('2d').putImageData(sim, 0, 0);
    if (glowStrip && eim) glowStrip.getContext('2d').putImageData(eim, 0, 0);
    // 版本号 +1：渲染器里这个部件的旧烘焙图自动作废（换色不用再手动清缓存）
    asset.rev = (asset.rev | 0) + 1;
  }

  class VoxelModel {
    constructor(w, d, h, spec) {
      this.w = w; this.d = d; this.h = h;
      this.spec = spec;
      const len = w * d * h;
      this.grid = new Uint8Array(len);
      this.parts = new Uint8Array(len);
    }

    idx(x, y, z) { return (z * this.d + y) * this.w + x; }
    inB(x, y, z) { return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && z >= 0 && x < this.w && y < this.d && z < this.h; }
    getId(x, y, z) { return this.inB(x, y, z) ? this.grid[this.idx(x, y, z)] : 0; }
    getPartId(x, y, z) { return this.inB(x, y, z) ? this.parts[this.idx(x, y, z)] : 0; }

    setRaw(x, y, z, val, partId) {
      if (!this.inB(x, y, z)) return;
      const i = this.idx(x, y, z);
      this.grid[i] = val;
      this.parts[i] = partId;
    }

    fillAABB(X0, X1, Y0, Y1, Z0, Z1, fn) {
      const hw = this.w * 0.5 - 0.5, hd = this.d * 0.5 - 0.5;
      const x0 = clamp(Math.floor(X0 + hw), 0, this.w - 1);
      const x1 = clamp(Math.ceil(X1 + hw),  0, this.w - 1);
      const y0 = clamp(Math.floor(Y0 + hd), 0, this.d - 1);
      const y1 = clamp(Math.ceil(Y1 + hd),  0, this.d - 1);
      const z0 = clamp(Math.floor(Z0),      0, this.h - 1);
      const z1 = clamp(Math.ceil(Z1),       0, this.h - 1);
      for (let z = z0; z <= z1; z++) {
        for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
