<script src="./bundle/boss_goblin_king.js" onerror="window.__MISSING_BOSS__=true"></script>
<script>
(function () {
  const PW = window.PixelWorkshop;
  const { HABITAT_DEFS, buildHabitatPlatform } = PW.Habitats;
  const { makeCanvas, StackRenderer } = PW.Renderer;
  const cv = document.getElementById('view');
  const stage = document.getElementById('stage');
  const hud = document.getElementById('hud');
  const warn = document.getElementById('warn');
  const habSel = document.getElementById('habSel');
  const actBox = document.getElementById('actBox');
  const skinBox = document.getElementById('skinBox');
  const metaBox = document.getElementById('metaBox');

  const renderer = new StackRenderer(cv);
  const habList = ['forest', 'ruins', 'desert', 'iceSea', 'volcano', 'swamp'];
  for (const id of habList) {
    const h = HABITAT_DEFS.find(x => x.id === id);
    if (!h) continue;
    const opt = document.createElement('option');
    opt.value = h.id;
    opt.textContent = `${h.code} · ${h.name}`;
    habSel.appendChild(opt);
  }
  let habId = 'forest';
  habSel.value = habId;

  const def = PW.Assets.byId['boss_goblin_king'] || PW.Assets.list[0];
  if (!def) {
    warn.style.display = 'block';
    warn.textContent = '尚未检测到 ./bundle/boss_goblin_king.js，请先将外包交付文件放入 bundle/ 后刷新预览。';
    hud.textContent = '等待交付物：./bundle/boss_goblin_king.js';
    const platform = buildHabitatPlatform(habId, makeCanvas, 200);
    renderer.fit(platform.def);
    renderer.renderScene(platform, [], 0);
    return;
  }

  document.getElementById('title').textContent = `${def.name || def.id}（${def.id}）`;
  const t0 = performance.now();
  const inst = PW.Assets.spawn(def, { seed: 42 });
  const initMs = performance.now() - t0;
  inst.def = def;
  inst.scaleMul = 1;
  inst.action = (def.actions && def.actions[0] && def.actions[0].id) || 'patrol';
  inst.actionTime = 0;

  const params = Object.assign({ gaitFreq: 1, tailFlex: 1, spring: 1, speed: 1, scale: 1 }, def.defaultParams || {});
  const partCount = inst.parts ? Object.keys(inst.parts).length : 0;
  metaBox.textContent = `初始化耗时：${initMs.toFixed(1)}ms · 部件资产：${partCount} 个 · 动作数：${(def.actions || []).length} · 皮肤数：${(def.skins || []).length}`;

  function renderActions() {
    actBox.innerHTML = '';
    for (const a of (def.actions || [])) {
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.textContent = a.name || a.id;
      if (a.id.startsWith('skill_') || a.id === 'stagger') b.classList.add('skill');
      if (inst.action === a.id) b.classList.add('on');
      b.onclick = () => { inst.action = a.id; inst.actionTime = 0; renderActions(); };
      actBox.appendChild(b);
    }
  }
  function renderSkins() {
    skinBox.innerHTML = '';
    const skins = def.skins || [];
    skins.forEach((s, i) => {
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.innerHTML = `<span class="skin-dot" style="background:${s.dot || '#999'}"></span>${s.name || s.id}`;
      if ((inst.curSkin || 0) === i) b.classList.add('on');
      b.onclick = () => { if (inst.applySkin) inst.applySkin(i); renderSkins(); };
      skinBox.appendChild(b);
    });
  }
  renderActions();
  renderSkins();

  let autoSpin = false;
  habSel.onchange = () => { habId = habSel.value; renderer.fit(buildHabitatPlatform(habId, makeCanvas, 200).def); };
  document.getElementById('btnSpin').onclick = (e) => { autoSpin = !autoSpin; e.target.classList.toggle('on', autoSpin); };
  document.getElementById('btnReset').onclick = () => { renderer.th = 0.56; renderer.zoom = 0; renderer.fit(buildHabitatPlatform(habId, makeCanvas, 200).def); };
  document.getElementById('btnMode').onclick = (e) => {
    renderer.modeIdx = (renderer.modeIdx + 1) % renderer.MODES.length;
    e.target.textContent = `模式：${renderer.MODE_LABELS[renderer.getRenderMode()]}`;
    renderer.fit(buildHabitatPlatform(habId, makeCanvas, 200).def);
  };

  let drag = null;
  stage.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => { drag = { x: e.clientX, th: renderer.th }; });
  window.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => { if (drag) renderer.th = drag.th + (e.clientX - drag.x) * 0.008; });
  window.addEventListener('pointerup', () => { drag = null; });
  stage.addEventListener('wheel', (e) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    renderer.zoom = Math.max(-3, Math.min(6, renderer.zoom + (e.deltaY < 0 ? 1 : -1)));
    renderer.fit(buildHabitatPlatform(habId, makeCanvas, 200).def);
  }, { passive: false });
  window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
    if (k === 'q') renderer.th -= Math.PI / 4;
    if (k === 'e') renderer.th += Math.PI / 4;
    if (k === 'c' && def.skins && def.skins.length && inst.applySkin) {
      inst.applySkin(((inst.curSkin || 0) + 1) % def.skins.length);
      renderSkins();
    }
  });
  window.addEventListener('resize', () => renderer.fit(buildHabitatPlatform(habId, makeCanvas, 200).def));

  renderer.fit(buildHabitatPlatform(habId, makeCanvas, 200).def);
  let last = performance.now(), animTime = 0, fpsAcc = 0, fpsFrames = 0, fpsShow = 60, drawMs = 0;

  function tick(now) {
    const dt = Math.min(0.05, Math.max(0.001, (now - last) / 1000));
    last = now;
    animTime += dt;
    if (autoSpin) renderer.th += dt * 0.55;

    const platform = buildHabitatPlatform(habId, makeCanvas, 200);
    const curAct = (def.actions || []).find(a => a.id === inst.action);
    inst.actionTime += dt;
    if (curAct && !curAct.loop && curAct.duration && inst.actionTime >= curAct.duration) {
      inst.action = (def.actions[0] && def.actions[0].id) || 'patrol';
      inst.actionTime = 0;
      renderActions();
    }

    const eco = {
      patrol: inst.action === 'patrol',
      action: inst.action,
      actionTime: inst.actionTime,
      homeX: 0, homeY: 0, radius: 68, others: []
    };
    inst.update(dt, animTime, params, platform.def, renderer.vfx, eco);
    if (typeof renderer.vfx.update === 'function') renderer.vfx.update(dt, platform.def);

    const r0 = performance.now();
    renderer.renderScene(platform, [inst], animTime);
    drawMs = performance.now() - r0;

    fpsAcc += dt; fpsFrames++;
    if (fpsAcc >= 0.4) { fpsShow = Math.round(fpsFrames / fpsAcc); fpsAcc = 0; fpsFrames = 0; }
    hud.textContent = `${fpsShow} fps · 渲染 ${drawMs.toFixed(2)}ms · 动作：${curAct ? curAct.name : inst.action} (${inst.actionTime.toFixed(1)}s)`;
    requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  requestAnimationFrame(tick);
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>


===== FILE: ./references/goblin.js =====
'use strict';

window.PixelWorkshop = window.PixelWorkshop || {};
window.PixelWorkshop.Creatures = window.PixelWorkshop.Creatures || [];

(function (PW) {
  const { clamp, lerp, smin, localToWorldXY, expSmooth } = PW.Math;
  const { createPaletteSpec, VoxelModel, recolorAsset } = PW.Voxel;
  const { MultiLegGaitSolver, SpringDamper1D, SocketTracker } = PW.Solvers;
  const { Propulsion, EnvFeedback } = PW.Behaviors;

  // =========================================================================
  // 苔绿哥布林 · 高清锐利 + 骨肉连贯 v3 骨骼化纯生态版（+Y 正前，z=0 贴地）
  // 重点修复腿身分离问题：
  //   1. 大腿上延至 z=10.5 并加粗髋根，躯干基准 lift=4.4 牢牢包裹大腿上段，0 悬空缝隙
  //   2. 收拢双足 restX=±2.4、stepThreshold=1.6、stepHeight=2.6，并限制脚部最大偏移半径，
  //      彻底杜绝急转或疾跑时双腿被甩出躯干外侧造成“身腿分离”
  //   3. 保持 1:1 锐利像素绘制、eyeGlint 不进 glowKeys、警觉快转头抖耳与摆臂甩棒
  // =========================================================================
  const SPEC = createPaletteSpec({
    pal: {
      skinDark:    '#2e6126',
      skinMid:     '#5ca339',
      skinHi:      '#9ad459',
      clothDark:   '#5e462d',
      clothMid:    '#8f6c47',
      clothHi:     '#bd966a',
      leatherDark: '#3d2417',
      leatherMid:  '#6e4228',
      leatherHi:   '#9e6540',
      woodDark:    '#4a2e1b',
      woodMid:     '#7d5030',
      woodHi:      '#b0794d',
      metalDark:   '#3a424d',
      metalMid:    '#6c788a',
      metalHi:     '#a6b5c7',
      boneDark:    '#8c8270',
      boneMid:     '#cfc5b2',
      boneHi:      '#f5efe1',
      goldDark:    '#9e6f14',
      goldMid:     '#e3ad24',
      goldHi:      '#ffe36e',
      eyeDark:     '#120f17',
      eyeIris:     '#f5d033',
      eyeGlint:    '#f7fcff',
      emissiveMid: '#ff4d1a',
      emissiveHot: '#ff9e26',
      emissiveCore:'#fff2b8'
    },
    glowKeys: ['emissiveMid', 'emissiveHot', 'emissiveCore'],
    parts: [
      'foot', 'leg', 'cloth', 'body', 'strap', 'pack', 'packTrim', 'packGold',
      'head', 'nose', 'mouth', 'eye', 'ear', 'arm', 'weapon', 'stud'
    ],
    noCrease: [
      ['eye', 'head'], ['nose', 'head'], ['mouth', 'head'], ['ear', 'head'],
      ['weapon', 'stud'], ['pack', 'packTrim'], ['pack', 'packGold'], ['leg', 'foot']
    ],
    materials: {
      skin:    ['skinDark',    'skinMid',    'skinHi'],
      cloth:   ['clothDark',   'clothMid',   'clothHi'],
      leather: ['leatherDark', 'leatherMid', 'leatherHi'],
      wood:    ['woodDark',    'woodMid',    'woodHi'],
      metal:   ['metalDark',   'metalMid',   'metalHi'],
      bone:    ['boneDark',    'boneMid',    'boneHi'],
      gold:    ['goldDark',    'goldMid',    'goldHi']
    },
    protectedParts: ['eye', 'mouth']
  });

  const SKINS = [
    { id: 'moss', name: '苔绿林贼', dot: '#6ab83e', outline: '#12100e', pal: {} },
    {
      id: 'ochre', name: '赭红精锐', dot: '#d9452b', outline: '#1a0c0c',
      pal: {
        skinDark:    '#6e1e18', skinMid:    '#b83c28', skinHi:    '#e86e48',
        clothDark:   '#22242b', clothMid:   '#3b3f4a', clothHi:   '#616878',
        leatherDark: '#2b1819', leatherMid: '#4f2a2c', leatherHi: '#7a4446',
        woodDark:    '#361b1b', woodMid:    '#5e2c2a', woodHi:    '#8a443f',
        metalDark:   '#7d161b', metalMid:   '#c7282b', metalHi:   '#f56258',
        eyeIris:     '#ffb829'
      }
    },
    {
      id: 'slate', name: '岩灰矿贼', dot: '#6e8c94', outline: '#101418',
      pal: {
        skinDark:    '#394a4f', skinMid:    '#627c82', skinHi:    '#96b4ba',
        clothDark:   '#6e4b16', clothMid:   '#b07d27', clothHi:   '#e3b14d',
        leatherDark: '#453322', leatherMid: '#73563a', leatherHi: '#a37e58',
        woodDark:    '#47321e', woodMid:    '#785636', woodHi:    '#ab7f55',
        metalDark:   '#576778', metalMid:   '#9ab0c4', metalHi:   '#dceaf5',
        eyeIris:     '#ffd83b'
      }
    },
    {
      id: 'indigo', name: '靛紫巫贼', dot: '#7d6bc4', outline: '#120e1c',
      pal: {
        skinDark:    '#3d3561', skinMid:    '#695e99', skinHi:    '#9f94cf',
        clothDark:   '#1d2452', clothMid:   '#32418a', clothHi:   '#576dc2',
        leatherDark: '#2c203d', leatherMid: '#4e3a6b', leatherHi: '#785d9e',
        woodDark:    '#6b6558', woodMid:    '#a8a08e', woodHi:    '#ded7c5',
        metalDark:   '#1a6b5e', metalMid:   '#2eb398', metalHi:   '#75ebd0',
        eyeIris:     '#4cf2d0'
      }
    }
  ];

  const EAR_PIVOT = { x: 4.5, y: 1.8 };
  const CLUB_PIVOT_Y = 2.0;

  // 1. 真屈膝连贯 IK 双腿（肌肉大腿 / 膝小腿 / 小皮靴，大腿根深嵌腰衣、皮靴踩实地面）
  function buildLegSeg(makeCanvas, rTop, rBot, hLen) {
    const S = Math.ceil(Math.max(rTop, rBot) * 2 + 3);
    const H = Math.ceil(hLen + 3);
    const m = new VoxelModel(S, S, H, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId } = SPEC;
    m.paintCapsule(0, 0, 1.0, 0, 0, H - 1.0, rBot, rTop, mat('skin'), partId.leg, true);
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(false);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: Math.max(rTop, rBot) });
  }

  function buildBoot(makeCanvas) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(7, 8, 4, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId } = SPEC;
    m.paintEllipsoid(0, 0.0, 2.2, 1.35, 1.35, 1.1, mat('skin'), partId.leg, true);
    m.paintEllipsoid(0, 1.0, 1.2, 1.75, 2.40, 1.25, mat('leather'), partId.foot, true);
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(false);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 2.5 });
  }

  function buildTorso(makeCanvas, runMode) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(13, runMode ? 15 : 13, 14, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId } = SPEC;
    const pitch = runMode ? 0.42 : 0.08;
    const cp = Math.cos(-pitch), sp = Math.sin(-pitch);
    m.fillAABB(-5.5, 5.5, -5.5, runMode ? 6.8 : 5.5, 0, 13, (x, y, z, X, Y) => {
      const dy = Y, dz = z - 4.2;
      const ly = dy * cp + dz * sp;
      const lz = 4.2 - dy * sp + dz * cp;
      const dTorso = (Math.hypot(X / 4.6, ly / 4.2, (lz - 7.2) / 4.6) - 1) * 4.2;
      const dNeck  = (Math.hypot(X / 2.7, (ly - 0.6) / 2.6, (lz - 11.4) / 2.2) - 1) * 2.2;
      if (smin(dTorso, dNeck, 1.3) <= 0) m.setRaw(x, y, z, mat('skin'), partId.body);
      const r = Math.hypot(X / 5.0, ly / 4.5);
      const inSkirt   = r <= 1.0 && r >= 0.52 && lz >= 1.2 && lz <= 5.4;
      const frontFlap = Math.abs(X) <= 2.4 && ly >= 2.0 && ly <= 4.7 && lz >= 0.6 && lz <= 4.8;
      const backFlap  = Math.abs(X) <= 2.5 && ly <= -2.0 && ly >= -4.5 && lz >= 0.8 && lz <= 4.6;
      if (inSkirt || frontFlap || backFlap) m.setRaw(x, y, z, mat('cloth'), partId.cloth);
    });
    const lyOff = runMode ? 1.4 : 0.3;
    m.paintCapsule(-3.9, lyOff + 2.6, 10.6, 3.7, lyOff + 2.8, 5.2, 1.05, 1.05, mat('leather'), partId.strap, true);
    m.paintCapsule(-3.7, lyOff - 2.4, 10.2, 3.3, lyOff - 2.8, 5.2, 1.05, 1.05, mat('leather'), partId.strap, true);
    m.paintEllipsoid(0.2, lyOff + 3.8, 8.0, 1.15, 0.9, 1.15, mat('metal'), partId.stud, true);
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: runMode ? 5.5 : 5.0 });
  }

  function buildPack(makeCanvas) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(11, 10, 12, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId } = SPEC;
    m.paintEllipsoid(0, 0, 5.2, 4.3, 3.4, 4.2, mat('leather'), partId.pack, true);
    m.paintCapsule(-3.2, -1.4, 7.4, 3.2, -1.4, 7.4, 1.05, 1.05, mat('bone'), partId.packTrim, true);
    m.paintEllipsoid(0, -2.8, 7.5, 1.3, 1.1, 1.1, mat('bone'), partId.packTrim, true);
    m.paintEllipsoid(-1.5, -0.6, 9.2, 1.35, 1.25, 1.2, mat('gold'), partId.packGold, true);
    m.paintEllipsoid( 1.6, -0.2, 9.0, 1.25, 1.15, 1.15, mat('gold'), partId.packGold, true);
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 4.5 });
  }

  function buildHead(makeCanvas) {
    const W = 13, D = 17, H = 13;
    const m = new VoxelModel(W, D, H, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId, keyToId } = SPEC;
    const hz = 5.6, hy = 0.0;
    m.paintEllipsoid(0, hy, hz, 5.1, 4.9, 4.8, mat('skin'), partId.head, true);
    m.paintCapsule(0, hy + 3.8, hz, 0, hy + 6.9, hz - 1.0, 1.55, 1.05, mat('skin'), partId.nose, true);
    m.fillAABB(-2.8, 2.8, hy + 3.8, hy + 5.6, hz - 3.5, hz - 2.0, (x, y, z, X, Y) => {
      if (Math.abs(X) <= 2.3 && Y >= hy + 4.1 && Y <= hy + 5.0 && z >= Math.round(hz - 3.1) && z <= Math.round(hz - 2.4)) {
        m.setRaw(x, y, z, keyToId.eyeDark, partId.mouth);
      }
    });
    const hw = W * 0.5 - 0.5, hd = D * 0.5 - 0.5;
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
      const tx = Math.round(hw + s * 1.8), ty = Math.round(hd + hy + 4.8), tz = Math.round(hz - 2.4);
      m.setRaw(tx, ty, tz, keyToId.boneHi, partId.mouth);
      m.setRaw(tx, ty, tz - 1, keyToId.boneMid, partId.mouth);
    }
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);

    const eyeZ = Math.round(hz + 1.8);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
      const ex = Math.round(hw + s * 2.6);
      let sy = -1;
      for (let y = D - 2; y >= 1; y--) {
        if (m.getPartId(ex, y, eyeZ) === partId.head) { sy = y; break; }
      }
      if (sy < 0) sy = Math.round(hd + hy + 4.4);
      for (const dx of [0, s]) {
        for (const dz of [0, 1]) {
          const kId = (dz === 1 && dx === 0) ? keyToId.eyeGlint : (dx === 0 ? keyToId.eyeDark : keyToId.eyeIris);
          m.setRaw(ex + dx, sy, eyeZ + dz, kId, partId.eye);
        }
      }
    }
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 5.2 });
  }

  function buildEar(makeCanvas, side) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(12, 8, 8, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId } = SPEC;
    const rx = -side * EAR_PIVOT.x, ry = EAR_PIVOT.y, rz = 2.4;
    m.paintCapsule(rx, ry, rz, rx + side * 4.4, ry - 1.6, rz + 1.1, 2.0, 1.55, mat('skin'), partId.ear, true);
    m.paintCapsule(rx + side * 4.4, ry - 1.6, rz + 1.1, rx + side * 8.8, ry - 4.0, rz + 2.6, 1.55, 1.22, mat('skin'), partId.ear, true);
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 3.5 });
  }

  function buildArm(makeCanvas, side) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(10, 10, 8, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId } = SPEC;
    m.paintCapsule(0, 0, 5.0, side * 2.6, 2.4, 3.6, 1.45, 1.35, mat('skin'), partId.arm, true);
    m.paintCapsule(side * 2.6, 2.4, 3.6, side * 3.4, 5.0, 4.4, 1.35, 1.25, mat('skin'), partId.arm, true);
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 2.5 });
  }

  function buildClub(makeCanvas) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(10, 12, 18, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId } = SPEC;
    const c0 = [0, -CLUB_PIVOT_Y, 2.0];
    const c1 = [1.8, 3.4 - CLUB_PIVOT_Y, 14.4];
    m.paintCapsule(c0[0], c0[1], c0[2], c1[0], c1[1], c1[2], 1.25, 2.08, mat('wood'), partId.weapon, true);
    const mx = lerp(c0[0], c1[0], 0.72), my = lerp(c0[1], c1[1], 0.72), mz = lerp(c0[2], c1[2], 0.72);
    m.paintEllipsoid(mx, my, mz, 2.1, 2.1, 1.35, mat('metal'), partId.stud, true);
    const tx = lerp(c0[0], c1[0], 0.88), ty = lerp(c0[1], c1[1], 0.88), tz = lerp(c0[2], c1[2], 0.88);
    for (const st of [[2.1,0,0.4],[-2.1,0,0.4],[0,2.1,0.4],[0,-2.1,0.4]]) {
      m.paintCapsule(tx, ty, tz, tx + st[0], ty + st[1], tz + st[2], 1.05, 0.85, mat('metal'), partId.stud, true);
    }
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 3.0 });
  }

  const GoblinDef = {
    id: 'goblin_scout',
    name: '苔绿哥布林',
    tag: '双足敏捷碎步',
    summary: 'v3 锐利骨肉连贯版：腿身紧密咬合 + 紧致高频碎步 + 滞后金币包袱 + 摆臂甩棒 + 警觉快转头抖耳',
    defaultHabitat: 'forest',
    footRadius: 7.2,
    ecoWeight: 0.78,
    useWaterFoam: true,
    spec: SPEC,
    skins: SKINS,
    blocks: ['双态微蹲/前倾躯干', '紧致连贯 2 足 IK', '滞后颠簸金币包袱', '摆臂甩木棒', '警觉快转头抖耳'],
    soulHabit: '原地微蹲警觉快转头抖耳掂棒 → 压低身子紧致高频小碎步窜跑扬尘',
    defaultParams: { gaitFreq: 1.0, tailFlex: 1.0, springiness: 1.0, speed: 1.0, scaleMul: 1.0 },

    createInstance(makeCanvas, opts = {}) {
      const parts = {
        legThigh:  buildLegSeg(makeCanvas, 1.78, 1.50, 3.8),
        legShin:   buildLegSeg(makeCanvas, 1.48, 1.38, 3.6),
        boot:      buildBoot(makeCanvas),
        torso:     buildTorso(makeCanvas, false),
        torsoRun:  buildTorso(makeCanvas, true),
        pack:      buildPack(makeCanvas),
        head:      buildHead(makeCanvas),
        earL:      buildEar(makeCanvas, -1),
        earR:      buildEar(makeCanvas,  1),
        armL:      buildArm(makeCanvas, -1),
        armR:      buildArm(makeCanvas,  1),
        club:      buildClub(makeCanvas)
      };

      // 2 足屈膝窜跑 IK（hipZ=6.8 深嵌腰衣裙摆，bendDir=1 膝盖向前弯）
      const gait = new MultiLegGaitSolver([
        { id: 'L', hipX: -2.2, hipY: -0.2, hipZ: 6.8, restX: -2.4, restY: 0.3, group: 0, L1: 3.8, L2: 4.0, bendDir: 1 },
        { id: 'R', hipX:  2.2, hipY: -0.2, hipZ: 6.8, restX:  2.4, restY: 0.3, group: 1, L1: 3.8, L2: 4.0, bendDir: 1 }
      ], { mode: 'biped', stepThreshold: 1.9, stepDuration: 0.115, stepHeight: 1.8, strideAnticipation: 0.44 });

      const headLookSpring = new SpringDamper1D(0, 160, 8.2);
      const earLSpring     = new SpringDamper1D(0, 210, 6.4);
      const earRSpring     = new SpringDamper1D(0, 210, 6.4);
      const packBounce     = new SpringDamper1D(0, 140, 5.8);
      const packSway       = new SpringDamper1D(0, 110, 6.0);
      const clubSpring     = new SpringDamper1D(0, 115, 5.8);
      const armSpring      = new SpringDamper1D(0, 150, 7.2);
      const bodyBounce     = new SpringDamper1D(0, 200, 8.4);

      const state = {
        x: opts.homeX ?? 0, y: opts.homeY ?? 0, z: 0,
        vx: 0, vy: 0, yaw: opts.homeYaw ?? 0,
        sprintBlend: 0, stepPhase: 0,
        lookTarget: 0, lookTimer: 0.7 + ((opts.seed || 0) % 1.0),
        habitTimer: opts.seed || 0, stepSign: 1, dustCd: 0
      };
      const sockets = new SocketTracker();
      let curSkin = SKINS[0];

      const instance = {
        def: GoblinDef,
        cachePrefix: `goblin_v3e_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
        parts, partListAssets: Object.values(parts), state, sockets, footContacts: [], scaleMul: 1.0,
        get curSkin() { return curSkin; },

        applySkin(idx) {
          curSkin = SKINS[((idx % SKINS.length) + SKINS.length) % SKINS.length];
          const tbl = SPEC.buildRgbaTable(curSkin);
          for (const a of instance.partListAssets) recolorAsset(a, tbl, SPEC.glowIdSet);
          return curSkin;
        },

        reset(hx, hy, hyaw) {
          state.x = hx; state.y = hy; state.yaw = hyaw; state.z = 0;
          state.vx = 0; state.vy = 0; state.sprintBlend = 0;
          gait.reset(hx, hy, hyaw);
        },

        update(dt, time, params, habitat, vfx, eco) {
          instance.scaleMul = params.scaleMul ?? 1.0;
          const sMul = instance.scaleMul;
          const sp = params.springiness ?? 1.0;
          const flex = params.tailFlex ?? 1.0;
          const gFreq = params.gaitFreq ?? 1.0;
          state.habitTimer += dt;

          Propulsion.multiLegWalk(state, dt, time, { ...params, speed: (params.speed ?? 1.0) * 1.25 }, {
            ...eco, walkStyle: 'scurry', baseLift: 0
          });
          state.z = 0;

          const spdNow = Math.hypot(state.vx, state.vy);
          const targetSprint = clamp((spdNow - 2.4) / 7.0, 0, 1);
          state.sprintBlend = lerp(state.sprintBlend, targetSprint, expSmooth(dt, 11.5));
          state.stepPhase += dt * (4.0 + state.sprintBlend * 15.0) * gFreq;

          const gaitRes = gait.update(dt, state.x, state.y, 0, state.yaw, state.vx, state.vy, {
            mode: 'biped',
            scaleMul: sMul,
            stepFreq: gFreq * 1.65,
            stepHeight: 1.8 * sMul * (0.75 + flex * 0.25),
            stepThreshold: 1.9 * sMul
          });
          state.tiltX = lerp(state.tiltX || 0, gaitRes.tiltX * 0.65 * sMul, expSmooth(dt, 14));
          state.tiltY = lerp(state.tiltY || 0, gaitRes.tiltY * 0.65 * sMul, expSmooth(dt, 14));

          // 温和钳位双脚相对髋部的极限偏移，保护急转不脱臼
          const maxFootOffset = 3.6 * sMul;
          for (const leg of gait.legs) {
            const [idealX, idealY] = localToWorldXY(state.x, state.y, state.yaw, leg.restX * sMul, leg.restY * sMul);
            const dx = leg.footX - idealX, dy = leg.footY - idealY;
            const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
            if (d > maxFootOffset) {
              leg.footX = idealX + (dx / d) * maxFootOffset;
              leg.footY = idealY + (dy / d) * maxFootOffset;
            }
          }

          if (gaitRes.landed.length > 0) {
            state.stepSign *= -1;
            bodyBounce.impulse(9.5 * sp);
            packBounce.impulse(-16.0 * sp);
            packSway.impulse(state.stepSign * 9.0 * sp);
            clubSpring.impulse(state.stepSign * 5.8 * sp);
            earLSpring.impulse(-7.0 * sp);
            earRSpring.impulse(7.0 * sp);
            for (const l of gaitRes.landed) {
              EnvFeedback.emitFootstep(vfx, habitat, l.footX, l.footY, 0.78 * sMul);
            }
          }

          if (state.sprintBlend > 0.5) {
            state.dustCd -= dt;
            if (state.dustCd <= 0) {
              state.dustCd = 0.09;
              EnvFeedback.emitFootstep(vfx, habitat, state.x, state.y, 0.45 * sMul);
            }
          }

          instance.footContacts = gait.legs.map(l => ({ x: l.footX, y: l.footY, z: l.footZ, r: 2.1 * sMul }));

          const moving = state.sprintBlend > 0.35;
          state.lookTimer -= dt;
          if (state.lookTimer <= 0) {
            state.lookTimer = moving ? 0.38 : (0.55 + Math.random() * 1.05);
            if (!moving) {
              const r = Math.random();
              state.lookTarget = r < 0.2 ? 0 : (r < 0.6 ? -0.52 - Math.random() * 0.22 : 0.52 + Math.random() * 0.22);
              if (state.lookTarget < -0.1) earLSpring.impulse(-14.0 * sp);
              else if (state.lookTarget > 0.1) earRSpring.impulse(14.0 * sp);
              clubSpring.impulse((Math.random() - 0.5) * 7.5 * sp);
            } else {
              state.lookTarget = 0;
            }
          }

          headLookSpring.update(dt, moving ? Math.sin(state.stepPhase * 0.5) * 0.14 : state.lookTarget, 160 * sp, 8.2);
          earLSpring.update(dt, moving ? -0.40 + Math.sin(state.stepPhase) * 0.12 : Math.sin(time * 6.5) * 0.16, 210 * sp, 6.4);
          earRSpring.update(dt, moving ?  0.40 - Math.sin(state.stepPhase) * 0.12 : -Math.sin(time * 5.8 + 1.1) * 0.16, 210 * sp, 6.4);

          bodyBounce.update(dt, 0, 200 * sp, 8.4);
          packBounce.update(dt, moving ? -0.4 : 0, 140 * sp, 5.8);
          packSway.update(dt, moving ? Math.sin(state.stepPhase) * 0.22 : Math.sin(time * 2.4) * 0.05, 110 * sp, 6.0);

          const armTarget = moving ? Math.sin(state.stepPhase) * 0.62 : Math.sin(time * 4.2) * 0.20;
          armSpring.update(dt, armTarget, 150 * sp, 7.2);

          const clubTarget = moving ? -0.52 + Math.sin(state.stepPhase - 0.5) * 0.30 : 0.16 + Math.sin(time * 4.8) * 0.26;
          clubSpring.update(dt, clubTarget, 115 * sp, 5.8);
        },

        buildPartList() {
          const list = [];
          const sMul = instance.scaleMul;
          const isRun = state.sprintBlend > 0.38;
          const yw = state.yaw;
          const hYaw = yw + clamp(headLookSpring.x, -0.78, 0.78);

          // 压实垂直弹跳范围 [-0.3, +1.0]，确保腰臀永远严密咬合大腿根部！
          const crouchBreath = Math.sin(state.habitTimer * 3.6) * 0.45 * (1 - state.sprintBlend * 0.5);
          const runBob = Math.abs(Math.sin(state.stepPhase)) * 0.72 * state.sprintBlend;
          const bob = clamp(bodyBounce.x * 0.06 + crouchBreath + runBob, -0.3, 1.05) * sMul;
          const packBob = clamp(packBounce.x * 0.12, -1.5, 2.0) * sMul;
          const packRo = yw + clamp(packSway.x, -0.36, 0.36);

          // 1. 2 条真屈膝 IK 双腿（大腿根深嵌腰衣，膝小腿连贯衔接，皮靴稳踩落点）
          for (let i = 0; i < gait.legs.length; i++) {
            const l = gait.legs[i];
            const hx = l.hipWX + (state.tiltX || 0);
            const hy = l.hipWY + (state.tiltY || 0);
            const hz = l.hipWZ + bob;
            const segs = [
              { k: 'legThigh', x: lerp(hx, l.kneeX, 0.25), y: lerp(hy, l.kneeY, 0.25), z: lerp(hz, l.kneeZ, 0.25), b: -0.32, sym: 90 },
              { k: 'legShin',  x: lerp(l.kneeX, l.footX, 0.45), y: lerp(l.kneeY, l.footY, 0.45), z: lerp(l.kneeZ, l.footZ + 1.4 * sMul, 0.45), b: -0.26, sym: 90 },
              { k: 'boot',     x: l.footX, y: l.footY, z: l.footZ, b: -0.20, sym: 0 }
            ];
            for (const pt of segs) {
              const item = {
                partKey: pt.k, asset: parts[pt.k],
                x: pt.x, y: pt.y,
                lift: Math.max(0, pt.z - (pt.k === 'boot' ? 0 : 1.7 * sMul)),
                ro: yw, bias: pt.b
              };
              if (pt.sym) item.symDeg = pt.sym;
              list.push(item);
            }
          }

          // 2. 躯干（叠加二足左右窜跑摇摆 + 深度嵌合大腿根）
          const tId = isRun ? 'torsoRun' : 'torso';
          const [tx, ty] = localToWorldXY(state.x + (state.tiltX || 0), state.y + (state.tiltY || 0), yw, 0, 0.4 * sMul);
          list.push({
            partKey: tId, asset: parts[tId],
            x: tx, y: ty, lift: 4.4 * sMul + bob, ro: yw, bias: 0.15
          });

          // 3. 背后鼓囊皮包袱
          const [pkX, pkY] = localToWorldXY(tx, ty, yw, 0, (isRun ? -5.8 : -6.2) * sMul);
          list.push({
            partKey: 'pack', asset: parts.pack,
            x: pkX, y: pkY, lift: 6.2 * sMul + bob + packBob, ro: packRo, bias: -0.25
          });

          // 4. 头部 + 两侧大尖耳
          const [hx, hy] = localToWorldXY(tx, ty, yw, 0, (isRun ? 2.8 : 1.2) * sMul);
          const headZ = (isRun ? 13.8 : 14.6) * sMul + bob * 1.05;
          list.push({
            partKey: 'head', asset: parts.head,
            x: hx, y: hy, lift: headZ, ro: hYaw, bias: 0.4
          });

          for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
            const [eShX, eShY] = localToWorldXY(hx, hy, hYaw, s * 4.5 * sMul, 0.2 * sMul);
            const eYaw = hYaw + clamp(s < 0 ? earLSpring.x : earRSpring.x, -0.64, 0.64);
            const [eWx, eWy] = localToWorldXY(eShX, eShY, eYaw, s * EAR_PIVOT.x * sMul, -EAR_PIVOT.y * sMul);
            const eKey = s < 0 ? 'earL' : 'earR';
            list.push({
              partKey: eKey, asset: parts[eKey],
              x: eWx, y: eWy, lift: headZ + 3.2 * sMul, ro: eYaw, bias: 0.25
            });
          }

          // 5. 左右臂 + 锁枢轴铁钉木棒
          const shY = (isRun ? 1.6 : 0.2) * sMul;
          const shZ = 9.4 * sMul + bob;
          const [lShX, lShY] = localToWorldXY(tx, ty, yw, -4.4 * sMul, shY);
          const [rShX, rShY] = localToWorldXY(tx, ty, yw,  4.4 * sMul, shY);
          const lArmY = yw + armSpring.x;
          const rArmY = yw - armSpring.x * 0.85;
          const armLBob = Math.max(0, Math.sin(state.stepPhase)) * 1.1 * state.sprintBlend * sMul;
          const armRBob = Math.max(0, -Math.sin(state.stepPhase)) * 1.1 * state.sprintBlend * sMul
                        + Math.sin(state.habitTimer * 4.8) * 0.6 * (1 - state.sprintBlend) * sMul;

          list.push({ partKey: 'armL', asset: parts.armL, x: lShX, y: lShY, lift: shZ + armLBob, ro: lArmY, bias: 0.2 });
          list.push({ partKey: 'armR', asset: parts.armR, x: rShX, y: rShY, lift: shZ + armRBob, ro: rArmY, bias: 0.2 });

          const [rHdX, rHdY] = localToWorldXY(rShX, rShY, rArmY, 3.4 * sMul, 5.0 * sMul);
          const cYaw = rArmY + clubSpring.x;
          const [cWx, cWy] = localToWorldXY(rHdX, rHdY, cYaw, 0, CLUB_PIVOT_Y * sMul);
          list.push({
            partKey: 'club', asset: parts.club,
            x: cWx, y: cWy, lift: shZ + 1.8 * sMul + armRBob, ro: cYaw, bias: 0.35
          });

          return list;
        },

        exportManifest(params) {
          return {
            id: GoblinDef.id, name: GoblinDef.name, outlineMode: 'actorUnified',
            skin: curSkin.id, footRadius: GoblinDef.footRadius, headZ: 26, centerZ: 13,
            blocks: GoblinDef.blocks, params: { ...params },
            parts: Object.keys(parts).map(k => ({ id: k, w: parts[k].w, d: parts[k].d, h: parts[k].h }))
          };
        }
      };

      instance.reset(state.x, state.y, state.yaw);
      return instance;
    }
  };

  PW.Creatures.push(GoblinDef);
})(window.PixelWorkshop);


===== FILE: ./references/slime.js =====
'use strict';

window.PixelWorkshop = window.PixelWorkshop || {};
window.PixelWorkshop.Creatures = window.PixelWorkshop.Creatures || [];

(function (PW) {
  const { TAU, clamp, lerp, smoothstep, smin, expSmooth, wrapAngle, localToWorldXY } = PW.Math;
  const { createPaletteSpec, VoxelModel, recolorAsset } = PW.Voxel;
  const { SpringDamper1D, SpringDamper3D, SocketTracker } = PW.Solvers;
  const { EnvFeedback } = PW.Behaviors;

  // =========================================================================
  // 弹性史莱姆（v3 标杆版 · 腹中随机食物彩蛋，+Y 正前）
  //   1. 去掉外壳旁飘浮气泡，保持极度干净圆润的水滴果冻轮廓
  //   2. 5 档无损体积守恒体素壳体（28×28×20 基准），半透绘制（alpha≈0.68）清晰透出腹中物
  //   3. 腹中内置 8 种高辨识度立体体素食物（红苹果 / 紫葡萄 / 弯香蕉 / 红瓢虫 /
  //      小橘鱼 / 斑点蘑菇 / 肉骨头 / 奶酪块），由二阶弹簧锁死在腹腔中央滞后晃动，
  //      工坊演示每隔 3.6 秒随机切换一种，游戏内可锁定单次随机结果
  //   4. 独立双层厚豆豆双眼（eyeGlint 绝不进 glowKeys）+ 皇冠金专属四面红宝石小金冠
  //   5. 真实 4 阶段蓄力弹性蹦跳巡游（蓄力压扁 -> 拉伸弹射 -> 抛物线滞空 -> 砸地回弹）
  // =========================================================================
  const SPEC = createPaletteSpec({
    pal: {
      bodyDark:       '#165e96',
      bodyMid:        '#2ea4e8',
      bodyHi:         '#8ce2ff',
      emissiveMid:    '#42d4f5',
      emissiveHot:    '#9cf2fc',
      emissiveCore:   '#e8feff',
      eyeDark:        '#111827',
      eyeGlint:       '#ffffff',
      crownDark:      '#8c5612',
      crownMid:       '#e0a422',
      crownHi:        '#ffe46b',
      rubyMid:        '#cc1e3a',
      rubyHi:         '#ff6680',
      foodRedDark:    '#9e1726',
      foodRedMid:     '#e62937',
      foodRedHi:      '#ff707a',
      foodGreenDark:  '#1f7a32',
      foodGreenMid:   '#3cd048',
      foodGreenHi:    '#96f578',
      foodYellowDark: '#b87d14',
      foodYellowMid:  '#f5cc22',
      foodYellowHi:   '#fff28c',
      foodOrangeDark: '#b54612',
      foodOrangeMid:  '#ff7b24',
      foodOrangeHi:   '#ffc46b',
      foodPurpleDark: '#4f1c8c',
      foodPurpleMid:  '#8c3be8',
      foodPurpleHi:   '#c88cff',
      foodBrownDark:  '#4a2a14',
      foodBrownMid:   '#7d4d28',
      foodCreamDark:  '#bfa988',
      foodCreamMid:   '#f2e5c9'
    },
    glowKeys: ['emissiveMid', 'emissiveHot', 'emissiveCore'],
    parts: ['body', 'core', 'eye', 'crown', 'ruby', 'foodMain', 'foodLeaf', 'foodDetail'],
    noCrease: [
      ['eye', 'body'], ['eye', 'core'],
      ['core', 'body'], ['crown', 'ruby'],
      ['foodMain', 'foodLeaf'], ['foodMain', 'foodDetail'], ['foodLeaf', 'foodDetail']
    ],
    materials: {
      body:       ['bodyDark',       'bodyMid',       'bodyHi'],
      crown:      ['crownDark',      'crownMid',      'crownHi'],
      foodRed:    ['foodRedDark',    'foodRedMid',    'foodRedHi'],
      foodGreen:  ['foodGreenDark',  'foodGreenMid',  'foodGreenHi'],
      foodYellow: ['foodYellowDark', 'foodYellowMid', 'foodYellowHi'],
      foodOrange: ['foodOrangeDark', 'foodOrangeMid', 'foodOrangeHi'],
      foodPurple: ['foodPurpleDark', 'foodPurpleMid', 'foodPurpleHi'],
      foodCream:  ['foodCreamDark',  'foodCreamMid',  'eyeGlint']
    },
    protectedParts: ['eye', 'ruby', 'foodDetail']
  });

  const SKINS = [
    {
      id: 'aqua', name: '水蓝（经典）', dot: '#2ea4e8', outline: '#0b1826',
      hasCrown: false,
      pal: {},
      fx: { dropMid: '#2ea4e8', dropHi: '#c4f2ff', core: '#e8feff' }
    },
    {
      id: 'acid', name: '毒绿（酸液）', dot: '#4ad638', outline: '#0a1f10',
      hasCrown: false,
      pal: {
        bodyDark:    '#165c24', bodyMid:     '#34b83d', bodyHi:       '#8ef054',
        emissiveMid: '#58e838', emissiveHot: '#b6ff47', emissiveCore: '#f0ffd4'
      },
      fx: { dropMid: '#48d63e', dropHi: '#c2f768', core: '#f0ffd4' }
    },
    {
      id: 'lava', name: '熔岩红（火胶）', dot: '#f04e24', outline: '#1e0b0c',
      hasCrown: false,
      pal: {
        bodyDark:    '#5c1214', bodyMid:     '#b8261e', bodyHi:       '#f06428',
        emissiveMid: '#ff5218', emissiveHot: '#ffad26', emissiveCore: '#fff5b8'
      },
      fx: { dropMid: '#eb461c', dropHi: '#ffad26', core: '#fff5b8' }
    },
    {
      id: 'crown', name: '皇冠金（稀有）', dot: '#f2b82e', outline: '#1f1508',
      hasCrown: true,
      pal: {
        bodyDark:    '#7d4b0f', bodyMid:     '#d99b1e', bodyHi:       '#fae06b',
        emissiveMid: '#f5b828', emissiveHot: '#ffe87a', emissiveCore: '#fffce8'
      },
      fx: { dropMid: '#e8ad24', dropHi: '#fff09c', core: '#fffce8' }
    }
  ];

  // 5 档无损体积守恒形变壳体（零非整数 scaleX/scaleY 拉伸模糊）
  const MORPHS = [
    { key: 'bodySquash2',  sXY: 1.24, sZ: 0.68, w: 32, d: 32, h: 15 },
    { key: 'bodySquash1',  sXY: 1.11, sZ: 0.84, w: 30, d: 30, h: 18 },
    { key: 'bodyNormal',   sXY: 1.00, sZ: 1.00, w: 28, d: 28, h: 20 },
    { key: 'bodyStretch1', sXY: 0.91, sZ: 1.15, w: 26, d: 26, h: 22 },
    { key: 'bodyStretch2', sXY: 0.83, sZ: 1.32, w: 24, d: 24, h: 25 }
  ];

  // 8 种腹中随机食物目录
  const FOODS = [
    { key: 'food_apple',    name: '红苹果' },
    { key: 'food_berry',    name: '紫葡萄' },
    { key: 'food_banana',   name: '弯香蕉' },
    { key: 'food_beetle',   name: '红瓢虫' },
    { key: 'food_fish',     name: '小橘鱼' },
    { key: 'food_mushroom', name: '斑点蘑菇' },
    { key: 'food_bone',     name: '肉骨头' },
    { key: 'food_cheese',   name: '奶酪块' }
  ];

  // 1. 平滑穹顶果冻躯壳（饱满贴地裙摆 + 圆润半球 + 微尖水滴顶 + 顶弧透亮窗）
  function buildSlimeBodyMorph(makeCanvas, mf) {
    const { sXY, sZ, w: W, d: D, h: H } = mf;
    const m = new VoxelModel(W, D, H, SPEC);
    const { partId, keyToId } = SPEC;
    const pBody = partId.body;
    const pCore = partId.core;
    const kDark = keyToId.bodyDark;
    const kMid  = keyToId.bodyMid;
    const kHi   = keyToId.bodyHi;
    const kHot  = keyToId.emissiveHot;
    const kEm   = keyToId.emissiveMid;

    const rMax = 12.6 * sXY;
    const zMax = Math.min(H - 1, Math.ceil(18.5 * sZ));

    m.fillAABB(-rMax, rMax, -rMax, rMax, 0, zMax, (x, y, z, X, Y) => {
      const uX = X / sXY;
      const uY = Y / sXY;
      const uZ = z / sZ;
      const dDome  = (Math.hypot(uX / 11.1, uY / 11.1, (uZ - 1.8) / 13.4) - 1.0) * 7.8;
      const dSkirt = (Math.hypot(uX / 11.9, uY / 11.9, (uZ - 0.8) / 4.8) - 1.0) * 4.8;
      const dPeak  = (Math.hypot(uX / 4.1, (uY + 0.3) / 4.1, (uZ - 13.4) / 3.7) - 1.0) * 4.0;
      const dAll   = smin(smin(dDome, dSkirt, 2.3), dPeak, 2.7);
      if (dAll <= 0) {
        if (uZ >= 14.0 && dAll > -1.3) {
          const topR2 = uX * uX + (uY + 0.3) * (uY + 0.3);
          if (topR2 <= 10.5) {
            m.setRaw(x, y, z, topR2 <= 2.9 ? kHot : kEm, pCore);
            return;
          }
        }
        m.setRaw(x, y, z, kMid, pBody);
      }
    });

    m.despeckle();

    // 平滑穹顶果冻明暗分级（避免台阶噪斑，呈现圆润胶质感）
    let minZ = H, maxZ = -1;
    const wd = W * D;
    for (let z = 0; z < H; z++) {
      const zOff = z * wd;
      for (let i = zOff, end = zOff + wd; i < end; i++) {
        if (m.parts[i] === pBody) {
          if (z < minZ) minZ = z;
          if (z > maxZ) maxZ = z;
        }
      }
    }
    const span = Math.max(1, maxZ - minZ);
    const snap = m.grid.slice();
    for (let z = 0; z < H; z++) {
      const zOff = z * wd;
      const localZ = (z - minZ) / span;
      for (let y = 0; y < D; y++) {
        const row = zOff + y * W;
        for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {
          const i = row + x;
          if (m.parts[i] !== pBody) continue;
          const topEmpty = z + 1 >= H || !snap[i + wd];
          const botEmpty = z > 0 && !snap[i - wd];
          if (botEmpty || z <= 1 || localZ < 0.16) m.grid[i] = kDark;
          else if (z >= maxZ - 2 || (topEmpty && localZ >= 0.78)) m.grid[i] = kHi;
          else m.grid[i] = kMid;
        }
      }
    }
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, {
      footRadius: 11.4 * sXY,
      headZ: Math.round(17.5 * sZ),
      centerZ: Math.round(9 * sZ)
    });
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // 2. 8 种腹中随机食物体素建模（全部控制在 12×12×9 内，360° 严丝合缝藏于腹中）
  // =========================================================================
  function buildFoodApple(makeCanvas) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(12, 12, 9, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId, keyToId } = SPEC;
    // 饱满红苹果果身（顶部微凹）
    m.fillAABB(-4.2, 4.2, -4.2, 4.2, 1, 6, (x, y, z, X, Y) => {
      const r = Math.hypot(X, Y);
      const dent = z >= 5 && r < 1.2 ? 0.55 : 0;
      const d = Math.hypot(X / 3.4, Y / 3.4, (z - 3.3 + dent) / 2.7);
      if (d <= 1.0) m.setRaw(x, y, z, mat('foodRed'), partId.foodMain);
    });
    // 棕色果柄 + 翠绿小嫩叶
    m.paintCapsule(0, 0, 5.4, 0.3, 0, 7.6, 0.75, 0.65, keyToId.foodBrownMid, partId.foodDetail, true);
    m.paintEllipsoid(1.9, 0.3, 7.0, 1.8, 1.1, 0.85, mat('foodGreen'), partId.foodLeaf, true);
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 3.6, headZ: 8, centerZ: 4 });
  }

  function buildFoodBerry(makeCanvas) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(12, 12, 9, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId, keyToId } = SPEC;
    // 串状圆润紫葡萄（上宽下尖 7 颗果粒团簇）
    const grapes = [
      [ 0.0,  0.0, 1.8, 1.55],
      [-1.5,  0.9, 3.4, 1.55],
      [ 1.5,  0.9, 3.4, 1.55],
      [ 0.0, -1.4, 3.4, 1.55],
      [-1.7, -0.6, 5.1, 1.55],
      [ 1.7, -0.6, 5.1, 1.55],
      [ 0.0,  1.4, 5.1, 1.55]
    ];
    for (const [gx, gy, gz, r] of grapes) {
      m.paintEllipsoid(gx, gy, gz, r, r, r, mat('foodPurple'), partId.foodMain, true);
    }
    m.paintCapsule(0, 0, 6.2, 0, 0, 7.8, 0.72, 0.62, keyToId.foodBrownMid, partId.foodDetail, true);
    m.paintEllipsoid(1.6, 0.4, 7.1, 1.6, 1.1, 0.8, mat('foodGreen'), partId.foodLeaf, true);
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 3.6, headZ: 8, centerZ: 4 });
  }

  function buildFoodBanana(makeCanvas) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(12, 12, 9, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId, keyToId } = SPEC;
    // 金黄弯月形香蕉
    const pts = [
      [-3.8, -0.6, 5.4, 0.95],
      [-2.3,  0.2, 3.4, 1.42],
      [ 0.0,  0.6, 2.5, 1.58],
      [ 2.3,  0.2, 3.4, 1.42],
      [ 3.8, -0.6, 5.4, 0.95]
    ];
    for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) {
      const a = pts[i], b = pts[i + 1];
      m.paintCapsule(a[0], a[1], a[2], b[0], b[1], b[2], a[3], b[3], mat('foodYellow'), partId.foodMain, true);
    }
    // 两端深棕果蒂
    m.paintEllipsoid(-4.1, -0.8, 5.9, 0.85, 0.85, 0.85, keyToId.foodBrownDark, partId.foodDetail, true);
    m.paintEllipsoid( 4.1, -0.8, 5.9, 0.85, 0.85, 0.85, keyToId.foodBrownDark, partId.foodDetail, true);
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 4.0, headZ: 7, centerZ: 4 });
  }

  function buildFoodBeetle(makeCanvas) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(12, 12, 9, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId, keyToId } = SPEC;
    // 红壳半球瓢虫背甲
    m.fillAABB(-4.0, 4.0, -4.5, 3.5, 1, 6, (x, y, z, X, Y) => {
      if (z < 2) return;
      const d = Math.hypot(X / 3.3, (Y + 0.5) / 3.6, (z - 2.2) / 3.2);
      if (d <= 1.0) {
        // 中缝黑线 + 两侧黑圆点
        const isSeam = Math.abs(X) <= 0.45 && z >= 4;
        const isSpot = (Math.hypot(Math.abs(X) - 1.8, Y + 0.4) <= 0.95 && z >= 3) ||
                       (Math.hypot(Math.abs(X) - 1.5, Y + 2.3) <= 0.85 && z >= 3);
        m.setRaw(x, y, z, (isSeam || isSpot) ? keyToId.eyeDark : mat('foodRed'), (isSeam || isSpot) ? partId.foodDetail : partId.foodMain);
      }
    });
    // 黑色小圆头 + 两根小触角 + 6 条短足
    m.paintEllipsoid(0, 3.2, 2.9, 1.85, 1.35, 1.35, keyToId.eyeDark, partId.foodDetail, true);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
      m.paintCapsule(s * 0.9, 4.1, 3.4, s * 1.8, 5.0, 4.4, 0.55, 0.50, keyToId.eyeDark, partId.foodDetail, true);
      for (const ly of [-2.0, -0.3, 1.4]) {
        m.paintCapsule(s * 2.6, ly, 2.0, s * 4.1, ly, 1.2, 0.58, 0.52, keyToId.eyeDark, partId.foodDetail, true);
      }
    }
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 4.0, headZ: 6, centerZ: 3 });
  }

  function buildFoodFish(makeCanvas) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(12, 12, 9, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId, keyToId } = SPEC;
    // 胖嘟嘟小橘鱼躯干（朝 +Y）
    m.paintEllipsoid(0, 0.8, 4.0, 2.2, 3.6, 2.3, mat('foodOrange'), partId.foodMain, true);
    // 扁扇尾鳍（-Y）
    m.paintCapsule(0, -2.2, 4.0, 0, -4.4, 5.8, 1.1, 0.85, mat('foodYellow'), partId.foodLeaf, true);
    m.paintCapsule(0, -2.2, 4.0, 0, -4.4, 2.2, 1.1, 0.85, mat('foodYellow'), partId.foodLeaf, true);
    // 背鳍与两侧小胸鳍
    m.paintEllipsoid(0, 0.2, 6.4, 0.75, 1.8, 1.1, mat('foodYellow'), partId.foodLeaf, true);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
      m.paintEllipsoid(s * 2.5, 0.8, 3.3, 1.1, 1.1, 0.75, mat('foodYellow'), partId.foodLeaf, true);
    }
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    // 左右 1px 清晰黑眼点
    const hw = 5.5, hd = 5.5;
    m.setRaw(Math.round(hw - 1.9), Math.round(hd + 2.8), 4, keyToId.eyeDark, partId.foodDetail);
    m.setRaw(Math.round(hw + 1.9), Math.round(hd + 2.8), 4, keyToId.eyeDark, partId.foodDetail);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 3.8, headZ: 8, centerZ: 4 });
  }

  function buildFoodMushroom(makeCanvas) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(12, 12, 9, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId, keyToId } = SPEC;
    // 奶白蘑菇柄
    m.paintCapsule(0, 0, 1.2, 0, 0, 4.2, 1.75, 1.45, mat('foodCream'), partId.foodLeaf, true);
    // 红伞盖 + 醒目白圆斑
    m.fillAABB(-4.2, 4.2, -4.2, 4.2, 4, 8, (x, y, z, X, Y) => {
      const d = Math.hypot(X / 3.8, Y / 3.8, (z - 4.0) / 3.4);
      if (d <= 1.0) {
        const spots = [
          [ 2.1,  1.6, 5.6], [-2.2,  1.3, 5.7],
          [ 0.2, -2.4, 5.5], [ 0.0,  0.3, 7.2]
        ];
        let isWhite = false;
        for (const [sx, sy, sz] of spots) {
          if (Math.hypot(X - sx, Y - sy, z - sz) <= 1.15) { isWhite = true; break; }
        }
        m.setRaw(x, y, z, isWhite ? keyToId.eyeGlint : mat('foodRed'), isWhite ? partId.foodDetail : partId.foodMain);
      }
    });
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 3.8, headZ: 8, centerZ: 4 });
  }

  function buildFoodBone(makeCanvas) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(12, 12, 9, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId } = SPEC;
    // 经典双头大肉骨头（斜摆 25° 更立体）
    m.paintCapsule(-3.0, -0.5, 3.6, 3.0, 0.5, 4.8, 1.15, 1.15, mat('foodCream'), partId.foodMain, true);
    for (const [bx, by, bz] of [
      [-3.5, -1.6, 3.5], [-3.5,  0.8, 3.7],
      [ 3.5, -0.8, 4.7], [ 3.5,  1.6, 4.9]
    ]) {
      m.paintEllipsoid(bx, by, bz, 1.35, 1.35, 1.35, mat('foodCream'), partId.foodMain, true);
    }
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 4.0, headZ: 7, centerZ: 4 });
  }

  function buildFoodCheese(makeCanvas) {
    const m = new VoxelModel(12, 12, 9, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId, keyToId } = SPEC;
    // 金黄三角奶酪块 + 表面深金孔洞
    m.fillAABB(-4.0, 4.0, -3.5, 3.5, 1, 6, (x, y, z, X, Y) => {
      // 三角楔形：从 -X 尖端向 +X 张开
      const u = (X + 3.6) / 7.2;
      if (u < 0 || u > 1) return;
      const halfY = 0.6 + u * 2.9;
      if (Math.abs(Y) <= halfY && z >= 1 && z <= 5) {
        const hole1 = Math.hypot(X - 1.2, Y - 1.2, z - 4.8) <= 1.05;
        const hole2 = Math.hypot(X - 2.6, Y + 1.0, z - 3.0) <= 1.0;
        const hole3 = Math.hypot(X + 0.6, Y + 0.5, z - 3.6) <= 0.9;
        if (hole1 || hole2 || hole3) {
          m.setRaw(x, y, z, keyToId.foodOrangeDark, partId.foodDetail);
        } else {
          m.setRaw(x, y, z, mat('foodYellow'), partId.foodMain);
        }
      }
    });
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 3.8, headZ: 6, centerZ: 3 });
  }

  // 3. 独立豆豆双眼部件（14×6×6，2×2 深色豆豆眼 + 右上 1px 亮白反点，双层厚防旋转断裂）
  function buildEyesPart(makeCanvas) {
    const W = 14, D = 6, H = 6;
    const m = new VoxelModel(W, D, H, SPEC);
    const { partId, keyToId } = SPEC;
    const hw = W * 0.5 - 0.5;
    const ey = 3;
    const ez = 2;
    for (const side of [-1, 1]) {
      const cols = side < 0
        ? [Math.round(hw - 4.0), Math.round(hw - 3.0)]
        : [Math.round(hw + 3.0), Math.round(hw + 4.0)];
      for (let ci = 0; ci < 2; ci++) {
        const x = cols[ci];
        for (let dz = 0; dz <= 1; dz++) {
          const isGlint = (dz === 1 && ci === 1);
          m.setRaw(x, ey,     ez + dz, isGlint ? keyToId.eyeGlint : keyToId.eyeDark, partId.eye);
          m.setRaw(x, ey - 1, ez + dz, keyToId.eyeDark, partId.eye);
        }
      }
    }
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 4.5, headZ: 5, centerZ: 3 });
  }

  // 4. 皇冠金专属小金冠（14×14×8，4 重对称 symDeg=90 + 四面红宝石）
  function buildMiniCrown(makeCanvas) {
    const W = 14, D = 14, H = 8;
    const m = new VoxelModel(W, D, H, SPEC);
    const { mat, partId, keyToId } = SPEC;
    const hw = W * 0.5 - 0.5, hd = D * 0.5 - 0.5;
    for (let z = 0; z < H; z++) {
      for (let y = 0; y < D; y++) {
        const Y = y - hd;
        for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {
          const X = x - hw;
          const r = Math.hypot(X, Y);
          const rOut = 3.8 + z * 0.16;
          const rIn  = 2.0 + z * 0.12;
          if (z <= 2 && r <= rOut && r >= rIn) {
            m.setRaw(x, y, z, mat('crown'), partId.crown);
            continue;
          }
          if (z >= 3 && z <= 4 && r <= rOut && r >= rIn - 0.2) {
            const ax = Math.abs(X), ay = Math.abs(Y);
            const tw = z === 3 ? 1.85 : 1.15;
            if ((ax <= tw && ay >= 2.0) || (ay <= tw && ax >= 2.0)) {
              m.setRaw(x, y, z, mat('crown'), partId.crown);
            }
          }
          if (z === 5) {
            const ax = Math.abs(X), ay = Math.abs(Y);
            if ((ax <= 0.9 && Math.abs(ay - 3.6) <= 0.9) || (ay <= 0.9 && Math.abs(ax - 3.6) <= 0.9)) {
              m.setRaw(x, y, z, keyToId.crownHi, partId.crown);
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    for (const [gx, gy] of [[0, 3.9], [0, -3.9], [3.9, 0], [-3.9, 0]]) {
      m.fillAABB(gx - 1.0, gx + 1.0, gy - 1.0, gy + 1.0, 1, 2, (x, y, z, X, Y) => {
        if (Math.hypot(X - gx, Y - gy) <= 1.0) {
          m.setRaw(x, y, z, z === 2 ? keyToId.rubyHi : keyToId.rubyMid, partId.ruby);
        }
      });
    }
    m.despeckle();
    m.applyArtShading(true);
    return m.toStripAsset(makeCanvas, { footRadius: 4.0, headZ: 7, centerZ: 3 });
  }

  const HOP_WAYPOINTS = [
    { x:  22, y: -14 },
    { x:  26, y:  14 },
    { x:   4, y:  24 },
    { x: -22, y:  16 },
    { x: -26, y: -12 },
    { x:  -2, y: -24 }
  ];

  const SlimeDef = {
    id: 'slime_jelly',
    name: '弹性史莱姆',
    tag: '弹性软体 · 腹中吞食',
    summary: 'v3 标杆版：干净无外飘气泡的 5 档无损半透果冻壳 + 腹中 8 种随机食物弹簧漂浮（每 3.6 秒轮换演示）+ 皇冠金小金冠',
    defaultHabitat: 'swamp',
    footRadius: 11.2,
    ecoWeight: 0.78,
    useWaterFoam: true,
    spec: SPEC,
    skins: SKINS,
    blocks: ['5 档无损果冻形变', '腹中 8 种随机食物', '二阶弹簧腹腔滞后', '独立双层豆豆眼', '皇冠金小金冠彩蛋'],
    soulHabit: '肚子里漂着刚吞下的水果、瓢虫、小鱼或奶酪，边 Q 弹颤动边蓄力四阶段蹦跳巡游',
    defaultParams: { gaitFreq: 1.0, tailFlex: 1.0, springiness: 1.0, speed: 1.0, scaleMul: 1.0 },

    createInstance(makeCanvas, opts = {}) {
      const parts = {};
      for (let i = 0; i < MORPHS.length; i++) {
        parts[MORPHS[i].key] = buildSlimeBodyMorph(makeCanvas, MORPHS[i]);
      }
      parts.food_apple    = buildFoodApple(makeCanvas);
      parts.food_berry    = buildFoodBerry(makeCanvas);
      parts.food_banana   = buildFoodBanana(makeCanvas);
      parts.food_beetle   = buildFoodBeetle(makeCanvas);
      parts.food_fish     = buildFoodFish(makeCanvas);
      parts.food_mushroom = buildFoodMushroom(makeCanvas);
      parts.food_bone     = buildFoodBone(makeCanvas);
      parts.food_cheese   = buildFoodCheese(makeCanvas);
      parts.eyes          = buildEyesPart(makeCanvas);
      parts.crown         = buildMiniCrown(makeCanvas);

      // 仅壳体随换色变化，腹中食物与双眼/金冠保持真实鲜明本色
      const recolorAssets = MORPHS.map(mf => parts[mf.key]);

      const squishSpring = new SpringDamper1D(0, 140, 9.0);
      const foodSpring   = new SpringDamper3D(opts.homeX ?? 0, opts.homeY ?? 0, 4.4, 145, 11.5);
      const crownSpring  = new SpringDamper3D(opts.homeX ?? 0, opts.homeY ?? 0, 15.6, 175, 12.5);

      const hx0 = opts.homeX ?? 0;
      const hy0 = opts.homeY ?? 0;
      const initFoodIdx = typeof opts.foodIdx === 'number'
        ? ((opts.foodIdx % FOODS.length) + FOODS.length) % FOODS.length
        : Math.floor(Math.random() * FOODS.length);

      const state = {
        x: hx0, y: hy0, z: 0,
        vx: 0, vy: 0, yaw: opts.homeYaw ?? 0,
        eyeYawOff: 0,
        morphIdx: 2,
        scaleXY: 1.0, scaleZ: 1.0,
        foodIdx: initFoodIdx,
        foodTimer: 0,
        foodSwitchInterval: opts.foodSwitchInterval ?? 3.6,
        foodSpin: 0,
        hopWpIdx: Math.abs(Math.floor(opts.seed || 0)) % HOP_WAYPOINTS.length,
        hopPhase: 0,
        hopSx: hx0, hopSy: hy0,
        hopTx: hx0 + HOP_WAYPOINTS[0].x,
        hopTy: hy0 + HOP_WAYPOINTS[0].y,
        hopLaunched: false,
        hopLanded: false
      };

      const sockets = new SocketTracker();
      let curSkin = SKINS[0];

      function spawnJellySplash(vfx, habitat, wx, wy, count, power, sMul) {
        if (!vfx) return;
        if (habitat && habitat.isWater) {
          vfx.addRipple(wx, wy, 6 * sMul, 20 * power * sMul, 0.58, 1.8);
          vfx.spawnSplash(wx, wy, count, power, habitat.fx);
        } else {
          vfx.addRipple(wx, wy, 5 * sMul, 18 * power * sMul, 0.46, 1.5);
          const fx = curSkin.fx || SKINS[0].fx;
          for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            const a = (i / count) * TAU + Math.random() * 0.35;
            const sp = (11 + Math.random() * 16) * power * sMul;
            const col = i % 3 === 0 ? fx.core : (i % 2 === 0 ? fx.dropHi : fx.dropMid);
            vfx.spawnParticle(
              wx + Math.cos(a) * 4 * sMul, wy + Math.sin(a) * 4 * sMul, 1.8 * sMul,
              Math.cos(a) * sp, Math.sin(a) * sp, (9 + Math.random() * 13) * power * sMul,
              0.36 + Math.random() * 0.22, 1.45 + Math.random() * 0.65,
              col, i % 3 === 0, 52
            );
          }
        }
      }

      const instance = {
        def: SlimeDef,
        cachePrefix: `slime_v3c_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 7)}`,
        parts,
        partListAssets: Object.values(parts),
        state,
        sockets,
        scaleMul: 1.0,
        get curSkin() { return curSkin; },

        applySkin(idx) {
          curSkin = SKINS[((idx % SKINS.length) + SKINS.length) % SKINS.length];
          const tbl = SPEC.buildRgbaTable(curSkin);
          for (const a of recolorAssets) recolorAsset(a, tbl, SPEC.glowIdSet);
          return curSkin;
        },

        reset(hx, hy, hyaw) {
          state.x = hx; state.y = hy; state.z = 0;
          state.vx = 0; state.vy = 0; state.yaw = hyaw;
          state.eyeYawOff = 0; state.morphIdx = 2;
          state.scaleXY = 1.0; state.scaleZ = 1.0;
          state.hopPhase = 0;
          state.hopSx = hx; state.hopSy = hy;
          const wp = HOP_WAYPOINTS[state.hopWpIdx];
          state.hopTx = wp.x; state.hopTy = wp.y;
          state.hopLaunched = false; state.hopLanded = false;
          squishSpring.x = 0; squishSpring.v = 0;
          foodSpring.reset(hx, hy, 4.4);
          crownSpring.reset(hx, hy, 15.6);
        },

        update(dt, time, params, habitat, vfx, eco) {
          instance.scaleMul = params.scaleMul ?? 1.0;
          const sMul = instance.scaleMul;
          const sp = clamp(params.springiness ?? 1.0, 0.4, 2.2);
          const gFreq = clamp(params.gaitFreq ?? 1.0, 0.4, 2.2);
          const speedMul = clamp(params.speed ?? 1.0, 0.4, 2.2);
          const patrol = eco && eco.patrol !== false;
          const maxR = (eco && eco.maxRadius) || 48;

          // 工坊预览：每隔几秒随机切换一种腹中食物（游戏内设 foodSwitchInterval = 0 即锁定单次随机结果）
          if (state.foodSwitchInterval > 0) {
            state.foodTimer += dt;
            if (state.foodTimer >= state.foodSwitchInterval) {
              state.foodTimer -= state.foodSwitchInterval;
              let nextIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * FOODS.length);
              if (nextIdx === state.foodIdx) nextIdx = (nextIdx + 1) % FOODS.length;
              state.foodIdx = nextIdx;
              foodSpring.impulse(0, 0, 14 * sp);
              squishSpring.impulse(1.6 * sp);
            }
          }
          state.foodSpin = wrapAngle(state.foodSpin + dt * 0.65);

          let targetSquish = 0;
          let targetEyeYaw = 0;

          if (patrol) {
            // 真实 4 阶段弹性蹦跳巡游：蓄力压扁 -> 拉伸弹射 -> 抛物线滞空 -> 砸地回弹
            const dur = clamp(1.20 / (gFreq * 0.6 + speedMul * 0.4), 0.58, 2.1);
            state.hopPhase += dt / dur;
            if (state.hopPhase >= 1.0) {
              state.hopPhase -= 1.0;
              state.hopSx = state.x;
              state.hopSy = state.y;
              state.hopWpIdx = (state.hopWpIdx + 1) % HOP_WAYPOINTS.length;
              const wp = HOP_WAYPOINTS[state.hopWpIdx];
              let tx = wp.x + ((eco && eco.avoidX) || 0) * 0.45;
              let ty = wp.y + ((eco && eco.avoidY) || 0) * 0.45;
              const dR = Math.hypot(tx, ty);
              if (dR > maxR) { tx *= maxR / dR; ty *= maxR / dR; }
              state.hopTx = tx;
              state.hopTy = ty;
              state.hopLaunched = false;
              state.hopLanded = false;
            }

            const p = state.hopPhase;
            const dx = state.hopTx - state.hopSx;
            const dy = state.hopTy - state.hopSy;
            const tgtYaw = Math.hypot(dx, dy) > 0.5 ? Math.atan2(-dx, dy) : state.yaw;
            state.yaw = wrapAngle(state.yaw + wrapAngle(tgtYaw - state.yaw) * expSmooth(dt, 10));

            if (p < 0.26) {
              const u = smoothstep(0, 1, p / 0.26);
              state.x = state.hopSx;
              state.y = state.hopSy;
              state.z = 0;
              state.vx = 0; state.vy = 0;
              targetSquish = u * 0.96;
            } else if (p < 0.74) {
              const u = (p - 0.26) / 0.48;
              if (!state.hopLaunched) {
                state.hopLaunched = true;
                squishSpring.impulse(-3.2 * sp);
                foodSpring.impulse(0, 0, -14 * sp);
                crownSpring.impulse(0, 0, -18 * sp);
                spawnJellySplash(vfx, habitat, state.hopSx, state.hopSy, 7, 0.76, sMul);
              }
              const nx = lerp(state.hopSx, state.hopTx, u);
              const ny = lerp(state.hopSy, state.hopTy, u);
              state.vx = (nx - state.x) / Math.max(dt, 0.001);
              state.vy = (ny - state.y) / Math.max(dt, 0.001);
              state.x = nx;
              state.y = ny;
              state.z = Math.sin(u * Math.PI) * 19.5 * clamp(0.8 + sp * 0.2, 0.8, 1.32) * sMul;
              const airVel = Math.abs(Math.cos(u * Math.PI));
              targetSquish = lerp(-0.24, -0.96, airVel);
            } else {
              const u = (p - 0.74) / 0.26;
              if (!state.hopLanded) {
                state.hopLanded = true;
                squishSpring.impulse(4.6 * sp);
                foodSpring.impulse(0, 0, -26 * sp);
                crownSpring.impulse(0, 0, -34 * sp);
                spawnJellySplash(vfx, habitat, state.hopTx, state.hopTy, 12, 1.05, sMul);
              }
              state.x = state.hopTx;
              state.y = state.hopTy;
              state.z = 0;
              state.vx = 0; state.vy = 0;
              const damp = Math.exp(-u * 4.2);
              const osc = Math.cos(u * Math.PI * 3.0);
              targetSquish = 0.96 * damp * osc;
            }
            targetEyeYaw = 0;
          } else {
            // 原地颤动：体积守恒果冻呼吸 + 偶发 Q 弹小抖动 + 好奇微张望
            const kHome = expSmooth(dt, 6.0);
            state.x = lerp(state.x, eco ? (eco.homeX ?? 0) : 0, kHome);
            state.y = lerp(state.y, eco ? (eco.homeY ?? 0) : 0, kHome);
            state.z = lerp(state.z, 0, expSmooth(dt, 10));
            state.vx *= 0.82; state.vy *= 0.82;
            const idleYaw = (eco ? (eco.homeYaw ?? 0) : 0) + Math.sin(time * 0.9) * 0.16;
            state.yaw = wrapAngle(state.yaw + wrapAngle(idleYaw - state.yaw) * expSmooth(dt, 6.5));

            const breath = Math.sin(time * 3.4 * gFreq);
            const jiggleCy = time % 4.4;
            const jiggle = jiggleCy > 3.2 && jiggleCy < 4.0
              ? Math.sin(((jiggleCy - 3.2) / 0.8) * Math.PI * 3) * Math.exp(-(jiggleCy - 3.2) * 3.8) * 0.65 * sp
              : 0;
            targetSquish = 0.42 * breath + jiggle;
            targetEyeYaw = Math.sin(time * 1.4) * 0.14;
          }

          // 二阶弹簧推进弹性形变，量化映射到 5 档无损体素壳体（0 模糊）
          const sq = clamp(
            squishSpring.update(dt, targetSquish, 130 * sp, clamp(9.5 / Math.sqrt(sp), 4.8, 13.5)),
            -1.15, 1.15
          );
          let mIdx = 2;
          if (sq >= 0.62) mIdx = 0;
          else if (sq >= 0.22) mIdx = 1;
          else if (sq <= -0.62) mIdx = 4;
          else if (sq <= -0.22) mIdx = 3;
          state.morphIdx = mIdx;

          const mf = MORPHS[mIdx];
          state.scaleXY = lerp(state.scaleXY, mf.sXY, expSmooth(dt, 18));
          state.scaleZ  = lerp(state.scaleZ,  mf.sZ,  expSmooth(dt, 18));
          state.eyeYawOff = lerp(state.eyeYawOff, targetEyeYaw, expSmooth(dt, 9));

          // 腹中食物弹簧滞后漂浮（严密锁在果冻腹腔中央）
          const foodTgtZ = state.z + (4.2 * state.scaleZ + Math.cos(time * 3.0 * gFreq) * 0.55) * sMul;
          foodSpring.update(dt, state.x, state.y, foodTgtZ, 145 * sp, clamp(11.5 / Math.sqrt(sp), 6.0, 15.0));

          const crownZ = state.z + 15.6 * state.scaleZ * sMul;
          crownSpring.update(dt, state.x, state.y, crownZ, 175 * sp, clamp(12.5 / Math.sqrt(sp), 6.5, 16.0));

          sockets.setWorld('food', foodSpring.x, foodSpring.y, foodSpring.z, state.yaw + state.foodSpin);
          sockets.setWorld('crown', crownSpring.x, crownSpring.y, crownSpring.z, state.yaw);
        },

        buildPartList(habitat) {
          const list = [];
          const sMul = instance.scaleMul;
          const mf = MORPHS[state.morphIdx] || MORPHS[2];
          const envSub = (habitat && habitat.isWater) ? EnvFeedback.calcSubmerge(state.z, 2.6 * sMul, 5.2 * sMul, true) : 0;
          // 半透果冻外壳：opacity 0.68（原先借 submerge=0.72 换算出来的同一个值），腹中食物清晰可辨；
          // submerge 只表示浸水深度（水里会染水色）。2026-11-23 渲染器把两者分开了。
          const BODY_OPACITY = 1 - 0.72 * 0.44;

          // 1. 腹中随机食物（bias=-12 严格锁在半透果冻腹腔中央，360° 零穿模、绝不外飘）
          const food = FOODS[state.foodIdx] || FOODS[0];
          const fOffX = clamp(foodSpring.x - state.x, -2.0 * mf.sXY * sMul, 2.0 * mf.sXY * sMul);
          const fOffY = clamp(foodSpring.y - state.y, -2.0 * mf.sXY * sMul, 2.0 * mf.sXY * sMul);
          const fLocZ = clamp(foodSpring.z - state.z, 1.8 * mf.sZ * sMul, 6.2 * mf.sZ * sMul);
          list.push({
            partKey: food.key, asset: parts[food.key],
            x: state.x + fOffX, y: state.y + fOffY, lift: state.z + fLocZ,
            ro: wrapAngle(state.yaw + state.foodSpin), symDeg: 360, bias: -12, submerge: 0
          });

          // 2. 半透果冻主躯壳（5 档无损体素形变，1:1 整数像素渲染）
          list.push({
            partKey: mf.key, asset: parts[mf.key],
            x: state.x, y: state.y, lift: state.z,
            ro: state.yaw, symDeg: 360, bias: 0, submerge: envSub, opacity: BODY_OPACITY
          });

          // 3. 独立正面豆豆双眼（贴合前表面 Y=8.2*sXY, Z=6.2*sZ，正面零遮挡、背面自然被果冻壳遮挡）
          const eyeYaw = wrapAngle(state.yaw + state.eyeYawOff);
          const [ex, ey] = localToWorldXY(state.x, state.y, eyeYaw, 0, 8.2 * mf.sXY * sMul);
          list.push({
            partKey: 'eyes', asset: parts.eyes,
            x: ex, y: ey, lift: state.z + 6.2 * mf.sZ * sMul,
            ro: eyeYaw, symDeg: 360, bias: 0, submerge: 0
          });

          // 4. 皇冠金稀有色系专属：四面红宝石弹簧小金冠（稳坐水滴顶峰之上）
          if (curSkin && curSkin.hasCrown) {
            const [cx, cy] = localToWorldXY(state.x, state.y, state.yaw, 0, -0.3 * mf.sXY * sMul);
            const crLocZ = clamp(crownSpring.z - state.z, 13.4 * mf.sZ * sMul, 20.5 * mf.sZ * sMul);
            list.push({
              partKey: 'crown', asset: parts.crown,
              x: cx, y: cy, lift: state.z + crLocZ,
              ro: state.yaw, symDeg: 90, bias: 12, submerge: 0
            });
          }

          return list;
        },

        exportManifest(params) {
          const food = FOODS[state.foodIdx] || FOODS[0];
          return {
            id: SlimeDef.id, name: SlimeDef.name, outlineMode: 'actorUnified',
            skin: curSkin.id, hasCrown: !!curSkin.hasCrown, morph: MORPHS[state.morphIdx].key,
            stomachFood: food.key, stomachFoodName: food.name,
            foodPool: FOODS.map(f => ({ id: f.key, name: f.name })),
            footRadius: SlimeDef.footRadius, headZ: 18, centerZ: 9,
            blocks: SlimeDef.blocks, params: { ...params },
            parts: Object.keys(parts).map(k => ({ id: k, w: parts[k].w, d: parts[k].d, h: parts[k].h }))
          };
        }
      };

      instance.reset(state.x, state.y, state.yaw);
      return instance;
    }
  };

  PW.Creatures.push(SlimeDef);
})(window.PixelWorkshop);


===== FILE: ./references/骨骼化改造交接与经验.md =====
# 像素堆叠魔物骨骼化（v3）与防糊清晰度经验指南

> 本文件同步自 `美术/教程-像素堆叠AI制作方案/骨骼化改造交接与经验.md` 并汇总了 `README.md` 中的**防糊与像素清晰度铁律**，供「像素堆叠生物工坊」逐只精制与排错时直接对照使用。
>
> **2026-11-23 渲染器换了一版**（原因和数字见 `性能与流畅度-2026-11-23.md`），下面几条的说法跟着变了，已就地改过：
> - 不再有 `actorBuf` / `actorRaw` 离屏拼合：渲染器先画所有部件外扩一圈的剪影、再按深度画本体，效果一样，生物脚本不用管（二.1）；
> - 半透明分两种：设计上的半透明写 `opacity`，浸水写 `submerge`（水里染水色、不变透明）；
> - 左右对称的部件自动只烘一半角度；
> - 换色后缓存自动作废，不用手动清（二.7）。

---

## 一、像素堆叠「发糊 / 发脏」核心排错清单（工坊必查）

1. **严禁把非自发光细节放进 `glowKeys`（头号发糊元凶）**
   - `glowKeys` 仅限真正需要霓虹/火焰辉光的 `['emissiveMid', 'emissiveHot', 'emissiveCore']`。
   - **眼睛高光（`eyeGlint`）、金属反光、金币高光绝不能放进 `glowKeys`**，否则渲染器的 1/4 降采样高斯模糊（`ebctx` + `screen` 叠加）会在眼睛和脸部糊上一层发白光晕，瞬间毁掉五官锐度。
2. **常态严禁对已烘焙精灵做非整数 `scaleX` / `scaleY` 缩放**
   - 部件精灵按 `BAKE_SCALE = 2.5` 离屏烘焙后已是定格像素图；若每帧用 `evalBreathing` 输出 `1.03`、`0.96` 这类小数去拉伸 `drawImage(sp.main, dx, dy, dw, dh)`，会产生像素列抖动、重影与发糊。
   - 呼吸感优先通过**部位微位移（`bodyBob` / `packBob` / `headLift`）**与骨骼姿态实现，仅在极短的爆发/变身瞬间临时使用弹性缩放，常态保持 `1:1` 像素无损绘制。
3. **高对比 3 档色阶 + 细分 `partId` 切出清晰结构暗缝**
   - 单材质严格用 3 档高对比色阶（暗面 / 主色 / 露边亮色），避免过多相近灰阶在低分下揉糊轮廓；
   - 盔甲（`armor`）、主鳞（`scale`）、腹甲（`belly`）、皮带（`strap`）、骨饰（`bone`）、金属（`metal`）必须分设独立 `partId`，靠 `applyArtShading` 的 Crease AO 自动切出 1 格清晰结构线；同时把 `eye:head`、`mouthCore:snout/jaw`、`chestCore:body` 加入 `noCrease` 白名单防止五官发黑。
4. **双眼与亮核必须「表面投影 + 0.6 俯视 0 遮挡」**
   - 眼睛不要埋在粗糙大块体素里，也不要被上方粗眉骨在 0.6 俯视倾角下遮挡；应在着色完成后沿表面扫描落点，精雕 `2×2` 锐利双眼（瞳孔 `eyeDark` + 虹膜 `eyeIris` + 单像素锐利高光 `eyeGlint`）。
5. **宽翼用 `paintWingQuad` 曲面弧边，替代粗糙三角堆叠**
   - 龙翼/蝠翼采用带尾缘扇贝弧（`scallop`）的 `paintWingQuad` 铺设翼膜，翼臂用 `armor`/`scale`、翼指用与龙身同色系的细 `scale` 骨、仅腕爪用 `horn`，且体素盒留足边界余量严防 `y = 0` / `y = d - 1` 被切平。
6. **切片严格二值透明 + 双子步压实**
   - 切片 `alpha` 严格 `0` 或 `255`；`BAKE_SCALE = 2.5` 配合 `yy + 1` 半步压实消除 `HS(0.8) > TILT(0.6)` 斜角百叶窗黑缝；所有普通绘制必须 `imageSmoothingEnabled = false`。

---

## 二、从 `snake`、`siren`、`goblin` 与 `feilong` 沉淀的 8 条骨骼化实战经验

1. **整只一圈 1px 外轮廓（渲染器自动做）**
   - 部件按 `ry + lift * 0.08 + bias` 深度排序；渲染器先把整只所有部件「外扩 1 美术像素的实心剪影」画出来，再按深度画本体。剪影的并集就是整只的外轮廓，内部零拼装黑缝、外圈轮廓锐利统一。
   - 旧版是每只先拼进局部画布 `actorRaw` 再整体描边（`actorBuf`），同屏多了很慢，2026-11-23 换掉了。生物脚本这边照旧只管排好 `bias`。
2. **用数学保证「零穿模」：净空预算 + 层序偏置 `bias` + Node 周期断言**
   - 被包覆的根节加微负 `bias`（如 `-0.3`），外罩件/头部加微正 `bias`（如 `+0.45`）；写完用 Node 脚本遍历动作周期检查零越界、零 NaN。
3. **偏心旋转部件必须「锁死关节枢轴」（防 1~2px 挤捏缝）**
   - 若部件真实关节位于局部 `(px, py)`，摆放世界坐标一律用 `localToWorldXY(jointX, jointY, partYaw, -px, -py)`。**翅膀翼根、大尖耳耳根、武器握柄、尾鳍/长尾尖**全都要用这一招。
4. **杜绝「浮空平移物体」，体现真实生物运动学**
   - **飞龙**：强翼下拍 → 躯干滞后 `0.72 rad` 被升力托起；巡航包含强拍爬升与展翼滑翔；多节龙颈 + 龙头独立环视，多节鞭尾行波摆动。
   - **哥布林**：原地微蹲警戒（转头张望、尖耳独立抖动、木棒轻晃）↔ 低重心前倾 `28°` 疾跑躯干 + 高频碎步 IK + 落地扬尘 + 滞后颠簸包袱。
5. **宽翼边界留白 + 材质统一 + 三段振翅（`up / mid / down`）**
   - 成年宽翼盒扩至 `30×28×26` 杜绝尾缘截断；肩肌内嵌背脊；补齐 `mid` 中间帧平滑过渡，落地增加收翼态（`tuck/rest`）。
6. **多节尾链 Z 高度单调平滑 + 颈头升降联动**
   - 尾链 Z 高度保持单调平滑（如 `[11.2, 10.8, 10.4]`）；抬头时颈部同步分摊 `headLift * 0.45` 防止头颈脱节。
7. **零重建模一键换色（`SKINS`）**
   - 总色数 `≤ 48` 色、0 重色、自亮色物理隔离；换色时只调用 `recolorAsset` 按索引重刷 `ImageData`，资产版本号自动加 1，烘焙缓存自己作废，不用手动清。
8. **量化校验闭环**
   - 每次修改生物脚本后，挂载本地工坊 `core/` 内核用 Node.js 实测全周期（建模耗时、色表数、零越界、零 NaN、状态机闭环），再进浏览器 360° 旋转实看清晰度。

---

## 三、水生游动、防穿模与柔顺动作精制经验（从 `siren`、`fairy`、`slime` 踩坑沉淀）

1. **水生生物「真游动」必须体现真实俯仰推进，严禁做成「改高度的陆地平移」**
   - **运动学还原**：人鱼/海豚类游动属于**垂直平面海豚式打水**，由腰腹发起向水平双叶尾鳍传递垂直波，并伴随**头朝下俯潜 → 平滑滑翔 → 抬头浮升 → 减速立身踩水**的完整节奏，切忌只上下平移 Y/Z 轴高度或照搬陆地爬行轨迹。
   - **高密连续倾角消除跳帧卡顿**：不要只烘 2~3 档姿态硬切（如俯潜与滑翔直接跳变）。应连续驱动实数倾角 `curPitch`（例如 `0°~108°`），颈口、肩关节、发根坐标全部由连续 `curPitch` 实时解算，并预烘 6~7 档躯干倾角（每档间距约 `15°~18°`），让 2.5D 堆叠呈现出接近 3D 的丝滑俯仰。
   - **游速与爆发节拍**：水生生物游动要敏捷利落，下打水阶段叠加向前爆发加速，上浮阶段顺势滑行，避免慢吞吞拖滞感。
2. **高速转弯时的「长发 / 飘带零穿模」铁律（禁用世界空间滞后插值）**
   - **典型踩坑**：在世界空间对头发节点做 `expSmooth` 滞后跟随，角色高速环游转弯时，发束会沿圆弧内侧弦直接切穿肩膀或背部。
   - **根治方案**：改在**机身局部坐标系**下，沿**脊柱背面外法向** `back = (-cos(pitch), sin(pitch))` 实时计算每个发束节点的位置，确保在直立（0°）、抬头浮升、水平滑行、俯潜（108°）任意倾角下，头发都严格浮在背部与肩鳍外侧净空之外；转弯飘动仅用带限幅的侧向微摆 `clamp(-yawRate * k, -max, max)` 表达，再配合合理的 `bias` 层序，从几何上彻底根治穿模。
3. **手臂/触须告别僵硬：拆分为「两段式关节 + 相位滞后随动」**
   - **典型踩坑**：整条刚性手臂按姿态档位硬切（手端瞬移数格），或仅靠肩关节整体旋转，挥动像木偶一样生硬。
   - **根治方案**：拆分为**大臂 + 小臂**两段独立资产（如 `upperArm` + `forearm`），以精确肩肘正运动学（FK）连续驱动，同时让小臂相对大臂保持约 `0.75~0.85 rad` 的**相位滞后随动（Overlapping Action）**——大臂引领、肘腕滞后柔顺舒展，彻底消除换姿跳变与僵硬感。
4. **俯冲/背视角「腿部与裙摆穿模」排查要点（以 `fairy` 为例）**
   - 飞行类人形生物向前俯冲时，躯干前倾容易在背视角把原本藏在裙内的双腿上部漏出。
   - **排查与修复**：将双腿挂点向内微收（调整局部 `ly`）、适当裁短大腿顶部使其低于裙腰上沿，并给腿部设置更明确的负 `bias`（如 `-2.2`），确保 360° 视角下裙摆都能干净遮盖。
5. **趣味细节与生态互动设计（以 `slime`、`siren` 为例）**
   - **体内可视化道具**：半透明生物可用体内高辨识度体素小道具（如多种水果/虫子/小鱼食物）替代单调气泡；工坊预览期可每隔数秒轮换展示，同时通过配置参数支持游戏内锁死单次随机结果。
   - **去武器化伴游生态**：偏灵动/亲和的水生或自然生物不强行配武器，可设计多款高辨识度小配角（如小丑鱼、章鱼、蓝唐王鱼、河豚、海马、海龟）按「入场 → 环绕嬉戏 → 游离」循环轮换，并让主体视线与伸手动作朝配角自然响应，大幅提升生命力。

---

## 四、四足生物步态与矢状面 IK 避坑经验（从 `earth3_zheng`、`sand4_karkadann` 踩坑沉淀）

1. **严禁用「髋 → 足射线平面」2D IK 解四足腿，必须用躯干矢状面 3D IK（`PW.Solvers.solveSagittalLegIK`）**
   - **典型踩坑**：旧版 `solveIK2D` 在「髋 → 足」连线的垂直平面内求膝盖位置；一旦后足踩在髋部后方，膝盖就会顺着向后向量甩到臀后，看起来像后腿折断被拖着走，且脚从髋后摆到髋前瞬间还容易发生 180° 翻转奇点。
   - **根治方案**：`core/solvers.js` 中的 `MultiLegGaitSolver` 对 4 足生物已内置 `solveSagittalLegIK`，在躯干前后矢状面内解算膝肘——**前肢肘部恒向后折（`kneeSign: -1`）**，**后肢膝盖恒向前折向腰腹（`kneeSign: +1`）**，360° 视角下始终呈现自然有力的四足 Z 字腿型。
2. **四足告别「阈值触发式」拖行步态，统一采用位移驱动循环步态**
   - **典型踩坑**：旧版 `walk4 / trot` 要等足掌偏离理想点超过 `stepThreshold` 才抬脚，加上对角/四拍互斥锁，稍快起步或转弯就会把后足硬拖到髋后 `7~11` 格，后足抬脚占空比跌到 20%~30%，视觉上像后肢瘫痪拖地。
   - **根治方案**：`MultiLegGaitSolver` 对 4 足已升级为**位移 + 转向角驱动的连续循环步态**（支持 `walk4` 四拍稳步、`trot` 微错相对角小跑、`bound` 前后足腾跃）：支撑期足掌世界坐标钉地（零滑冰）并带最大髋后滞后保护，摆动期后足干脆抬离并主动前探至腰腹下方，停步自动轻抬收回肩胯正下方。
3. **四足中性落点、分节挂点与抬脚高度规矩**
   - **后足中性站位不偏后**：后足 `restY` 应放在髋关节 `hipY` 正下方或微偏前（`restY ≈ hipY + 0.2`），不要把 `restY` 设在 `hipY` 后方 `1~2` 格，避免起步先落后半拍；
   - **分节脊柱必须绑独立锚点**：拆出独立胸肩与腰胯（如五尾红豹）时，后腿须通过 `legAnchors: { BL: pelvisAnchor, BR: pelvisAnchor }` 挂在腰胯局部坐标上，转弯屈脊时后腿不脱胯；
   - **抬脚高度可辨识**：俯视角下 `stepHeight` 建议设在 `2.4~3.6` 格（按体型缩放），低于 `2.0` 格容易显得贴地擦行。
4. **四足 Node 仿真量化红线（改完必跑）**
   - 行进时后足相对髋部的最大滞后应控制在 **`≤ 4.2` 格**以内；
   - 后膝前后位置全程保持在髋部前方（`> 0`），绝不倒拖至臀后；
   - 后足摆动抬脚占空比保持在 **`35%~50%`**，且所有腿段坐标零 `NaN`。



===== FILE: ./verify.mjs =====
// 外包交付自动化校验脚本（零外部依赖，直接运行：node verify.mjs）
// 用途：硬性校验 ./bundle/boss_goblin_king.js 是否 100% 符合主工坊接口、像素堆叠规范与防糊红线。

import fs from 'node:fs';
import vm from 'node:vm';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const ROOT = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const TARGET = path.join(ROOT, 'bundle', 'boss_goblin_king.js');

if (!fs.existsSync(TARGET)) {
  console.error('❌ 未找到交付文件：./bundle/boss_goblin_king.js');
  process.exit(1);
}

function makeCanvas(w, h) {
  return {
    width: Math.max(1, w),
    height: Math.max(1, h),
    getContext() {
      return {
        imageSmoothingEnabled: false,
        createImageData: (iw, ih) => ({ width: iw, height: ih, data: new Uint8ClampedArray(iw * ih * 4) }),
        putImageData() {}, clearRect() {}, fillRect() {}, drawImage() {},
        save() {}, restore() {}, translate() {}, rotate() {}, scale() {},
        beginPath() {}, arc() {}, ellipse() {}, fill() {}, stroke() {}, setTransform() {}
      };
    }
  };
}

const sandbox = {
  window: {},
  document: { createElement: () => makeCanvas(1, 1), currentScript: null },
  performance: { now: () => Number(process.hrtime.bigint()) / 1e6 },
  console, Math, Array, Object, Number, String, Boolean, Set, Map, WeakMap,
  Uint8Array, Uint8ClampedArray, Int32Array, Float32Array, Error, TypeError, RangeError
};
sandbox.window = sandbox;
sandbox.globalThis = sandbox;
vm.createContext(sandbox);

function loadScript(rel) {
  const code = fs.readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, rel), 'utf8');
  vm.runInContext(code, sandbox, { filename: rel });
}

for (const f of ['core/math.js', 'core/voxel.js', 'core/solvers.js', 'core/behaviors.js', 'core/renderer.js', 'core/registry.js', 'core/habitats.js']) {
  loadScript(f);
}

const sourceCode = fs.readFileSync(TARGET, 'utf8');
vm.runInContext(sourceCode, sandbox, { filename: 'bundle/boss_goblin_king.js' });

const PW = sandbox.PixelWorkshop;
const errors = [];
const warns = [];
const check = (cond, msg) => { if (!cond) errors.push(msg); };

const glowMatch = sourceCode.match(/glowKeys\s*:\s*\[([^\]]*)\]/);
if (glowMatch && /(eyeGlint|goldHi|metalHi)/i.test(glowMatch[1])) {
  errors.push('违规：eyeGlint / goldHi / metalHi 被放进了 glowKeys（会导致面部或金属发糊）');
}

const stringLiterals = sourceCode.match(/(['"`])(?:\\.|(?!\1).)*\1/g) || [];
const badWords = [];
for (const lit of stringLiterals) {
  const m = lit.match(/[灯烬影夜魂噬幽渊星光]|守.者|.之./);
  if (m) badWords.push(`${m[0]} in ${lit.slice(0, 24)}`);
}
check(badWords.length === 0, `字符串含禁用字：${badWords.slice(0, 5).join('；')}`);

const def = PW.Assets.byId['boss_goblin_king'];
check(!!def, '未通过 PW.Creatures.push 登记 id 为 "boss_goblin_king" 的资产');

if (def) {
  const t0 = sandbox.performance.now();
  const inst = PW.Assets.spawn(def, { seed: 42 });
  const initMs = sandbox.performance.now() - t0;
  check(initMs <= 60, `单次建模耗时过长：${initMs.toFixed(1)}ms（上限 60ms）`);

  const parts = inst.parts || {};
  const partKeys = Object.keys(parts);
  check(partKeys.length >= 8, `部件资产过少（仅 ${partKeys.length} 个），需拆出躯干/披风/宝袋/头/金冠/双耳/两段手臂/重锤/双腿与技能道具`);
  for (const k of partKeys) {
    const p = parts[k];
    if (!p || typeof p.w !== 'number') continue;
    check(p.w <= 40 && p.d <= 40 && p.h <= 40, `部件 ${k} 尺寸超限：${p.w}×${p.d}×${p.h}（单盒必须 <= 40）`);
    if (p.palette) {
      const hexes = Object.values(p.palette).filter(v => typeof v === 'string');
      check(hexes.length <= 48, `部件 ${k} 色表超 48 色（当前 ${hexes.length}）`);
    }
  }

  const skins = def.skins || [];
  check(skins.length >= 4, `皮肤数不足 4 套（当前 ${skins.length}）`);
  for (let i = 0; i < skins.length; i++) {
    try { if (inst.applySkin) inst.applySkin(i); }
    catch (e) { errors.push(`applySkin(${i}) 报错：${e.message}`); }
  }

  const requiredActions = [
    'patrol', 'idle', 'stagger',
    'skill_mace_smash', 'skill_gold_rain', 'skill_rally_horde', 'skill_greed_charge'
  ];
  const actIds = (def.actions || []).map(a => a.id);
  for (const req of requiredActions) {
    check(actIds.includes(req), `Def.actions 缺少必需动作：${req}`);
  }

  const hab = PW.Habitats.HABITAT_MAP['forest'] || PW.Habitats.HABITAT_DEFS[0];
  const vfx = new PW.Renderer.VFXPool();
  const params = Object.assign({ gaitFreq: 1, tailFlex: 1, spring: 1, speed: 1, scale: 1 }, def.defaultParams || {});

  for (const act of (def.actions || [])) {
    const dur = act.loop ? 2.5 : (act.duration || 2.5);
    const steps = Math.ceil(dur * 60);
    let maxParts = 0;
    for (let s = 0; s <= steps; s++) {
      const dt = 1 / 60;
      const t = s * dt;
      inst.update(dt, t, params, hab, vfx, {
        patrol: act.id === 'patrol',
        action: act.id,
        actionTime: t,
        homeX: 0, homeY: 0, radius: 68, others: []
      });
      const list = inst.buildPartList(hab) || [];
      maxParts = Math.max(maxParts, list.length);
      for (const item of list) {
        for (const f of ['x', 'y', 'lift', 'ro']) {
          const val = item[f];
          if (typeof val !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(val)) {
            errors.push(`动作 ${act.id} 在 t=${t.toFixed(2)}s 部件 ${item.partKey || '?'} 出现非法值 ${f}=${val}`);
            break;
          }
        }
        if (Math.abs(item.x) > 110 || Math.abs(item.y) > 110 || item.lift < -8 || item.lift > 90) {
          warns.push(`动作 ${act.id} 部件 ${item.partKey} 位置偏远：(${item.x.toFixed(1)}, ${item.y.toFixed(1)}, z=${item.lift.toFixed(1)})`);
        }
      }
      if (errors.length >= 12) break;
    }
    check(maxParts >= 10, `动作 ${act.id} 渲染部件仅 ${maxParts} 个（应包含躯干/披风/宝袋/头/冠/双耳/双臂/锤/双腿）`);
  }

  console.log(`\n[哥布林王 自动化校验报告]`);
  console.log(`- 资产 ID：${def.id}（${def.name || '未命名'}）`);
  console.log(`- 建模耗时：${initMs.toFixed(2)} ms（红线 <= 60ms）`);
  console.log(`- 部件资产数：${partKeys.length} · 皮肤数：${skins.length} · 动作数：${actIds.length}`);
}

if (warns.length) console.log(`⚠️ 提示（${warns.length} 条，展示前 3 条）：\n  - ${warns.slice(0, 3).join('\n  - ')}`);
if (errors.length) {
  console.error(`\n❌ 校验未通过（${errors.length} 项错误）：\n  - ${errors.join('\n  - ')}`);
  process.exit(1);
}
console.log('\n✅ 全部硬性校验通过！可以打开 preview.html 做 360° 视觉终检并打包交回 bundle/。');


===== FILE: ./开工提示词.md =====
# 开工提示词（给无上下文独立网页端 AI · 直接整段复制）

> **发包方式**：把本目录生成的 **`all_in_one.txt`** 拖进网页端 AI 对话框，统一发这句短口令：
> `严格按附件末尾的 开工提示词.md 开发。务必用单个脚本一次性生成 3 个文件、自检并打包出可下载的 bundle_boss_goblin_king.zip；正文严禁贴任何代码、JSON 或文档内容，若未打包完成直接停下汇报进度。`

```text
你正在接手一个独立、自包含的像素堆叠（Sprite Stacking）美术外包任务。你只需要阅读本外包目录内的文件，严禁修改 ./core/ 和 ./references/ 中的任何文件，也不要虚构外部依赖。

请先依次阅读本目录内的以下文件，彻底对齐像素堆叠管线与代码格式：
1. ./README.md（包内结构、交付规范与验收方式）
2. ./设计文档-哥布林王.md（外观、三维坐标预算、二足重步、4 套换色、4 大技能分段时间轴）
3. ./references/骨骼化改造交接与经验.md（像素清晰度、glowKeys 隔离、防身腿分离与零穿模铁律）
4. ./core/voxel.js、./core/solvers.js、./core/registry.js、./core/renderer.js（真实渲染内核接口）
5. ./references/goblin.js 与 ./references/slime.js（同物种哥布林标杆与标准结构源码，务必保持血脉统一并放大 Boss 气场）

【交付目标】
请将全部产出写入 ./bundle/ 目录，最终打包成可下载的 bundle_boss_goblin_king.zip（内含以下 3 个文件）：

1. ./bundle/boss_goblin_king.js（核心资产脚本）：
   - 必须完全照 ./references/goblin.js 与 slime.js 的格式编写，通过 window.PixelWorkshop 与 PW.Creatures.push(Def) 登记，id 严格为 'boss_goblin_king'；
   - 必须使用 ./core/voxel.js 的 createPaletteSpec + VoxelModel + applyArtShading + recolorAsset 构建体素部件，严禁绕开内核手画 2D 矢量图；
   - 按设计文档实现大 Boss 四大标志特征：歪戴的嵌红绿蓝三宝石巨型锯齿金冠 + 宽大狞笑头颅（0.6 俯视 0 遮挡双邪眼 + 露白獠牙 + 枢轴锁死超长金环双尖耳）+ 宽厚大肚腩与镶金赤红王者披风 + 滞后颠簸的满溢金币黄金大宝袋 + 两段式手臂与枢轴锁死黑铁镶金巨型狼牙重锤 + 粗壮深嵌不脱胯的二足重步；
   - 实现 4 套零重建模一键换色 SKINS（苔绿金冠 / 赤皮战酋 / 霜青雪酋 / 玄铁黑酋），单盒严格 <= 40 格、总色数 <= 42（硬限 48）、0 重色、eyeGlint / goldHi / metalHi 绝不进 glowKeys、常态严禁小数 scaleX/scaleY 拉伸；
   - 在 Def.actions 中登记并完整实现全部 7 个连续动作（由 eco.action 与 eco.actionTime 驱动）：
     * patrol（霸道巡营，loop: true）
     * idle（叉腰狞笑，loop: true）
     * stagger（破盾歪冠，duration: 1.5，loop: false：踉跄下沉 + 金冠弹起扣歪 + 宝袋颠币 + 双耳耷拉）
     * skill_mace_smash（霸王重锤，duration: 2.4：高举蓄力脉冲 -> 前跨狠砸地面 + addDecal 裂纹与碎石粒子 -> 拔锤回位）
     * skill_gold_rain（撒币乱砸，duration: 2.8：叉腰大笑抖耳 -> 漫天抛射 6 枚复用立体 goldChunk 金块弹跳溅屑 -> 拍肚归位）
     * skill_rally_horde（摇旗召众，duration: 3.0：左手高举赤红 warBanner 部落战旗狂摇出声波环 -> 召出 2 只复用 minionGoblin 迷你喽啰绕场冲锋挥棒 -> 收旗退场）
     * skill_greed_charge（贪财蛮冲，duration: 3.2：低身刨地蓄势 -> 扛锤猛冲 +18 格并沿途犁地掉币 -> 终点大回旋横扫 -> 挺肚走回）
   - 严格守住所有中文命名与字符串零禁用字（严禁出现 灯、烬、影、夜、魂、噬、幽、渊、星、光 及 守×者、×之×）。

2. ./bundle/manifest.json：
   - 列出 id、名称、部件尺寸表、4 套皮肤、7 个 actions 的时长与对应战斗机制说明、以及技能特效挂点说明。

3. ./bundle/交付说明.md：
   - 记录自检与性能数据。

【执行步数与交付熔断铁律（最高优先级）】
1. 压缩包命名格式（固定规范）：输出文件必须严格命名为 bundle_boss_goblin_king.zip（格式：bundle_<英文名>.zip），包内目录结构为 bundle/boss_goblin_king.js、bundle/manifest.json、bundle/交付说明.md。
2. 原子化打包：严禁分多轮试探环境或逐个读写文件。请在 1～2 次代码执行内，用单个脚本一次性写出上述 3 个文件，紧接着在同一脚本里完成静态红线校验（禁用字正则、glowKeys 隔离、色表上限与去重、括号/语法校验；若环境支持 Node.js 则直接跑 node verify.mjs），全部通过后立刻打包输出可下载的 bundle_boss_goblin_king.zip。
3. 正文零内容熔断：聊天正文仅允许包含 bundle_boss_goblin_king.zip 下载链接与不超过 10 行的自检摘要，严禁贴出任何 JS 代码、JSON 清单或 Markdown 文档正文。
4. 未完成即停：如果本轮因步数或环境限制未能生成 bundle_boss_goblin_king.zip，严禁把文件内容贴进聊天补救——只输出 1 句话说明已完成到哪一步，等待我回复「继续打包」。
```
